Result: 在真实无线条件下，CHARGE-FL在精度、稳定性和收敛速度方面显著优于现有OTA-FL方法，尤其在落后客户端和强噪声场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: CHARGE-FL通过信道自适应的鲁棒聚合机制，有效提升了联邦学习在6G无线环境中的实用性和可靠性。

Abstract: The growing demand for privacy-preserving, data-intensive applications such as IoT, augmented reality, and autonomous systems positions Federated Learning (FL) as a key enabler in 6G networks. Over-the-Air FL (OTA-FL) leverages the superposition property of the wireless multiple access channel for efficient aggregation via simultaneous transmissions. Existing methods rely on fixed aggregation schedules and do not jointly address noise, fading, and client heterogeneity. We propose CHARGE-FL (CHannel-Adaptive Robust agGrEgation), a framework that adaptively schedules aggregation based on channel dynamics and application readiness. By combining a tailored optimization strategy with a dual-purpose precoding mechanism, CHARGE-FL mitigates channel distortion and bias from partial updates, achieving superior accuracy, stability, and convergence under realistic wireless conditions. Empirical results under realistic wireless conditions show that CHARGE-FL significantly improves accuracy, stability, and convergence over state-of-the-art OTA-FL methods, particularly in straggler-prone and noisy scenarios.

</details>


### [135] [Quantize the Target, Quantize the Drafter: Efficient Inference with Qwen3.5-4B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04244)
*Jaeyeon Kim,Jewon Lee,Bo-Kyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Efficient Qwen Competition提出了一种结合量化感知蒸馏和块扩散草稿模型的推测解码系统，在NVIDIA A10G GPU上实现了Qwen3.5-4B的低延迟推理，最终获得6.978倍加速比并排名第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的NVIDIA A10G GPU上实现低延迟的Qwen3.5-4B模型推理服务。

Method: 1) 对目标模型进行量化并采用量化感知蒸馏恢复精度；2) 训练块扩散草稿模型（block-diffusion drafter），通过两阶段训练（先学习高精度目标，再适应低精度目标）专门适配量化后的目标模型；3) 对草稿模型进一步量化并采用滑动窗口注意力减少推理开销。

Result: 相比基线实现了6.978倍的平均加速，同时满足所需的质量阈值，最终排名第三。

Conclusion: 该方案为实际LLM推理中的低延迟服务提供了有价值的参考，代码和资源已开源。

Abstract: This report describes our approach to the Efficient Qwen Competition, where the goal is to enable low-latency serving of Qwen3.5-4B on a resource-constrained NVIDIA A10G GPU. Our system combines a quantized target model with speculative decoding. To recover accuracy, we apply quantization-aware distillation to the target model while retaining the original quantization grid. To speed up decoding, a block-diffusion drafter specialized for the quantized target model is trained using a two-stage procedure: first learning from the high-precision target and then adapting to the low-precision target. Because the drafter is invoked at every speculative decoding step, we further reduce its overhead with quantization and sliding-window attention, preserving draft-token acceptance while improving long-context decoding latency. As a result, our submission achieves a 6.978$\times$ average speedup over the baseline while satisfying the required quality thresholds, ranking 3rd overall. We hope these results provide useful insights for practical LLM inference. The code and resources are available at https://github.com/nota-github/adaptfm-quant-dflash

</details>


### [136] [Signal or Noise? Understanding Generative Models for Real-World Sensor Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04245)
*Zitao Shuai,Zongzhe Xu,Yuntian Wu,Sirui Li,Tianhong Li,Yuzhe Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SensorGen，一个覆盖4个领域、7个数据集、12种信号模态的传感器信号生成大规模研究。通过系统评估5大生成模型家族，发现：(1)流匹配模型在多数场景表现最佳；(2)信号特性至关重要，人口协变量改善纵向生成，时频建模改善高频信号；(3)生成信号具有超越视觉逼真度的实际效用，缩放提升生成质量，合成数据改善下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传感器数据生成建模碎片化严重，跨模态、数据集和任务形式不统一，缺乏对生成模型在真实世界场景中何时、如何以及为何成功或失败的系统性理解。

Method: 构建SensorGen研究框架，涵盖14个实验设置、4个领域、7个数据集和12种信号模态，系统评估5大生成模型家族（包括流匹配模型等），并进行多维度比较分析。

Result: 三大核心发现：(1)流匹配模型在多数设置中表现最佳；(2)人口协变量改善纵向生成，时频建模改善高频信号生成；(3)生成信号具有超越视觉质量的实用价值，缩放法则和合成数据增强均有效。

Conclusion: SensorGen为真实世界传感器数据生成提供了对设计选择、评估协议和失败模式的广泛理解，建立了系统化的基准和认知框架。

Abstract: Generative models have changed how machine learning represents complex data distributions, especially in language and vision, yet many real-world systems are observed instead as continuous, high-dimensional, and noisy sensor time series. Existing generative modeling of sensor data, however, remains fragmented across modalities, datasets, and task formulations, limiting a systematic understanding of when, how, and why generative models succeed or fail in real-world settings. To address this gap, we introduce SensorGen, a large-scale study of sensor-signal generation spanning 14 settings across 4 domains, 7 datasets, and 12 signal modalities. Leveraging SensorGen, we systematically evaluate generative models from five major families and uncover three key findings: (1) flow-matching models provide strong overall performance across most settings; (2) signal properties matter, with demographic covariates improving longitudinal generation and time-frequency modeling improving high-frequency signal generation; and (3) generated signals have practical utility beyond visual realism, with scaling improving generation quality and synthetic data improving downstream performance. Together, SensorGen establishes a broader understanding of design choices, evaluation protocols, and failure modes in real-world sensor data generation.

</details>


### [137] [On Preserving Geometrical Invariance for Superpixel Image Classification using Graph Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04262)
*Sarabeshwar Balaji,Shubham Mohanty,Akash Anil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SuperGT框架，基于图Transformer进行图像分类，通过超像素图表示减少冗余，利用Transformer捕获长距离依赖，并设计预处理方案保持平移/旋转不变性。在CIFAR-10上表现优于多数基线，性能与ShapeGNN相当但无需边界点坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN和ViT在密集像素网格上计算复杂度高，难以部署；现有基于超像素的GNN方法难以捕获长距离依赖，且多数方法未能显式保持平移/旋转不变性。

Method: 提出SuperGT框架：采用图Transformer架构捕获长距离依赖，同时设计预处理方案以保持平移/旋转不变性。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上，SuperGT显著优于多个基线模型，整体性能与之前最先进的ShapeGNN相当，且无需依赖每个超像素边界点坐标。

Conclusion: SuperGT通过图Transformer有效解决了超像素图上的长距离依赖问题，并在保持平移/旋转不变性的同时实现了有竞争力的分类性能。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) and Vision Transformer (ViT) for image classification exploit a dense grid of pixels containing redundant information. Consequently, for a larger image dataset, CNNs and ViTs face deployability challenges due to high computational complexity. Representing images as graphs of superpixels offers an efficient alternative that preserves key information while eliminating pixel-level redundancy. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have been utilized on such graphs to perform image classification. However, GNNs are known to struggle with capturing long-range dependencies which is important in the domain of image classification. Furthermore, a majority of these superpixel-based image classification approaches do not explicitly preserve translation/rotation invariance. Nevertheless, preserving translation/rotation invariance is important for robust image classification. Thus, this paper proposes SuperGT, a Graph Transformer-based framework for image classification, which captures the long range dependencies, along with a pre-processing scheme that preserves translation/rotation invariance. We evaluate SuperGT on CIFAR-10 dataset and observe that it performs significantly better than many baselines. Furthermore, we note that the overall performance of SuperGT is comparable to the previous state-of-the-art model, namely, ShapeGNN, without relying on coordinates of the boundary points of each superpixel required by ShapeGNN.

</details>


### [138] [HiFA4: Training-Free 4-bit FlashAttention on Ascend HIF4 NPUs for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04302)
*Hui Dong,Yanzhao Li,Jie Gao,Chunlu Li,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yupeng Sun,Zhenyuan Chen,Zhiqiang Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiFA4 是一种面向 Ascend NPU 的后训练算子级设计，将 FlashAttention 中的 QK^T 和 PV 均以 4-bit HIF4 Cube GEMM 执行，同时保持在线 softmax 状态为 FP16，通过 Smooth-QK 和 P-Reordering 两种机制减少量化引起的精度损失，在多个 LLM 上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 LLM 推理中，FlashAttention 的 QK^T 和 PV 计算存在量化困难，现有低精度方法在 Ascend NPU 的 HIF4 格式上未能充分利用硬件能力，导致精度损失。本文旨在设计一种针对 Ascend NPU 的 4-bit 算子级方案，在保持推理效率的同时最小化精度退化。

Method: HiFA4 包含两个核心机制：(1) Smooth-QK：在 RoPE 后对 Q 和 K 进行校准静态的逐通道等价重缩放，将量化难度从 K 转移到 Q，避免推理时的逐 tile 在线归约；(2) P-Reordering：从与 PV GEMM 相同的量化注意力权重 P_hat 中累积 softmax 归一化因子，而非使用高精度重建版本，并将该归一化因子融合进 PV Cube GEMM 中。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上，HiFA4 恢复了直接 HIF4 量化引入的 37.5% 精度差距，样本加权准确率损失从 1.12 pp 降至 0.70 pp，BF16 不一致 MMLU 预测从 16.3% 降至 8.2%，MMLU 准确率回归减少 57%（1071→465）。Gemma2-9B 上保持在 BF16 的 0.7 pp 以内，回归减少 27%。在 Smooth-QK 被禁用的 LLaMA3.1-8B、Mistral-7B、Phi-4B 上，P-Reordering 仍使完整 MMLU 回归减少 41-52%。指令调度分析显示相比 BF16 的关键路径延迟可降低 35.4%。

Conclusion: HiFA4 是首个在 Ascend NPU 上针对 HIF4 格式设计的 4-bit FlashAttention 方案，通过 Smooth-QK 和 P-Reordering 有效缓解了低精度量化带来的精度损失，同时为算子融合和延迟优化提供了可行性分析，硬件验证留待未来工作。

Abstract: We present HiFA4, a post-training operator-level design that executes both QK^T and PV in FlashAttention as 4-bit HIF4 Cube GEMMs for LLM inference on Ascend NPUs, while maintaining the online softmax state in FP16. To our knowledge, HiFA4 is the first Ascend-HIF4-targeted design of this kind evaluated on standard NLP benchmarks.
  HiFA4 combines two mechanisms. Smooth-QK applies a calibration-static per-channel equivalent rescaling to Q and K after RoPE, transferring quantization difficulty from K to Q without per-tile online reduction at inference. P-Reordering accumulates the softmax normalizer from the same quantized attention weights P_hat used in the PV GEMM, rather than from a higher-precision reconstruction. We show that this inconsistent formulation introduces a coherent output-scaling error, and validate the effect on a Qwen3-8B Layer-0 MMLU trace, where all 3.6M measured attention tiles exhibit net probability-mass loss with median epsilon_bar = -0.064. P-Reordering also allows the normalizer to be fused into the PV Cube GEMM.
  Across five LLMs, HiFA4 reduces quantization-induced decision drift. On Qwen3-8B, it recovers 37.5% of the accuracy gap introduced by direct HIF4 quantization, narrows the sample-weighted accuracy loss from 1.12 pp to 0.70 pp, reduces BF16-inconsistent MMLU predictions from 16.3% to 8.2%, and cuts MMLU accuracy regressions by 57% (1071 to 465). On Gemma2-9B, mild smoothing keeps HiFA4 within 0.7 pp of BF16 while reducing MMLU regressions by 27%. On LLaMA3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, and Phi-4B, where Smooth-QK is disabled, P-Reordering with the adopted Q-Mean auxiliary still reduces full-set MMLU regressions by 41-52%. A preliminary instruction-scheduling analysis projects a 35.4% critical-path latency reduction relative to BF16 by fusing the softmax normalizer into the PV Cube GEMM; on-hardware validation is left to future work.

</details>


### [139] [SAD-LoRA: Spectral Alignment for Low-Rank Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04306)
*Omer Tariq,Syed Muhammad Raza,Jeongbae Son*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAD-LoRA方法，通过谱对齐蒸馏让LoRA适配器占据最优的秩r子空间，在合成数据和GLUE任务上均显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识蒸馏在将微调教师蒸馏到LoRA学生时，无法显式控制适配器占据的秩r权重子空间，导致子空间错位，影响压缩效率。

Method: 从数据加权的学生空间参考更新ΔWᵀΣˣ^{1/2}中选择子空间，并通过可微主角损失约束colspan(B)来保持该子空间，从而实现谱对齐蒸馏。

Result: 在合成问题中，子空间错位项从51%降至接近零，对齐度从0.49提升至1.00。在GLUE六个任务上，r=4时五项超越最强谱基线，r=8时SST-2和CoLA取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: SAD-LoRA通过显式子空间对齐有效提升LoRA蒸馏的秩效率，子空间对齐是主要贡献因素，系数匹配为辅助作用。

Abstract: Distilling a fine-tuned teacher into a LoRA-adapted student is a standard recipe for parameter-efficient compression, but output-level KD does not explicitly control which rank-$r$ weight subspace the adapter occupies. We propose \textbf{SAD-LoRA} (\textbf{S}pectral \textbf{A}lignment \textbf{D}istillation), which selects this subspace from the data-weighted student-space reference update $\DWT\Sigx^{1/2}$ and maintains it during training via a differentiable principal-angle loss on $\colspan(B)$. We show that the data-weighted distillation error decomposes exactly into subspace misalignment, within-subspace coefficient mismatch, and irreducible rank residual; standard KD can affect the first term only indirectly through output gradients. On controlled synthetic problems with a flat teacher spectrum, SAD-LoRA reduces the subspace-misalignment term from $51\%$ to nearly zero and lifts final subspace alignment from $0.49$ to $1.00$. On RoBERTa-large to RoBERTa-base distillation across six GLUE tasks, SAD-LoRA improves rank efficiency: at $r{=}4$, it matches or beats the strongest included spectral baseline on five of six tasks, and at $r{=}8$ it gives the best result on SST-2 and CoLA. Ablations identify subspace alignment as the load-bearing component, while coefficient matching is auxiliary.

</details>


### [140] [On the effectiveness of reward functions in reinforcement learning for confidence calibration of large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04332)
*Chee Heng Tan,Zhuoyi Lin,Mehul Motani,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在强化学习训练大语言模型时，如何设计置信度奖励方案以避免模型通过故意答错来操纵置信度表达（即置信度奖励黑客现象），并提出了不可破解置信度奖励方案的概念及其谱系，实验表明最优方案取决于数据集和应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL训练LLMs同时提升推理准确性和置信度表达时，可能存在设计缺陷导致模型通过故意答错来获得负面影响奖励，即置信度奖励黑客现象。

Method: 设计了两种奖励函数（正确时和错误时分别使用不同奖励），提出不可破解置信度奖励方案的概念及其谱系，并将奖励方案作为超参数来优化准确率-置信度校准的权衡。

Result: 实验证明实际数据集中存在选择性置信度奖励黑客现象；不同数据集和应用场景下最优的奖励方案不同，最佳校准-准确率权衡取决于具体任务。

Conclusion: 奖励方案应被视为超参数，根据应用需求优化准确率与置信度校准之间的权衡，不可破解的奖励方案设计对防止奖励黑客至关重要。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the setting where large language models (LLMs) are trained using reinforcement learning (RL) to simultaneously improve reasoning accuracy and verbalize its confidence. Our reward scheme uses two functions for rewarding confidence verbalized by the LLM: one when the LLM is correct and a different one when the LLM is incorrect. With a poorly designed reward scheme, the LLM may be incentivized to answer incorrectly so that it can be confident that its answer is indeed incorrect, a phenomenon that we call confidence reward hacking. We propose the concept of non-hackable confidence reward schemes and define a spectrum of such reward schemes for RL confidence calibration training in LLMs. We demonstrate that selective confidence reward hacking can occur in practical datasets with reward schemes that are not designed to be non-hackable. We also demonstrate that the reward scheme with the best calibration to accuracy tradeoff depends on the dataset and the application, and propose using the reward scheme as a hyperparameter to optimize the tradeoffs in accordance to what is important for the application. The code of our experiments is available in https://anonymous.4open.science/r/rl-confidence-calibration-9ED4/README.md.

</details>


### [141] [Structure-Specific Representational Priors Causally Control the Grokking Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04333)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因果实验证明，grokking（泛化延迟）的本质是模型形成正确表征结构所需的时间。注入任务真实结构（模加法）的对比损失可加速泛化（最高2.75倍），注入错误但相干的结构不改变grokking，注入随机结构则完全消除泛化能力，且表征结构形成先于并预测泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前grokking中"结构形成导致泛化延迟"仅为观察性论断，缺乏因果证据。研究者希望通过注入不同结构信息的表征先验，直接检验表征结构是否是grokking延迟的必要且充分原因。

Method: 在单层transformer学习模加法任务中，引入监督对比辅助损失，其正样本编码三种不同结构：(i) 任务真实结构 (a+b) mod p，(ii) 相干但错误结构 (a-b) mod p，(iii) 随机划分。控制损失形式、强度、类别大小和几何特性一致，同时设置权重范数匹配对照实验，并通过表征探针追踪结构形成过程。

Result: 真实结构22/30运行泛化，错误结构14/15泛化，随机结构0/20泛化（Fisher精确p=1.3×10⁻⁷）。仅真实结构加速grokking（最高2.75倍），但有剂量依赖性和种子双峰分布，考虑对比损失开销后仅在最强情况下有净加速。权重范数匹配对照组0/15泛化，排除范数作为中介变量。表征探针显示结构形成先于并预测所有95次运行的泛化。

Conclusion: Grokking延迟的因果本质是形成正确表征结构所需的时间，且"正确"由特征层级而非标签层级决定：相干但错误的结构保留grokking，随机结构消除grokking，仅真实结构加速grokking。

Abstract: Grokking -- generalization arriving long after training-set interpolation -- can be accelerated by structure-agnostic interventions: gradient filtering, weight-norm clamping, geometric penalties on hidden representations. Whether the delay specifically measures the time to form task-structured representations has remained an observational claim. We test it causally by injecting representational priors of varying structural content into a one-layer transformer learning modular addition: a supervised-contrastive auxiliary loss whose positives encode (i) the task's true equivalence structure ($(a+b) \bmod p$), (ii) a coherent-but-wrong sibling structure ($(a-b) \bmod p$), or (iii) a random partition, all with identical loss form, strength, class sizes, and geometry. Whether generalization occurs follows a clean gradation: true structure 22/30 runs; sibling structure, which needs the same periodic features but the wrong combination, 14/15; random partition, satisfiable only by memorization, 0/20 (Fisher exact $p = 1.3 \times 10^{-7}$). A weight-norm-matched control replaying each intervention's norm trajectory onto plain cross-entropy generalizes in 0/15, collapsing into logit-scale saturation, ruling out the norm as mediator. Representation probes show structure formation precedes and predicts generalization in all 95 runs. Only the true structure also accelerates grokking, up to $2.75\times$ faster than baseline, but the acceleration is dose-dependent, bimodal across seeds, and a net wall-clock win only in its strongest cases given the contrastive term's overhead. The grokking delay is, causally, the time to form the right representational structure, where "right" is decided at the level of features rather than labels: coherent-but-wrong structure leaves grokking intact, random structure abolishes it, and only the true structure hastens it.

</details>


### [142] [One Framework for All: Cross-Modal Membership Inference for Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04339)
*Dayong Ye,Tainqing Zhu,Kun Gao,Junhao Liu,Yichuan Chen,Shuai Zhou,Hengzhu Liu,Bo Liu,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出统一框架，基于生成模型输出分布近似训练数据分布这一模态无关观察，在共享嵌入空间对文本-文本、文本-图像、图像-文本三类生成模型进行成员推断攻击，在黑盒设置下优于各类现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对生成模型的成员推断攻击方法彼此孤立、无法跨模态通用，限制了实际应用价值，需要统一框架。

Method: 利用生成模型输出分布可近似训练数据分布的特性，在共享嵌入空间中建模模型生成样本与非成员辅助样本的分布，通过似然比检验进行成员推断。

Result: 在严格黑盒设置下（部分知识/零知识威胁模型），对微调数据和预训练数据的成员推断均优于针对单一模型类优化的现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该统一框架跨模态有效，填补了生成模型成员推断领域缺乏通用方法的空白。

Abstract: Large generative models across text-to-text, text-to-image, and image-to-text modalities have been shown to pose significant privacy risks. One fundamental threat is membership inference attacks (MIA), which aim to determine whether a given data point was used in a model's training set. Although prior work has investigated MIAs against these three classes of generative models, existing approaches treat them in isolation and are not cross-applicable, thereby limiting their real-world utility. To address this limitation, we present the first comprehensive study of a unified membership inference framework that applies across text-to-text, text-to-image, and image-to-text modalities. Our approach is grounded in a key modality-agnostic observation: the output distribution of a generative model can approximate its training data distribution. Leveraging this property, we model the distributions of model-generated outputs and auxiliary non-member samples in a shared embedding space, and perform membership inference via likelihood ratio testing. We conduct extensive experiments in a strict black-box setting under both partial-knowledge and zero-knowledge threat models, and evaluate membership inference against both fine-tuning and pre-training data. Experimental results demonstrate our approach's superior performance in comparison to existing state-of-the-art methods, which are typically optimized for a single model class.

</details>


### [143] [How Many Initial Points Does Bayesian Optimization Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04356)
*Mujin Cheon,James Odgers,Dong-Yeun Koh,Calvin Tsay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 贝叶斯优化中的初始随机采样点数 n₀ 存在U型成本曲线：n₀过小或过大都会浪费资源。Thompson Sampling对n₀不敏感但整体表现较差。建议使用多步前瞻BO，或无法调优n₀时采用Thompson Sampling，可调优时选取较大的n₀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化的初始化阶段通常需要随机采样n₀个点，但n₀的选择一直缺乏理论指导，主要依赖经验启发式方法。本文旨在系统研究n₀对BO总成本的影响，揭示其背后的机制，并为实践者提供可操作的指导。

Method: 通过大量实验对比不同n₀值下BO的总成本（初始随机点+后续迭代次数），考察MLE、贝叶斯MCMC和精确GP超参数等多种设定，以及多种采集函数（EI、UCB、Thompson Sampling等）。通过3D BO轨迹可视化分析方差驱动BO的边界效应。

Result: 总成本关于n₀呈现U型曲线；该现象在多种超参数估计方法和采集函数中普遍存在；Thompson Sampling是例外，其总成本和简单遗憾对n₀不敏感，但整体成本更高；U型现象归因于方差驱动BO在初始阶段倾向于探索超立方体角点，消耗预算后才转向内部区域。

Conclusion: 推荐在可能情况下使用多步前瞻BO；当n₀无法调优时使用Thompson Sampling；当可以调优n₀时，使用较大的n₀值以避免边界探索浪费。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) generally begins with an initialization phase: a batch of $n_0$ uninformed evaluations. The choice of $n_0$ remains largely heuristic, and we empirically observe that the total cost (random initial points plus BO iterations needed to find the global optimum) is U-shaped in $n_0$, i.e., a practitioner wastes resources by selecting either too low or too high a value of $n_0$. We find this tradeoff persists across MLE, Bayesian MCMC, and exact GP hyperparameters, as well as across acquisition functions. Toward the latter, Thompson Sampling appears an exception, with both total cost and simple regret essentially $n_0$-agnostic, though higher in our experiments. We attribute this U-shape to the known boundary issue of variance-driven BO: BO burns early budget on corners of the hypercube before turning inward. We demonstrate this effect using a 3D BO trajectory where the exact hyperparameters are known. We conclude with practical recommendations: use multi-step lookahead BO where possible; otherwise use Thompson Sampling when $n_0$ cannot be tuned, and a generously large $n_0$ when it can.

</details>


### [144] [RL Forgets! Towards Continual Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04364)
*Mao-Lin Luo,Zhe-Xu Wang,Zi-Hao Zhou,Bo Ye,Jian Zhao,Min-Ling Zhang,Tong Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“强化学习比监督微调更不易遗忘”的主流观点，提出了多模态推理持续学习基准MRCL，并设计了一种无需回放的持续策略优化框架CPO，通过基于先验任务行为KL散度的参数移动正则化来缓解灾难性遗忘，实验证明CPO在多个模型规模上均能有效减少遗忘并提升预训练能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来持续后训练中强化学习被认为比监督微调更不易遗忘，但这一假设缺乏充分验证，现有证据多来自过时或同质化基准。作者旨在重新审视这一假设。

Method: 1) 构建MRCL基准（基于多样化的近期多模态数据集）；2) 提出CPO框架，利用先验任务行为KL散度目标，通过理论合理的参数移动正则化限制策略漂移，无需经验回放。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B上，CPO将遗忘降低13.7%，预训练能力提升7.0%，并在多个模型规模上一致有效。

Conclusion: 强化学习在持续后训练中同样存在严重的灾难性遗忘问题，CPO作为一种无需回放的框架能有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Continual post-training is becoming a central paradigm for adapting vision-language models to evolving tasks. Recent work has increasingly favored reinforcement learning over supervised fine-tuning, driven by the belief that reinforcement learning is inherently less prone to forgetting. However, the belief remains insufficiently validated, as existing evidence is largely drawn from outdated or homogeneous benchmarks. To revisit this assumption, we introduce MRCL, a Multimodal Reasoning Continual Learning benchmark built from diverse and recently released multimodal datasets. Experiments on MRCL show that reinforcement learning can still suffer from severe catastrophic forgetting during continual post-training. To address this challenge, we propose Continual Policy Optimization (CPO), a replay-free framework grounded in the prior-task behavioral KL objective. CPO uses a theoretically justified parameter-movement regularization to limit policy drift on previous tasks. Extensive experiments across multiple model scales demonstrate that CPO consistently reduces forgetting while preserving, and in some cases improving, pretrained model capabilities. On Qwen3-VL-8B, CPO reduces forgetting by 13.7\% and improves pretrained capability by 7.0\%. The implementation code is available at https://github.com/MaolinLuo/CPO.

</details>


### [145] [NKI-Agent: Domain-Specific Fine-Tuning and Agentic Tool Use for Neuron Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04395)
*Junjie Tang,Jun Huan,Hao Zhou,Yuhao Zhang,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NKI-Agent是首个结合领域特定监督微调(SFT)与编译-验证-修复智能体循环的NKI内核生成系统，在AWS Trainium/Inferentia硬件上实现了77.3%的通过率，并发现工具使用至关重要，而GRPO二值编译奖励未能超越SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴AI加速器（如AWS Trainium和Inferentia）缺乏自动化内核生成与优化方案，开发者需手动处理多引擎架构、分块编程及显式数据移动等复杂问题，且该领域无公开训练数据、基准或工具增强智能体。

Method: 1) 将CUDA-Agent框架适配到Neuron硬件；2) 构建6,000个NKI内核生成训练任务及250任务的NKIBench基准（三个难度等级）；3) 采用领域特定监督微调(SFT)结合编译-验证-修复智能体循环；4) 探索GRPO强化学习方法。

Result: Claude Opus 4.8配合秩感知系统提示词在150任务NKIBench上达77.3%通过率；Opus 4.8无工具单次模式仅6%；SFT微调的Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B以1/100成本达25.0%，超越Claude Sonnet 4（15.0%）；GRPO二值编译奖励未优于SFT。

Conclusion: 工具增强对NKI内核生成至关重要，SFT可有效提升小模型性能并降低成本，GRPO的二值编译奖励设计需要改进，为基于强化学习的内核生成提供了奖励设计指导。

Abstract: Recent agentic approaches to LLM-based kernel generation have achieved impressive results on CUDA. For emerging AI accelerators such as AWS Trainium and Inferentia, automated kernel generation and optimization remain largely unaddressed. Writing kernels for these chips via the Neuron Kernel Interface (NKI) is particularly challenging: developers must navigate a multi-engine architecture, tile-based programming, and explicit data movement across multi-level memory hierarchy. Moreover, no publicly-available training data, benchmarks, or tool-augmented agents exist for this domain. We introduce NKI-Agent, the first system combining domain-specific supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with a compile-verify-fix agent loop for NKI kernel generation. We adapt the existing CUDA-Agent framework to Neuron hardware, curate 6,000 NKI kernel generation tasks for training, and construct NKIBench, a 250-task benchmark across three difficulty levels. Evaluated on real Trn1 hardware, NKI-Agent with Claude Opus 4.8 and a rank-aware system prompt achieves a 77.3% pass rate on the 150-task NKIBench. We show that tool use is critical: Opus 4.8 scores 6% in single-shot mode without agent tools. On a 60-task subset, we show that an SFT-trained Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B achieves 25.0% pass rate at 1/100th the cost, outperforming Claude Sonnet 4 (15.0%). We also report that Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with binary compilation reward fails to improve over SFT, providing guidance on reward design for RL-based kernel generation.

</details>


### [146] [Learning Task-Sufficient World Models by Synergizing Agentic Exploration and Structured Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04409)
*Fan Feng,Yujia Zheng,Minghao Fu,Yongqiang Chen,Guangyi Chen,Kevin Murphy,Biwei Huang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能体与世界模型闭环协同的范式，通过结构化世界模型学习从交互数据中提炼任务充分的最小化表征，从而提升样本效率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于想象世界模型的方法常依赖高维潜空间或通用视觉嵌入，保留了大量与控制无关的因素，限制了效率与跨任务泛化能力。因此，研究如何学习任务特定、最小且充分的决策表征。

Method: 构建智能体与世界模型之间的闭环协同：智能端通过自适应课程主动收集揭示任务相关潜在因子的信息轨迹；世界模型端对观测进行结构化表征学习，从交互数据中蒸馏出紧凑、任务充分的潜在状态。

Result: 该方法能够实证恢复任务充分的潜在表征，捕获所有控制相关因子；基于这些表征的策略在样本效率、跨技能泛化、物体-技能组合泛化以及未见任务上均取得提升。

Conclusion: 通过智能体与世界模型的协同学习，可以自动发现任务充分的压缩表征，为基于想象的世界模型决策范式提供更高效且泛化能力更强的方案。

Abstract: Learning and planning in imagination using world models provides an effective paradigm for training agents for decision-making. However, existing approaches often rely on high-dimensional latent spaces or generic visual embeddings that retain many factors irrelevant to control, limiting efficiency and generalization across tasks. To this end, we study how agents can learn world models with representations that are task-specific, minimal, and sufficient for decision-making. We achieve this via a closed-loop synergy between the agent and the world model, in which structured world-model learning distills task-sufficient representations from informative interaction data. On the agent side, agents actively probe the environment to collect informative trajectories that expose task-relevant latent factors, guided by an adaptive curriculum. On the world-model side, we learn structured representations over observations to distill compact, task-sufficient latent states from the collected interaction data. This synergy enables the empirical recovery of task-sufficient latent representations that capture all control-relevant factors. Leveraging these representations, the resulting policies achieve improved sample efficiency and generalization, including generalization across skills, object-skill compositions, and previously unseen tasks on standard continuous-control and robotic-manipulation benchmarks.

</details>


### [147] [Environmental Drivers of Respiratory Disease: A District Level Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04416)
*Rahim Iqbal,Asfi Ahamed,Izzath Nisfer,Shazan Shaheed,Muhammadu Ilham,Nathali Athukorala,Madara Mendis,Nisansa de Silva,Sandareka Wickramanayake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究构建了斯里兰卡25个行政区2014-2024年的面板数据集，利用XGBoost模型和SHAP分析发现累积空气质量负担（80.1%）是呼吸系统疾病率的主要驱动因素，森林退化（15.6%）和火灾活动（4.3%）次之，并提出了森林-空气-健康（FAH）风险指数进行区域风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯里兰卡面临森林退化和大气污染加剧，但地区级呼吸系统入院率却呈现矛盾性下降，提示医疗可及性等混杂因素的存在。现有研究缺乏将环境退化与呼吸健康关联的区级定量框架，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了2014-2024年25个行政区的面板数据集，整合卫星植被指数、火辐射功率、污染物浓度（PM2.5、NO2、SO2）、碳通量指标及人口归一化呼吸入院率。建立了两个XGBoost模型（年度呼吸率R²=0.937，月度PM2.5浓度R²=0.976），在21/25个区验证了泛化能力（MAPE≤20%），并采用SHAP分析量化各因素贡献权重。

Result: 累积空气质量负担是呼吸率方差的主要驱动因素（80.1%），森林退化贡献15.6%，火灾活动贡献4.3%。基于SHAP权重构建的FAH风险指数识别出高风险区：科伦坡（0.802）、加姆珀哈（0.708）和卡卢特勒（0.682）。

Conclusion: 本研究首次提供了斯里兰卡环境退化与呼吸健康之间基于证据的区级关联框架，为精准公共卫生和环境政策提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Sri Lanka has experienced a decade of progressive forest degradation and rising atmospheric pollution, yet district-level respiratory admissions have paradoxically declined, pointing to the confounding role of healthcare access. This study addresses that gap by constructing an 11-year (2014-2024) panel dataset across all 25 administrative districts, integrating satellite-derived vegetation indices, fire radiative power, pollutant concentrations (particulate matter (PM2.5), nitrogen dioxide (NO2), sulfur dioxide (SO2)), carbon flux metrics and population-normalized respiratory admission rates. Two temporally validated XGBoost models were created for annual district-level respiratory rate (R^2 = 0.937) and monthly PM2.5 concentration (R^2 = 0.976) with generalization validated in 21 out of 25 districts (Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) <= 20%). Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP) analysis established that cumulative air quality burden is the overwhelming driver of respiratory rate variance (80.1%), ahead of forest degradation (15.6%) and fire activity (4.3%). The Forest-Air-Health (FAH) Risk Index used these SHAP-derived weights to find the districts with the highest risk: Colombo (FAH = 0.802), Gampaha (0.708), and Kalutara (0.682). These findings present the inaugural evidence-based, district-level framework correlating environmental degradation with respiratory health in Sri Lanka, establishing a quantitative basis for focused public health and environmental policy.

</details>


### [148] [Full-Stack FP4: Stable LLM Pretraining with Quantized Projections, Optimizers, and Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04422)
*Siyu Ding,Mingchuan Ma,Jiabo Tong,Xingrun Xing,Ziming Wang,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Full-Stack FP4，首个完整的NVFP4预训练框架，通过模块级精度策略解决线性层、优化器状态和注意力机制的4-bit量化稳定性瓶颈，在3B/64B-token预训练中实现接近BF16的性能（仅1.47%损失差距）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NVFP4预训练方法主要集中在Transformer线性层，忽视了优化器状态、优化器算法和注意力机制的4-bit量化，这三个核心模块存在独特的数值不稳定性模式，阻碍了稳定的全栈4-bit预训练。

Method: 针对线性层使用LoRA-SVD轻量分解抑制量化噪声；针对优化器设计AdamW二阶矩变换和原生NVFP4 Newton-Schulz迭代支持Muon优化器；针对注意力机制提出混合精度方案量化Q/K/V和反向dS，用BF16保护脆弱路径，配合统一张量复用保持前后向对齐。

Result: 线性层损失差距从1.40%降至0.61%；完整3B/64B-token预训练仅1.47%损失差距，验证了可行的端到端NVFP4 LLM预训练。

Conclusion: Full-Stack FP4首次实现了全栈NVFP4稳定预训练，所有模块即插即用，兼具稳定性和效率提升，证明了低比特预训练的可行性。

Abstract: Recent NVFP4 pretraining methods mainly target transformer linear layers, leaving optimizer states, optimizer arithmetic and attention underexplored in 4-bit pipelines. This critical gap blocks stable full-stack 4-bit pretraining, as the three core modules exhibit unique numerical failure patterns: linear layers hit hard quantization noise limits with dimension-propagated error amplification; AdamW second moments are heavy-tailed non-negative values fragile to low-precision denominators; attention carries error-prone computation paths demanding strict forward-backward quantization consistency. We propose Full-Stack FP4, the first complete NVFP4 pretraining framework resolving all three stability bottlenecks via module-wise precision strategies. For linear projections, LoRA-SVD lightweight decomposition suppresses quantization noise and breaks the direct-quantization error ceiling, shrinking the linear-only loss gap from 1.40% to 0.61%. For optimizers, we design AdamW second-moment transformation for robust NVFP4 storage and fully support native NVFP4 Newton-Schulz iterations for the Root (Muon) optimizer. For attention, a mixed-precision scheme quantizes Q/K/V and backward dS while guarding vulnerable paths in BF16, paired with unified tensor reuse to sustain forward-backward alignment. We further analyze fast error accumulation in naive low-bit matrix multiplication and the extreme sensitivity of PV / dOV^T attention branches. All modules are plug-and-play with cumulative stability and efficiency improvements. Our 3B/64B-token pretraining validates near-BF16 performance with merely 1.47% loss gap, verifying feasible stable end-to-end NVFP4 LLM pretraining.

</details>


### [149] [Covert Trait Propagation Is Representation Alignment: Mechanistic Evidence from Hidden-Channel Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04432)
*Kargi Chauhan,Aditya Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在MLP蒸馏设置中，共享初始化使得学生模型即使训练于纯均匀噪声也能继承教师的数字分类能力。这种继承机制（CTP）是几何对齐驱动的：共享初始化使输出投影W_2成为共同坐标键，KL梯度重塑学生输入投影W_0直至隐藏表示与教师对齐。实验表明通道关闭跟踪权重漂移而非教师准确率；冻结W_0破坏迁移而冻结W_2不影响；多教师集成相互抵消；线性CKA与学生准确率相关系数达0.98。该几何视角同样适用于指令微调LLM中的CTBE现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人证明当教师学习率足够小时可保证知识迁移，但未解释这些通道在网络的什么位置存在以及什么决定了其容量。本文旨在揭示共享初始化下知识传递的具体几何机制。

Method: 在MNIST上的MLP蒸馏设置中，通过五个实验验证CTP机制：通道关闭实验、冻结W_0/W_2实验、多教师集成实验、线性CKA相关性分析，并将几何视角扩展到指令微调LLM的CTBE分析。

Result: 通道关闭跟踪权重漂移而非教师准确率；冻结W_0破坏迁移而冻结W_2不变；多教师集成相互抵消；线性CKA与学生准确率相关系数达0.98；CTBE在LLM中由对齐训练激活，标准对数比率指标产生的主要是循环性伪影。

Conclusion: 共享初始化下的知识迁移本质上是几何对齐过程，而非单纯的信息传递；输出投影W_2作为公共坐标键，输入投影W_0通过KL梯度与教师对齐。该几何视角同样适用于解释LLM指令微调中的行为纠缠现象。

Abstract: A student model trained on pure uniform noise can still inherit its teacher's digit-classification ability, provided the two share initialization. Previous work proves this transfer is guaranteed when the teacher's learning rate is small enough, but does not explain where in the network the channel lives or what sets its capacity. Working in an MLP distillation setting on MNIST, we show these channels are not purely informational: geometric alignment gates access to the information the channel carries. Shared initialization makes the output projection W_2 a common coordinate key, and KL gradients reshape the student's input projection W_0 until its hidden representations align with the teacher's. We call this covert trait propagation (CTP). Five experiments support this mechanism: channel closure tracks weight drift, not teacher accuracy; freezing W_0 destroys transfer while freezing W_2 leaves it intact; multi-teacher ensembles cancel out despite each teacher carrying comparable label information; and linear centered kernel alignment (CKA) tracks student accuracy at r=0.98 across a continuous initialization sweep. Applying the same geometric lens to cross-token behavioral entanglement (CTBE) in instruction-tuned LLMs, we find the effect appears to be activated by alignment training, acting on an inherited substrate, and that the standard log-ratio metric produces an apparent frequency bias that is largely a circularity artifact.

</details>


### [150] [Knowledge-Informed Local Causal Discovery of Optimal Adjustment Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04447)
*Seong Woo Ahn,Alessandro Leite,José Lucas De Melo Costa,Fabrice Popineau,Bich-Liên Doan,Arpad Rimmel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: b-LOAD是一种知识增强的局部因果发现算法，通过将先验边约束融入局部结构学习过程，并利用Meek规则动态扩展发现边界，在数据稀缺场景下提升最优调整集的识别能力，从而改善下游因果效应估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部因果发现在数据稀缺场景下因有限样本不确定性、局部邻域不完整和马尔可夫等价类未解决而难以识别有效调整集，而现有方法对结构化背景知识的利用有限。

Method: 在LOAD算法基础上，将先验边约束直接融入局部结构学习过程，使用Meek规则动态扩展发现边界，构建知识约束的部分有向图，实现局部子图上最优调整集的发现。

Result: 在数据稀缺和结构复杂场景下，b-LOAD相比纯数据驱动和标准知识增强基线显著改善了下游因果效应估计；在真实生物网络上，局部定向先验知识带来最大增益，且在中度结构噪声下仍有效。

Conclusion: b-LOAD提供了一种可扩展的方法，能将碎片化的领域知识转化为更可靠的因果效应估计，证明了在合理背景知识下，该过程能单调优化可容许等价类并扩大可识别因果查询集。

Abstract: Local causal discovery is a scalable alternative to global structure learning. However, it can struggle to identify valid adjustment sets in data-scarce settings because of finite-sample uncertainty, incomplete local neighborhoods, and unresolved Markov equivalence. Although many application domains provide structured background knowledge, its integration into local causal discovery remains limited. We propose b-LOAD, a knowledge-informed extension of the LOAD algorithm for local discovery of optimal adjustment sets. b-LOAD incorporates prior edge constraints directly into the local structure-learning procedure and uses Meek's rules to expand the discovery frontier dynamically, yielding a knowledge-constrained partially directed graph over the relevant local subgraph. This strategy helps prevent structurally relevant nodes introduced by prior knowledge from being excluded by local search. We prove that, under sound background knowledge, the procedure monotonically refines the admissible equivalence class and can enlarge the set of identifiable causal queries, enabling recovery of optimal adjustment sets that are not identifiable from observational conditional-independence information alone. Empirically, b-LOAD improves downstream causal effect estimation relative to purely data-driven and standard knowledge-augmented baselines, particularly in data-scarce and structurally complex regimes. Results on real-world biological networks show that locally targeted prior knowledge provides the largest gains and remains beneficial under moderate structural noise. These findings position b-LOAD as a scalable approach for converting fragmented domain knowledge into more reliable causal-effect estimation.

</details>


### [151] [A Deep Learning-based surrogate model for Severe Accidents in nuclear reactors using ASTEC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04450)
*Alessandro Longhi,Danny Lathouwers,Zoltán Perkó*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的代理模型，结合自编码器和神经常微分方程，将ASTEC严重事故模拟器的计算时间从数天缩短至一分钟以内，实现约80个物理变量的同步预测，并在最高50000时间步下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统严重事故模拟器（如ASTEC）计算耗时长达数天，无法用于核电站操作员的实时培训与应急响应，亟需一种能够加速仿真且保持足够精度的替代方法。

Method: 构建一个两阶段深度学习代理模型：(1) 通过自编码器（AutoEncoder）对高维时空场进行超过300倍的降维；(2) 利用神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）进行时间步进预测。模型使用ASTEC模拟的全厂断电（SBO）和冷却剂丧失事故（LOCA）场景数据进行训练。

Result: 模型能同时预测约80个物理变量（标量+场量），在CPU和GPU上均能在1分钟内完成40小时仿真，自回归滚动预测稳定超过50000时间步，降维因子超过300倍。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统评估了深度学习代理模型在逼近ASTEC高度非线性严重事故物理中的能力与局限，证明了该方法在实时核安全仿真中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Integral codes like the Accident Source Term Evaluation Code (ASTEC) are powerful tools to study the physics of Severe Accidents (SAs) in nuclear reactors. Real time SA simulators can also be helpful in training operators of nuclear plants to react correctly to malfunctions. However, SA simulators can take up to several days per simulation, making their use infeasible for real time applications. In this work we show how to speed up a SA simulator with a fast, Deep Learning based (DL), surrogate model (SM). The SM is built as a combination of a dimensionality reduction stage, via an AutoEncoder, and a time-stepping stage, via a Neural Ordinary Differential Equation. The data on which the SM is trained are obtained from the ASTEC simulator, by sampling a set of operator actions for station blackout (SBO) and loss-of-coolant accidents (LOCA). The objective of the developed SM is to approximate multiple spatio-temporal fields for the thermal-hydraulic physics, core degradation, and fission product release modules in ASTEC's vessel domain. The SM predicts simultaneously around $80$ different physical variables (both scalar and fields), maintaining a stable autoregressive rollout up to $50$ thousand time steps. In addition, the AutoEncoder achieves a dimensionality reduction by a factor of over $300$, which allows the SM to predict up to $40$ hours of simulation in under a minute, both on CPU and GPU. This work is the first study of the capabilities and limits of DL based surrogate modeling in approximating the challenging, highly non-linear physics of ASTEC.

</details>


### [152] [Operator-on-F complements value-equivalence: a planning-time diagnostic for latent world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04464)
*Donna Vakalis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的世界模型诊断方法——operator-on-F，通过比较模型在可观测子集F上的k步潜变量推前与真实环境的差异，来检测奖励预测误差无法捕捉的规划相关错误。在TD-MPC2模型规模扫描实验中，operator error在0.28-2.62之间变化（10倍差异），与回报损失的秩相关为-0.90，而奖励预测误差仅0.028-0.091（3倍差异），与回报相关性弱。该方法能有效区分不同架构的世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型强化学习中，世界模型评估通常只检查奖励和价值的预测能力，但这会遗漏模型中影响规划质量的潜变量展开误差。需要一个能够捕捉这些规划相关错误的互补诊断方法。

Method: 提出operator-on-F诊断方法，在可观测子集F上比较模型的k步潜变量推前（pushforward）与环境真实推前之间的差异，使用模型自身的预测器进行对比。

Result: 在TD-MPC2模型规模扫描中：operator error范围0.28-2.62（约10倍变化），与回报损失秩相关-0.90；而奖励预测误差仅0.028-0.091（约3倍变化），与回报相关性很弱（Spearman -0.10和-0.30）。在317M参数规模时operator error高达2.62，规划回报崩至0.9。该方法还能有效区分不同架构（TD-MPC2 vs 纯SSL潜变量世界模型）。

Conclusion: operator-on-F是一种有效的世界模型诊断工具，能够检测奖励预测误差无法捕捉的规划相关错误，补充而非替代值等价（value-equivalence）方法。

Abstract: World-model evaluation for model-based reinforcement learning typically asks whether the learned model predicts reward and value well, which can leave planning-relevant errors in the model's latent rollouts unmeasured. We introduce a complementary diagnostic, operator-on-F, that compares a model's k-step latent pushforward to the environment's on an observable subset F, using the model's own predictor. On a TD-MPC2 size sweep over cheetah-run, reward-prediction error stays within [0.028, 0.091] for every model size - only about 3x variation - so an unnormalized reward-fit check has narrow resolution to distinguish them; the (unnormalized) Bellman residual and reward error themselves have weak relationships with return (Spearman -0.10 and -0.30). Operator error spans 0.28 to 2.62 over the same sizes. At 317M the operator error is 2.62 - an order of magnitude above the 0.28-0.36 cluster - and the planning return collapses to 0.9, while reward-prediction error (0.091) is the highest of the five but stays within the same small [0.028, 0.091] range as the rest of the sweep. The rank correlation between operator error and return loss is -0.90 (anchor-bootstrap 95% CI [-0.90, -0.70] at n=5 sizes; leave-one-out removal of any single size leaves it at -0.80 or stronger). The operator also returns informative, architecture-discriminating estimates in a cross-architecture comparison between TD-MPC2 and a pure-SSL latent world model. The operator diagnostic complements value-equivalence rather than replacing it.

</details>


### [153] [Regime-Conditional Stabilisation of LLM-Augmented Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04470)
*Faid Keddouri,Sohaib Houhou,Aissa Boulmerka,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在离策略多智能体强化学习（MARL）中动态更新LLM生成的奖励权重会违反势能基奖励塑形（PBRS）的平稳性假设，并污染经验回放缓冲区。提出两种稳定策略（基于阶段的冻结调度和指数移动平均平滑），并在三个环境中验证了不同基线能力水平下的三机制分类：增强型（基线有效，EMA提升12.3%）、必要型（基线无效，任何塑形都能解锁任务）和补充型（基线接近饱和，稳定塑形保持性能）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM为MARL提供奖励信号的研究缺乏对训练动态的深入理解，特别是离策略更新中奖励权重动态变化带来的平稳性破坏问题尚未被系统分析。

Method: 1) 理论分析动态奖励权重对PBRS平稳性假设的破坏机制；2) 提出Phase-Based Freeze Schedule和EMA平滑两种稳定策略；3) 在Simple Spread、Level-Based Foraging和SMAC 3m三个环境中使用QMIX算法进行实验验证，辅以VDN扩展实验。

Result: 在增强型机制（Simple Spread）中，基线准确率74.4%，EMA提升至86.7%（+12.3pp），动态更新则崩溃至15.2%；在必要型机制（LBF）中，基线仅0.1%，任何塑形均解锁至95.9%；在补充型机制（SMAC 3m）中，基线98.8%，稳定塑形保持99.9%，不稳定塑形增加方差无增益。

Conclusion: 奖励信号平稳性是动态LLM塑形在MARL中的必要设计约束，而基线能力水平决定塑形效果的三机制分类可作为预测LLM塑形效果的实用指标。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a natural interface for translating human objectives into reward signals for cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), yet the training-time dynamics of this integration remain poorly understood. We show that dynamically updating LLM-generated reward weights during off-policy MARL violates the stationarity assumption of Potential-Based Reward Shaping (PBRS) and contaminates the experience replay buffer, whose stored transitions carry reward labels computed under stale shaping weights. We characterise the result as a regime-dependent failure whose severity depends on how competent the unshaped baseline already is. To control it we propose two stabilisation strategies: a Phase-Based Freeze Schedule that enforces strict stationarity within training phases, and Exponential Moving Average (EMA) smoothing that bounds per-episode weight drift. We evaluate across three cooperative environments and five random seeds with QMIX, complemented by an exploratory VDN extension, yielding a three-regime taxonomy. In the augmentative regime (Simple Spread), where the baseline is functional (74.4 %), EMA significantly improves success to 86.7 % ($+12.3$ pp, $p<0.01$) while naive dynamic updates collapse it to 15.2 %. In the essential regime (Level-Based Foraging), where the baseline is broken (0.1 %), any shaping unlocks the task (95.9 % under EMA). In the supplementary regime (SMAC 3m), where the baseline is near-saturated (98.8 %), stabilised shaping preserves performance (99.9 %) while unstabilised shaping adds variance without gain. These findings establish reward-signal stationarity as a necessary design constraint and indicate that regime placement is a practical predictor of whether dynamic LLM shaping helps or harms.

</details>


### [154] [LeukocyteCount: Automatic Identification and Counting for leukocytes using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04486)
*Ahmed M. Sayed,Sondos A. Refaat,Abdallah M. Mostafa,Mariam S. El-Rahmany,Ensaf Hussein Mohamed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的自动化解决方案，通过集成Yolov5检测、MobileNetV2和逻辑回归分类的混合模型，对白细胞进行检测、计数和四类分类，在BCCD数据集上实现了98%的检测准确率和99.04%的分类准确率，同时红细胞检测模块F1分数达99.73%，显著优于传统手工方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统白细胞计数和分类方法依赖人工操作，耗时且易出错，影响了疾病诊断和监测的准确性与可靠性，亟需自动化解决方案来克服这些局限。

Method: 采用混合机器学习模型：Yolov5用于白细胞检测，微调的预训练MobileNetV2结合逻辑回归分类器用于白细胞识别、计数及四类分类，使用BCCD数据集进行训练和验证。

Result: Yolov5检测准确率98%，MobileNetV2与逻辑回归分类准确率99.04%，YOLOv5的红细胞检测模块F1分数99.73%，优于基线。

Conclusion: 该混合模型有望变革传统实验室白细胞分析实践，为实现更准确、高效、可靠的疾病诊断和监测提供可行路径。

Abstract: Diagnosing and monitoring diseases frequently involves the analysis of human biological samples, with blood analysis being pivotal. Specifically, leukocytes, or white blood cells (WBCs), are essential markers for evaluating the body's defense mechanisms against infections. Traditional methods for WBC counting and classification are labor-intensive and prone to inaccuracies, primarily due to human error. The conventional processes for blood cell analysis, especially those concerning WBCs, are beset with difficulties. These include the laborious nature of manual counting and the susceptibility to errors, which can significantly impact the accuracy and reliability of disease diagnosis and monitoring. This study proposes an automated, machine learning-based solution aimed at mitigating the identified challenges. By employing a hybrid model that integrates Yolov5 for the detection of WBCs, coupled with a finely tuned, pre-trained MobileNetV2 model and a Logistic Regression classifier, the study innovates in the accurate identification, counting, and classification of WBCs into four distinct types. The methodology leverages the BCCD dataset for training and validation purposes. The application of the proposed hybrid machine learning model has yielded remarkable results, demonstrating a detection accuracy rate of 98\% through the Yolov5 stage, and an unparalleled classification accuracy of 99.04\% in subsequent stages utilizing MobileNetV2 and Logistic Regression. Additionally, Our proposed YOLOv5-based RBC detection module achieves an F1 score of 99.73\%, which outperforms the baseline. These findings underscore the model's potential in transforming traditional laboratory practices for WBC analysis, offering a path towards more accurate, efficient, and reliable disease diagnostics and monitoring.

</details>


### [155] [Two Black Boxes, One Solver: Encoder Probing and Decoder Attribution for Neural Multi-Attribute VRP under Hard-Mask and Recourse Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04487)
*Sohaib Afifi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套统一的协议，用于打开多属性车辆路径问题（MAVRP）神经自回归求解器的编码器和解码器黑箱。编码器侧通过线性探针、自组织度量、秩基丰富度分析和发现方向分析等方法，揭示潜在表示如何在图、节点和边层面表征约束族；解码器侧使用梯度、积分梯度和DeepLIFT三种归因方法，从溯因、对比（与最佳可行替代方案）和反事实（最小输入变化以改变动作或恢复可行性）三个角度解释决策。实验发现：图归纳偏置提升编码器可预测性和解码器合理性；MoE编码器以分布式而非轴对齐方式表征约束；Recourse训练机制（而非仅软掩码）使策略能有效表征不可行性并生成可恢复可行性的反事实解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经自回归求解器在MAVRP上取得了有竞争力的成本，但缺乏每步决策的可解释性，导致调度员无法验证、接受或比较这些解。现有黑箱求解器无法提供逐步骤的决策依据，阻碍了实际应用中的信任和采纳。

Method: 提出统一的编码器-解码器可解释性协议。编码器侧：线性探针、自组织度量（CLS/SLS）、秩基丰富度、发现方向分析与干预验证。解码器侧：三种归因方法（梯度、积分梯度、DeepLIFT）×三种视角（溯因、对比、反事实）。在6种变体（3编码器×2解码器）上评估保真度、集中度、稳定性、合理性和可操作性五个指标。

Result: (1) 图归纳偏置同时提升编码器的表征可预测性和解码器的合理性；(2) MoE编码器以分布式（非轴对齐）方式表征约束；(3) Recourse训练机制（而非仅软掩码）产生有用表征不可行性的策略，能生成"使之可行"的反事实解释，而Hard-Mask策略即使外部输入不可行替代方案也无法产生。

Conclusion: 本研究系统性地打开了MAVRP神经求解器的编码器和解码器黑箱，为理解神经组合优化模型的约束表征和决策推理提供了方法论框架和实证见解，有助于提升神经求解器在实际调度场景中的可信度和实用性。

Abstract: Neural autoregressive solvers for the Multi-Attribute Vehicle Routing Problem (MAVRP) reach competitive cost but offer no per-step justification, a problem when dispatchers must validate, accept, or compare them. We open two complementary black boxes in one protocol. On the encoder side, linear probes, spontaneous-organization metrics, rank-based richness measures, and discovered-direction analyses with intervention validation characterize how the latent represents constraint families at the graph, node, and edge level. On the decoder side, three attribution methods (gradient, integrated gradients, DeepLIFT) feed three reading angles: abductive, contrastive against the best feasible alternative, and counterfactual (smallest input change that switches the action or restores feasibility). Explanations are scored on fidelity, concentration, stability, sanity, and actionability. Across six variants combining three encoders (Attention baseline, Unimp, UnimpMoe) with two decoders (Hard-Mask, Recourse), we find that graph inductive bias improves both representational predictability and decoder sanity, that the Mixture-of-Experts encoder represents constraints in a distributed rather than axis-aligned way, and that the Recourse training regime, not merely its softer mask, produces policies that represent infeasibility usefully, exposing make-feasible counterfactuals that Hard-Mask policies fail to produce even when fed infeasible alternatives externally.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond travel mode: urban context shapes active mobility's mental health effects over time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04520)
*Shujuan Chen,Yue Li,Ying Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于264,168名英国成年人的因果机器学习与因果深度学习分析，发现主动出行对心理健康的个性化效应存在巨大不平等，且随时间推移加剧，这种不平等主要由城市环境（绿色空间、安全、污染、 deprivation）而非基因因素驱动。在最支持性环境中，81.8%的个体获得高于平均水平的心理健康收益（焦虑风险降低26.4%），而在最不支持环境中仅10.4%的个体受益（降低7.4%），表明无条件推广主动出行可能加剧健康不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究主动出行（步行、骑行等）是否能带来公平的心理健康收益，以及个体间和地区间的效应异质性及其随时间变化趋势，为主动出行推广政策提供公平性视角的实证依据。

Method: 采用因果机器学习（causal machine learning）和因果深度学习（causal deep learning）方法，对264,168名英国成年人的纵向数据进行分析，评估主动出行对焦虑、抑郁及常见精神障碍的个性化因果效应，并纳入城市环境（绿色空间、安全、污染、剥夺程度）和遗传因素进行效应修饰分析。

Result: 主动出行对焦虑风险的个性化效应范围从减少40.6%到增加10.1%（随访期），相比基线期（减少10.4%到增加0.1%）显著扩大；在绿色、安全、低污染、低剥夺环境中，81.8%的个体获得高于平均水平的收益，平均焦虑风险降低26.4%；在最不支持的环境中，仅10.4%的个体受益，平均降低7.4%；城市紧凑形态通过非线性交互放大支持性环境中的收益；遗传修饰效应可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 主动出行的心理健康收益存在显著的个体间和情境间不平等，且随时间推移加剧，主要受城市环境因素而非遗传因素驱动；若不考虑个体和情境差异，普遍推广主动出行可能反而扩大健康不平等。

Abstract: Active mobility is widely promoted for sustainable and healthier living, but whether it translates into equitable mental health benefits across individuals and places over time remains unknown. Using causal machine learning and causal deep learning in 264168 UK adults, we find substantial inequalities in individualized effects of active mobility on anxiety, depression, and common mental disorders. These inequalities widen over time and are strongly structured by urban context. For example, anxiety risk at follow-up ranges from a 40.6% reduction to a 10.1% increase across individuals, versus a 10.4% reduction to a 0.1% increase at baseline. Benefits are greatest in greener, safer, less polluted, and less deprived neighborhood environments, with 81.8% of individuals experiencing above-average benefits and mean anxiety risk reduced by 26.4%, versus 10.4% of individuals and 7.4% reduction in the least supportive environments. Urban compact form further modifies these effects through nonlinear interactions with neighborhood environments, amplifying benefits only under supportive conditions. Despite these strong environmental gradients, genetic moderation is negligible. These findings suggest universal active mobility promotion could widen health inequalities if individual and contextual differences are not accounted for.

</details>


### [157] [Lyapunov-Guided Training for Hardware-Safe Neural Networks Under Fixed-Point Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04531)
*Anis Hamadouche,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对低精度神经网络在硬件级包装算术下因二进制补码溢出导致精度退化的问题，本文提出了一种基于李雅普诺夫稳定的量化框架，通过逐层单调投影约束隐藏状态能量，将激活溢出率从11%以上降至0.012%以下，在12位定点精度下恢复稳定学习并达到86.55%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度神经网络有利于资源受限硬件，但定点算术的二进制补码溢出包装会改变隐藏激活的幅度和符号，导致不稳定的数值误差传播和严重精度退化，而理想化量化模型常隐藏这些故障模式。

Method: 提出李雅普诺夫稳定量化框架：通过逐层李雅普诺夫函数监控隐藏状态能量，并施加单调投影强制状态演化有界且非递增。在紧凑型patch-based transformer上进行实验，采用后训练量化和量化感知训练，定点位宽4-16位。

Result: 未约束的包装量化感知训练在6-16位下精度崩溃至接近随机水平，激活溢出率超11%；而所提单调李雅普诺夫投影将激活溢出率抑制至0.012%以下，在12位下恢复稳定学习，准确率达86.55%。

Conclusion: 基于李雅普诺夫的状态控制可作为硬件感知的稳定机制，实现可靠的定点神经网络推理与训练。

Abstract: Low-precision neural networks are attractive for resource-constrained hardware, but fixed-point arithmetic introduces failure modes that are often hidden by idealised quantisation models. In particular, two's-complement overflow wrapping can corrupt hidden activations by changing both their magnitude and sign, leading to unstable numerical error propagation and severe accuracy degradation. This paper proposes a Lyapunov-stabilised quantisation framework for low-precision neural networks operating under hardware-style wrapping arithmetic. The hidden-state energy is monitored through a layerwise Lyapunov function, and a monotone projection is applied to enforce bounded and non-increasing state evolution across depth. The method is evaluated on MNIST using a compact patch-based transformer under post-training quantisation and quantisation-aware training with fixed-point bit-widths from 4 to 16 bits. Monte Carlo results show that unconstrained wrapped quantisation-aware training collapses to near-chance accuracy across 6-16 bits, with activation overflow rates exceeding 11%. In contrast, the proposed monotone Lyapunov projection suppresses activation overflow to below 0.012% and restores stable low-precision learning, achieving 86.55% accuracy at 12 bits. These results demonstrate that Lyapunov-based state control can act as a hardware-aware stabilisation mechanism for reliable fixed-point neural inference and training.

</details>


### [158] [ManifoldFlow: SPD-Relaxed Stiefel Layers with Learnable Singular Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04535)
*Haiwen Yi,Xinyuan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ManifoldFlow提出了一种最小化松弛的Stiefel层，通过可学习正定谱（SPD）代替固定单位奇异值，在保持正交基约束的同时实现方向相关的衰减或放大，实验表明在序列、表格和图像任务中优于固定谱Stiefel层，尤其适用于语言模型投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准正交/Stiefel层（Orthogonal/Stiefel layers）固定所有奇异值为1，对需要正交基但还需要方向相关衰减或放大的场景（如语言模型投影）施加了过强约束。

Method: 将权重矩阵分解为 W = Q S^{1/2}，其中 Q 保持在 Stiefel 流形上（Q^T Q = I），S 为正定矩阵（SPD）。W^T W = S，即 S 的特征值就是权重奇异值的平方，从而通过特征值裁剪实现直接的奇异值控制。

Result: 在配对序列、表格和图像实验中，可学习SPD谱优于固定谱Stiefel层，在循环语言模型投影中提升最大。分类器头部的边界案例澄清了ManifoldFlow的适用范围。

Conclusion: ManifoldFlow不是通用的密集层替代方案，而是针对正交基先验有用且谱可学习的Stiefel松弛方法。当基应该正交时，其谱不必冻结。

Abstract: Orthogonal and Stiefel layers give neural weights exact spectral control, but they also impose a strong modeling constraint: all represented singular values are fixed at one. Many settings that benefit from an orthonormal basis still need direction-dependent attenuation or amplification. We introduce ManifoldFlow, a minimal relaxation of a fixed-spectrum Stiefel layer that keeps the basis on the Stiefel manifold while learning a bounded positive spectrum through W = Q S^{1/2}, with Q^T Q = I and S positive definite. Since W^T W = S, the eigenvalues of S are exactly the squared singular values of the realized weight, making eigenvalue clipping a direct singular-value control mechanism. Across paired sequence, tabular, and image experiments, the learnable SPD spectrum improves the fixed-spectrum Stiefel counterpart in the reported settings where the Stiefel prior is useful, with the largest gains in recurrent language-model projections. Boundary cases in convolutional classifier heads clarify the intended scope: ManifoldFlow is not a universal dense-layer replacement, but a spectrum-learnable Stiefel relaxation for settings where an orthonormal basis is a useful prior. When the basis should be orthonormal, its spectrum need not be frozen. Code available at https://github.com/Hik289/manifold_flow

</details>


### [159] [Auto: The AGI Compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04542)
*Jaber Jaber,Osama Jaber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto编译器，将LLM Agent的运行时行为记录、分析、编译为WebAssembly认知二进制文件，通过分层运行时和校准守卫实现高效执行，在AUTO-BENCH基准上实现87.1%的确定性行为提取，边际成本从59降至2微美元/项，同时保持96.9%的正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM Agent每次运行都从头开始逐token推理，虽然效果出色但昂贵、缓慢且无边界。作者希望将Agent行为中确定性部分自动提取为永久、可验证、近乎免费的技能，避免重复计算。

Method: Auto编译器包含三阶段：1）记录Agent行为并测量确定性部分；2）将确定性行为提取为验证程序或蒸馏专家模型；3）发射为WebAssembly认知二进制文件。运行时采用分层架构，通过校准守卫（calibrated guards）进行物理沙箱强制执行，守卫触发时回退到参考Agent并重新编译。

Result: 在AUTO-BENCH基准上，560个记录中87.1%为见证确定性（四个任务族中三个达100%）。在300项流式任务中，闭环编译产生三代构件，边际成本从59降至2微美元/项（端到端6.4倍），在见证输入上保持96.9%正确率且零错误。但校准不严会导致48.9%错误，参考模型不可靠会导致重编失败。

Conclusion: 校准的精度和参考Agent的保真度，而非模型能力本身，决定了低成本执行能否保持正确性。Auto展示了将LLM Agent行为编译为永久、廉价、可验证技能的可行路径。

Abstract: Every LLM agent run re-derives its behavior token by token on a frontier model: brilliant, expensive, slow, and unbounded. We present Auto, a compiler that records live agent behavior, measures which parts are secretly deterministic, extracts them into verified programs or distilled specialists, and emits cognition binaries: WebAssembly artifacts whose manifests carry measured guarantees and whose declared capabilities are physically enforced by the sandbox. A tiered runtime executes compiled behavior behind conformally calibrated guards; guard trips deopt to the reference agent, and the captured trace recompiles back down, so nothing is figured out twice. We use "AGI compiler" in one narrow, testable sense: a system that autonomously converts novel experience into permanent, verified, near-free skill while measuring what it does not know. On AUTO-BENCH, a benchmark we introduce and pre-register, 87.1% of 560 recorded frontier-agent spans are witnessed-deterministic (three of the four censused task families measure 100.0%). On a 300-item stream with three scheduled distribution shifts, the closed loop compiles three artifact generations and drives marginal cost from 59 to 2 micro-dollars per item (6.4x end-to-end) at 96.9% parity on witnessed inputs with zero errors. The same stream also quantifies the failure modes: a loose guard silently mislabels 48.9% of compiled answers, and an unfaithful deopt reference causes the verification gate to refuse recompilation. Calibration and reference fidelity, not model capability, decide whether cheap stays correct. Code: https://github.com/RightNow-AI/auto

</details>


### [160] [Predicting Therapeutic Outcome via Aligning Patient-Specific Knowledge Graph and Gene-Level Perturbation Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04557)
*Dongmin Bang,Sugyun An,Inyoung Sung,Ilho Yun,Sun Kim,Sangseon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PREDIKTOR是一个患者中心的多视图框架，通过对齐个性化基因调控网络视图和可迁移的转录组扰动视图来预测临床药物反应，在TCGA和I-SPY2试验中优于现有方法，并提供可解释的基因和通路归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，临床反应标签和后处理分子谱的稀缺阻碍了治疗反应预测；预训练迁移学习模型难以解释且不稳定，而知识图谱方法静态且无法捕捉药物诱导的转录组扰动动态。

Method: PREDIKTOR构建双视图框架：1) 个性化视图：使用DysRegNet构建个体化基因调控网络，结合DrugBank药物-靶点链接，经图神经网络编码得到药物中心嵌入；2) 扰动视图：基于LINCS L1000预训练的条件特异性基因-基因注意力模型生成模拟扰动转录组谱。通过CLIP风格对比学习对齐两视图，拼接后用于端到端反应分类。

Result: 在TCGA上，PREDIKTOR在患者、药物和组织分割评估中一致优于基线方法；在I-SPY2试验中零样本迁移，AUROC提升5.6%。对齐的嵌入产生稳定的基因和通路归因，可恢复已知机制。

Conclusion: PREDIKTOR通过融合可解释的个性化网络视图和可迁移的转录组扰动视图，实现了准确、可解释的药物反应预测，支持可操作的精准肿瘤学。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of patient-specific therapeutic response from pre-treatment transcriptomes is hindered by the scarcity of matched clinical response labels and post-treatment molecular profiles. Preclinical transfer-learning models can simulate drug-induced expression changes but are often hard to interpret and unstable, whereas knowledge-graph methods provide mechanistic context yet remain static and fail to capture drug-induced transcriptomic perturbation dynamics. We propose PREDIKTOR, a patient-centered multi-view framework that aligns a personalized network view with a transferable transcriptomic perturbation view to predict clinical drug response. For each patient, we construct an individualized gene regulatory network from tumor expression using DysRegNet and augment it with drug-target links from DrugBank; a graph neural encoder yields a drug-centric, mechanistically grounded embedding. In parallel, a frozen condition-specific gene-gene attention model pretrained on LINCS L1000 generates a simulated post-perturbation transcriptomic profile for the same patient-drug pair. We align the two views in a shared latent space via a CLIP-style contrastive objective with drug-context hard negatives, then concatenate the representations for end-to-end response classification. On TCGA, PREDIKTOR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines under patient-, drug-, and tissue-split evaluations, and transfers zero-shot to the I-SPY2 trial, improving AUROC by 5.6% over competing methods. The aligned embeddings yield stable gene and pathway attributions that recover known mechanisms, supporting actionable and interpretable precision oncology.

</details>


### [161] [A Few Teacher Steps Go a Long Way: Cost-Efficient On-Policy Data Augmentation for Agent Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04574)
*Junze Ye,Jiayi Cheng,Miao Lu,Michal Mankowski,Jose Blanchet,Mohsen Bayati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将LLM智能体的在线数据构建形式化为预算分配问题，研究了在匹配监督资源下，如何分配教师输出（完整演示、延续长度、结果过滤、覆盖范围）以优化行为克隆效果。实验表明，在学员诱导的上下文中提供少量无过滤的教师步骤，比纯行为克隆或更长/更严格过滤的教师完成更成本高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯行为克隆中使用教师完整演示会导致训练时教师诱导的上下文与测试时学员诱导的上下文不匹配，现有工作通过查询学员所处上下文的教师输出来解决此问题，但缺乏对如何最优分配有限监督资源的系统研究。

Method: 通过形式化设计空间（包括rollout策略、切换时间分布、延续视界、过滤规则）和两类成本（教师推理成本与监督保留成本），将在线数据构建建模为预算分配问题，并在HotpotQA、ALFWorld、Terminal-Bench-Dev三个基准上比较不同分配策略的效果。

Result: 在匹配预算下，学员诱导上下文中的有界无过滤教师延续优于纯行为克隆；在HotpotQA和ALFWorld上，少量步骤的延续匹配或超越了成功过滤和关键上下文过滤的替代方案。

Conclusion: 在学员诱导的上下文中放置少量教师步骤，比更长或更严格筛选的教师完成是一种更成本高效的监督分配策略。

Abstract: For LLM agents, supervised fine-tuning is not only about teacher labels' quality, but also about which interaction contexts those labels condition on. Pure behavioral cloning uses full teacher demonstrations, creating a mismatch between teacher-induced contexts seen in training and student-induced contexts encountered at test time. Recent work addresses this mismatch by querying a teacher at contexts reached by the student, often with increasingly elaborate filtering of the teacher's continuations. We instead frame on-policy data construction as a budget-allocation problem: under matched supervision resources, should teacher output be spent on more start-to-finish demos, longer continuations, outcome filtering, or broader coverage of learner-induced contexts? We formalize this design space through the rollout policy, switch-time distribution, continuation horizon, filtering rules, and two complementary costs: teacher inference generated before filtering and teacher supervision retained for SFT. Across HotpotQA, ALFWorld, and Terminal-Bench-Dev, bounded unfiltered teacher continuations at learner-induced contexts improve over pure behavioral cloning at matched budgets. On HotpotQA and ALFWorld, where we run the full comparison, few-step continuations match or exceed success-filtered and critical-context-filtered alternatives. Our findings suggest that a few teacher steps, placed at learner-induced contexts, can be a more cost-efficient supervision allocation than longer or more heavily curated teacher completions.

</details>


### [162] [Beyond the Need for Speed: Energy-Aware Code Generation via Simulation-Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04577)
*Saurabhsingh Rajput,Tushar Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Green Tea，通过确定性架构仿真替代物理硬件测量，构建含350万次评估的能效代码数据集，结合监督微调与闭环强化学习（GRPO）训练能感知能耗的代码生成模型，并引入CARET指标评估部署就绪性。在143个保留问题上实现12.63%的CARET提升，58.4%的有效输出超越人类专家能效，同时揭示IPC指标在67.8%的问题上会误导能效排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型严格优化功能正确性，将软件能效作为未优化的副产品。训练能效感知模型需要可重复的规模化反馈，但物理硬件测量因方差过大无法可靠提供。

Method: 1) 用确定性架构仿真替代硬件分析，构建Green Tea语料库（1474个C++问题，350万次评估）；2) 在能效对比对上监督微调，再用仿真闭环的GRPO强化学习训练；3) 提出CARET指标（正确性调整的能耗总量减少），惩罚牺牲功能换取能效的行为。

Result: 在143个保留问题上实现12.63% CARET，比仅微调提升近3倍；58.4%有效输出能效超越人类专家；揭示IPC指标在67.8%问题上错误排序能效，证明直接能耗仿真的必要性。

Conclusion: 确定性仿真闭环训练能有效生成能效代码，直接能耗仿真不可或缺，发布的数据集和基础设施可节省26.3万CPU小时的计算资源，赋能社区部署能效代码生成模型。

Abstract: Code models strictly prioritize functional correctness, leaving software energy efficiency as an unoptimized byproduct. Training models to generate energy-efficient code requires reproducible feedback at scale, which physical hardware measurement cannot reliably provide due to variance.
  In this paper, we replace hardware profiling with a deterministic architectural simulation harness to build Green Tea, a corpus of $3.5$ million evaluations across $1{,}474$ C++ problems. We train an energy-aware code model via supervised fine-tuning on energy-contrastive pairs, followed by closed-loop reinforcement learning (GRPO) using simulation-in-the-loop feedback. To rigorously evaluate deployment readiness, we introduce the Correctness-Adjusted Reduction in Energy Total (CARET), a metric that explicitly penalizes code that sacrifices functionality for efficiency.
  On $143$ held-out problems, our simulation-in-the-loop pipeline achieves $12.63\%$ CARET, nearly tripling the gain of fine-tuning alone, and successfully beats the energy efficiency of human-expert references on $58.4\%$ of its valid outputs. Furthermore, our analysis exposes the IPC trap: standard throughput proxies like Instructions-Per-Cycle (IPC) actively misrank true energy efficiency on $67.8\%$ of problems, proving the absolute necessity of direct energy simulation. By releasing our dataset and infrastructure, we bypass the $263{,}000$ CPU-hours required for reproduction, structurally empowering the community to deploy inherently energy-efficient code generation models.

</details>


### [163] [Machine Learning for Depression Screening and Intervention: an Original Circadian Rhythm Score-based Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04648)
*Bin Wang,Shuo Lian,Yuanyuan Hou,Dexian Wang,Peilan He,Feng Hong,Yanwei Yu,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出昼夜节律评分（CRS）压缩多领域日常行为数据，结合梯度提升树和SHAP分析构建可解释的抑郁筛查框架，在CHARLS数据集上取得0.825的ROC-AUC，并识别出可干预的行为阈值（如约300 MET-min/周的最低有效运动量和约65分钟的最佳恢复性午睡时长）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抑郁筛查方法受限于碎片化的昼夜节律指标、缺乏可解释性以及缺乏面向干预的分析，通常孤立分析睡眠、活动和社交行为，未能捕捉其联合昼夜节律结构。

Method: 提出CRS（昼夜节律评分）压缩多领域日常行为为统一表示，采用非负约束保留行为语义；基于梯度提升树和SHAP分析构建可解释筛查框架，结合交互建模和反事实回归估计异质性和剂量依赖性行为效应。

Result: 在CHARLS数据集（n=15,233）上实现0.825的ROC-AUC；单变量CRS几乎保持完整预测能力（近无损压缩）；识别出约300 MET-min/周的最低有效运动剂量和约65分钟的最佳恢复性午睡时长。

Conclusion: 本文通过监督表示学习与可解释建模的融合，为抑郁筛查和干预感知的医疗数据挖掘提供了一个可扩展框架。

Abstract: Depression screening from large-scale behavioral data is challenged by fragmented circadian indicators, limited interpretability, and the lack of intervention-oriented analysis. Existing approaches typically analyze sleep, activity, and social behaviors in isolation, failing to capture their joint circadian structure. To address this limitation, we first propose the Circadian Rhythm Score (CRS), a composite index that compresses multi-domain daily behaviors into a unified representation of circadian rhythm. CRS is constructed to maximize discriminative power for depression screening while preserving behavioral semantics through non-negativity constraints. Empirical results demonstrate near-lossless compression, where a single CRS retains almost the full predictive capability compared with multiple raw behavioral indicators. Building upon CRS, we develop an interpretable depression screening framework based on gradient-boosted trees and SHAP analysis, revealing nonlinear and saturation-like associations between circadian rhythm and depression risk. Beyond risk prediction, we further integrate interaction modeling and counterfactual regression to estimate heterogeneous and dose-dependent behavioral effects, enabling intervention-oriented reasoning under different circadian contexts. Experiments on the China Health and Retirement Longitudinal Study (CHARLS, n=15,233), demonstrate robust screening performance (ROC-AUC=0.825) and identify actionable behavioral thresholds, including a minimum effective exercise dose of approximately 300 MET-min/week and an optimal restorative nap duration of approximately 65 minutes for sleep-deprived individuals. By bridging supervised representation learning and interpretable modeling, this work provides a scalable framework for depression screening and intervention-aware healthcare data mining.

</details>


### [164] [Score Distributions, Not Cells: Evaluating Single-Cell Perturbations Under Class Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04595)
*Youssef Marrakchi,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 单细胞扰动数据中，由于扰动间高度重叠，逐细胞分类准确率无法反映模型质量。本文提出Classifier Discrimination Score (CDS)，通过聚合扰动内所有细胞的概率向量形成群体轮廓进行排序，无需重新训练即可从弱模型恢复近乎完美的识别性能，显著优于逐细胞准确率和伪批量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞扰动数据违反了传统分类问题中类别大致可分的假设——两种扰动产生的细胞群体高度重叠，单个细胞无法可靠地归属到某一类。此时逐细胞准确率衡量的实际上是重叠程度而非模型质量，现有基准测试中线性分类器、MLP和Transformer的macro-F1均停滞在0.2-0.3，亟需新的评估指标。

Method: 提出Classifier Discrimination Score (CDS)：将分类器对每个细胞的概率向量在扰动内所有细胞上取平均，得到该扰动的群体轮廓（population profile），然后基于该轮廓对候选扰动进行排序。取排名最高的类别即得到预测的扰动。该方法无需重新训练，时间复杂度为线性，可应用于任意弱分类器。

Result: 在Tahoe-100M和Virtual Cell Challenge两个数据集上，CDS从原本macro-F1仅0.2-0.3的弱模型中恢复了近乎完美的扰动识别性能。与已有方法Perturbation Discrimination Score (PDS)相比，CDS在识别真实扰动方面更可靠，且随着细胞数量减少，优势进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 逐细胞准确率和基于原始表达量的伪批量评分（如PDS）在比较扰动模型时应谨慎使用，因为它们可能错误地排名真实扰动，进而误导对模型的评估。CDS通过在判别空间而非原始表达空间取平均，提供了更可靠的扰动级评估指标。

Abstract: Most classification problems assume the classes are roughly separable, so that an individual sample can usually be assigned to one class. Single-cell perturbation data violates this assumption: two perturbations can produce different populations of cells while overlapping so much that an individual cell could belong to either. Per-cell accuracy then measures this overlap rather than model quality. We see this on Tahoe-100M and the Virtual Cell Challenge, where a linear classifier, an MLP, and a Transformer all plateau near macro-F1 0.2-0.3 even though almost every pair of perturbations is statistically distinguishable.
  The fix is to score perturbations across the whole population rather than cell by cell. We average a classifier's per-cell probability vectors over all cells of a perturbation to form a population profile, then rank candidate perturbations by this profile; we call the resulting score the Classifier Discrimination Score (CDS). Taking the top-ranked class recovers the winning perturbation. It needs no retraining, costs linear time in the number of cells, and recovers near-perfect identification from the same weak models. CDS differs from the pseudobulk-based Perturbation Discrimination Score (PDS) used in recent benchmarks only in where the average is taken, raw gene expression for PDS versus a learned discriminative space for CDS, and identifies the true perturbation more reliably on both datasets, with the gap widening as cells grow scarce. Because a metric that misranks the ground truth will misrank the models scored against it, per-cell accuracy and raw-pseudobulk scores should be used with caution when comparing perturbation models.

</details>


### [165] [URSA: Chemistry-Aware Benchmark for Utilitarian Retrosynthesis Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04688)
*Bogdan Zagribelnyy,Ivan Ilin,Nikita Bondarev,Anton Morgunov,Arkadii Lin,Maksim Kuznetsov,Rim Shayakhmetov,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了URSA评估框架，用于在化学合理性和实际可用性两个维度上基准测试逆合成规划系统，包括专用深度学习方法和大语言模型，发现LLMs在高层次策略规划上有优势，但在可靠性上不及专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆合成规划是药物发现中的关键挑战，但现有系统缺乏灵活、化学可解释的基准测试协议，难以实现客观比较。

Method: 提出URSA评估框架，不仅从形式收敛性（商业化起始原料）评估合成路线，还从化学合理性角度模拟化学家评估反应和路线的方式。对端到端逆合成方案和LLMs在新型多样化目标分子上进行综合评估。

Result: LLMs能够支持高层次策略规划，但在可靠解决合成规划任务方面不如专用逆合成模型。

Conclusion: 专用逆合成模型在解决合成规划任务上目前优于LLMs，LLMs更适合作为辅助策略规划工具。

Abstract: Synthesis planning aiming to find pathways of reactions for a target molecule is one of the most important and challenging tasks in drug discovery. Recent progress has produced both specialized deep-learning retrosynthesis systems and general-purpose large language models, but objective comparison remains difficult due to the lack of flexible, chemically interpretable benchmarking protocols. In the current study, we are introducing the URSA (Utilitarian RetroSynthesis Assessment) evaluation framework that provides the opportunity to benchmark the synthetic routes not only from a formal perspective, such as convergence to commercially available starting materials, but also from a chemical plausibility perspective, mimicking the way expert chemists evaluate the reactions and routes. The study covers a comprehensive evaluation of both conventional end-to-end retrosynthesis solutions and LLMs for the synthesis planning task on a set of novel, diverse target molecules with undisclosed synthetic routes, which represent realistic tasks in the daily drug design routine. We find that while LLMs can support high-level strategic planning, they currently underperform specialized retrosynthesis models in reliably solving synthesis planning tasks.

</details>


### [166] [Minimum Block Width for Universal Approximation by Residual Neural Networks with Inner Width One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04597)
*Qi Zhou,Xuan Zhou,Xiao-Song Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究残差神经网络的通用逼近性质，给出了在LeakyReLU、ReLU等激活函数下块宽度的上下界，证明了精确最小块宽度为max{dx, dy}（Lp逼近），并进一步证明了在特定约束下可实现一致逼近，同时指出块宽度小于max{dx, dy}时无法逼近所有目标函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究残差神经网络在通用逼近性质中块宽度的精确下界，填补现有理论空白。

Method: 针对LeakyReLU、ReLU等激活函数，从Lp逼近和一致逼近两个角度，推导块宽度的上下界，并通过构造反例证明下界的紧致性。

Result: Lp逼近下最小块宽度精确为max{dx, dy}；一致逼近下块宽度可达min{dx+dy, max{2dx+1, dy}}；块宽度小于max{dx, dy}时无法实现任意逼近。

Conclusion: 给出了残差神经网络通用逼近所需块宽度的精确刻画，为网络结构设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the universal approximation property of residual neural networks, and obtain some new results. For input and output dimensions $d_x$ and $d_y$, and LeakyReLU, ReLU, ReLU-like activation functions, the upper and lower bounds of the block width are established. To achieve $L^p$ approximation $(1\leq p <+\infty)$ on any compact domain, we show that the exact minimum block width is $\max\{d_x,d_y\}$ when the inner width is 1. Furthermore, we show that residual neural networks with block width $\min\{d_x+d_y, \max\{2d_x+1,d_y\}\}$ can achieve uniform approximation on any compact domain under the constraint that each residual branch has inner width 1. Besides, for any activation function family, we prove that residual neural networks with block width less than $\max\{d_x, d_y\}$ cannot approximate all target functions, both in the $L^p$ sense and the uniform sense, regardless of inner width.

</details>


### [167] [RSPO: Reward-Swap Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04713)
*Qiang Liu,Taian Guo,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reward-Swap Policy Optimization (RSPO)方法，通过奖励交换机制结合密集过程奖励与稀疏结果奖励的优势，在长期多轮交互任务中提升强化学习训练大语言模型的性能，在WebShop和ALFWorld基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长期多轮交互任务中，稀疏结果奖励导致收敛慢且模型可能无法学习到未采样的成功轨迹；而密集过程奖励虽加速收敛但可能与真实结果奖励存在偏差，误导训练方向。现有方法难以兼顾丰富信号与目标一致性。

Method: 提出RSPO方法，核心是奖励交换（reward-swap）机制，在利用密集过程奖励丰富信息的同时，保证优化目标与真实结果奖励的一致性，提升采样轨迹多样性。

Result: 在WebShop和ALFWorld两个挑战性Agent基准上，将RSPO应用于GRPO、PPO、GiGPO等多种强化学习算法，均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RSPO有效解决了过程奖励与结果奖励之间的不对齐问题，提升了模型在长期多轮交互任务中的性能上限，是一种普适性的强化学习训练增强方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning holds significant potential for training large language models (LLMs) to handle multi-turn interactive tasks. However, in long-horizon, multi-turn tasks characterized by sparse outcome rewards, directly training with outcome rewards often results in slow convergence due to the sparsity of signals and the lack of fine-grained feedback. Furthermore, the model may fail to learn successful trajectories that are not sampled during training, thereby limiting its performance. Conversely, while employing customized dense process rewards provides richer signals and accelerates convergence, these surrogate rewards may exhibit potential misalignment with the ground-truth outcome rewards. This inconsistency can bias the training direction and ultimately degrade the model's final performance. In this work, we propose Reward-Swap Policy Optimization (RSPO), a method designed to leverage the rich information from dense process rewards to facilitate training with outcome rewards. By utilizing a reward-swap mechanism, RSPO ensures the diversity of sampled trajectories while guaranteeing consistency between the optimization objective and the true outcome rewards, thereby elevating the performance ceiling of the model. We conduct extensive experiments on two challenging agent benchmarks, WebShop and ALFWorld. By applying our method to various reinforcement learning algorithms, including GRPO, PPO, and GiGPO, we demonstrate that RSPO achieves consistent performance improvements across different baselines and benchmarks.

</details>


### [168] [Measuring What Matters: A Unified Evaluation Framework for GNN Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04600)
*Francesco Paolo Nerini,Mirko Zaffaroni,Paolo Baracco,Gabriele Ciravegna,Alan Perotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无需真实标注的统一量化基准框架用于图神经网络可解释性评估，通过将拓扑结构和节点特征作为独立组件进行度量，在大规模实验中识别出帕累托最优的解释器，并提炼出可操作的可用性指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图神经网络可解释性方法众多，但缺乏统一的评估标准和实践指南，导致方法选择不明确、输出可信度难以评估，阻碍了实际落地应用。

Method: 构建无需真实标注的统一量化基准框架，将表格数据的可解释性度量形式化适配到图数据，分别评估拓扑结构和节点特征两个独立维度；通过大规模基准测试识别不同任务和度量对下的帕累托前沿解释器。

Result: 识别出在多种度量对和任务上一致处于帕累托前沿的鲁棒非支配解，同时确认没有任何单一解释器在所有情况下具有普遍优越性。

Conclusion: 提炼出可操作的G-XAI可用性指南，帮助机器学习从业者评估和部署可信赖的基于GNN的管线。

Abstract: Graph eXplainable AI (G-XAI) is increasingly important for making Graph Neural Networks interpretable and accountable. While a growing number of explainers are available, choosing the right method and assessing the trustworthiness of its outputs remains unclear. Consistent evaluation practices and actionable guidance are still missing, hindering practical adoption. In this paper, we introduce a unified, quantitative benchmarking framework for G-XAI that requires no ground-truth assumptions. We formalize tabular explainability metrics for graph data, evaluating topological structure and node features as independent components.
