Our large-scale benchmarking study identifies explainers that consistently lie on the Pareto front across metric pairs and tasks, establishing robustly non-dominated solutions - while confirming that no single explainer achieves universal superiority. We distill our findings into actionable G-XAI usability guidelines to support Machine Learning practitioners in evaluating and deploying trustworthy GNN-based pipelines.

</details>


### [169] [Trust Region Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04751)
*Zhengpeng Xie,Li Lyna Zhang,Zeke Xie,Mao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Trust Region Policy Distillation (TOP-D)，通过动态构建近端教师将不稳定的On-Policy Distillation转化为稳定训练范式，在数学推理任务上显著提升训练稳定性、样本效率和最终性能，且无额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-Policy Distillation (OPD)训练范式具有 notoriously unstable 和高方差的问题，导致训练不稳定，亟需一种更稳定的蒸馏训练方法。

Method: 提出Trust Region Policy Distillation (TOP-D)，通过动态构建一个proximal teacher来约束策略更新，将不稳定的OPD转化为稳定的训练范式。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，TOP-D显著提升了训练稳定性、样本效率和最终性能，且引入零额外计算开销。

Conclusion: TOP-D通过理论上的梯度方差控制、全局收敛分析和单调改进界，以及在数学推理任务上的实证验证，证明其是OPD范式的有前景替代方案。

Abstract: Big goals are hard to achieve all at once; breaking them into small steps is wiser. We present Trust Region Policy Distillation (TOP-D), which transforms the notoriously unstable, high-variance On-Policy Distillation (OPD) into a stable training paradigm by dynamically constructing a proximal teacher. Theoretically, we establish a rigorous framework demonstrating that TOP-D inherently controls gradient variance. By providing a formal global convergence analysis alongside a monotonic improvement bound, we mathematically formalize the reliability and stability of the overall training dynamics. Empirically, TOP-D dramatically enhances training stability, sample efficiency, and final performance on mathematical reasoning tasks. More importantly, TOP-D introduces zero additional computational overhead, positioning itself as a promising alternative to the well-established OPD paradigm.

</details>


### [170] [Reliability and Identifiability in Persona-Trained Monte Carlo: Variance Decomposition, Stability Bounds, and the Identifiability of Heterogeneous News Reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04627)
*Salavat Ishbulatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为基于人格训练的蒙特卡洛方法（PTMC）建立了完整的统计理论，包括方差分解与最优分配、耦合稳定性误差预算、异质新闻反应的识别理论，以及分离定理刻画PTMC相对于同质模拟器和简化预测器的优势边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为PTMC模拟器提供严格的统计理论基础，使"可靠"一词具有精确的数学含义，解决异质智能体模拟中的方差来源分解、计算预算优化、模型误设传播等核心统计问题。

Method: 提出ANOVA方差分解（人格抽取方差σ_P²与运行内方差σ_w²）及最优预算分配公式；基于耦合方法推导稳定性界，给出三部分可分别估计的总误差预算；构建了异质新闻反应的非参数识别理论，利用Jensen间隙检测异质性，通过奇矩和Hausdorff确定性局部识别分布Q；提出√n一致估计量和边界修正的同质性检验；给出两个分离定理刻画PTMC的优越性边界。

Result: 推导了方差最优分配公式；建立了包含三部分的可估计误差预算体系（分布误估、策略误差、蒙特卡洛噪声）；证明了在固定响应非线性下，聚合冲击曲线A(z)的严格Jensen间隙可检测异质性，且通过奇矩可局部识别Q，并给出识别失败的条件；分离定理形式化了不可约Jensen偏差下限和Lucas批判作为干预外推的最小最大极限。

Conclusion: PTMC具有完整的统计保证体系，包括无条件的大数定律与中心极限定理、条件最坏情况误差界、以及开放的大K平均场极限问题。该理论框架为异质智能体金融模拟的可靠性提供了严格基石。

Abstract: Persona-Trained Monte Carlo (PTMC) estimates distributions of market-outcome functionals by repeatedly simulating limit-order-book interaction among $K$ neural policy bots whose behavioral personas are drawn from a learned heterogeneity distribution $\mathcal{P}$. This paper develops the statistical theory that makes the word "reliable" precise for such estimators.
  We decompose estimator variance into a persona-draw component $σ_P^2$ and a within-run component $σ_w^2$, give unbiased ANOVA estimators of both, and derive the variance-optimal allocation of a fixed compute budget between outer persona draws and inner replications. A coupling-based stability bound quantifies how misestimation of $\mathcal{P}$ and error in the trained policy propagate into the estimand, yielding a three-term total-error budget whose terms are separately estimable; a uniform-in-horizon version holds under a Doeblin condition on the market chain.
  The main contribution is an identification theory for heterogeneous news reaction: under a fixed response nonlinearity, the aggregate impact curve $A(z)=\mathbb{E}_Q[g(ηz)]$ detects heterogeneous news sensitivity through a strict Jensen gap and identifies the distribution $Q$ locally via odd moments and Hausdorff determinacy, with sharp failure when the response family is unknown. We provide $\sqrt{n}$-consistent estimators and a boundary-corrected test of homogeneous news reaction. Two separation theorems delimit when PTMC is provably preferable to homogeneous-population simulators and reduced-form forecasters, formalizing an irreducible Jensen bias floor and the Lucas critique as a minimax limit on intervention extrapolation. All proofs are given in full; guarantees are classified as unconditional (Monte Carlo convergence), conditional worst-case (the error budget), or open (the large-$K$ mean-field limit).

</details>


### [171] [Multi-Turn On-Policy Distillation with Prefix Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04763)
*Baohao Liao,Hanze Dong,Christof Monz,Xinxing Xu,Li Dong,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ReOPD的高效离线式策略蒸馏方法，通过重用预收集的教师轨迹作为重放前缀，避免了在线策略蒸馏中昂贵的环境交互成本，同时解决了多轮蒸馏中的"前缀陷阱"问题，在数学推理和搜索任务上达到或超过在线OPD的性能，且训练速度提升4倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在智能体任务中成本高昂，因为每次更新都需要学生与环境的全新交互以及教师对每个历史状态的查询，导致训练效率低下。

Method: 提出ReOPD方法，通过重用预收集的教师轨迹作为离线重放前缀，让学生仅在选择步骤上行动，教师提供密集的逐步骤监督而不执行新的环境交互。针对"前缀陷阱"（学生占据分布与教师可靠性之间的双向分布偏移），采用逐步衰减的采样调度，优先使用早期、偏移较小的前缀。

Result: 在数学推理和搜索环境的多尺度教师/学生模型实验中，ReOPD保持或提升了OPD级别的准确性，学生训练期间零工具调用，每训练步骤速度至少比OPD快4倍。

Conclusion: ReOPD将昂贵的智能体-环境交互转化为可重用的离线资源，实现了跨工具、任务和环境的可扩展蒸馏。

Abstract: We study on-policy distillation (OPD) for agentic tasks, where an LLM agent interacts with an environment over multiple turns and a student imitates a teacher over these multi-turn interaction histories. Fully online OPD is costly because each update requires fresh student rollouts through the environment and teacher queries at visited histories. We propose Replayed-Prefix On-Policy Distillation (ReOPD), an off-environment alternative that reuses pre-collected teacher trajectories as replayed prefixes: the student acts at selected steps, while the teacher provides dense per-step supervision without executing new environment interactions. We show that multi-turn OPD introduces a prefix trap: making histories more student-on-policy improves relevance to the student, but can query the teacher on histories where its target is unreliable. This creates a two-sided distribution shift between student occupancy and teacher reliability. ReOPD addresses this by treating multi-turn OPD as a reliability-aware prefix distribution design and implements it with a simple step-decaying sampling schedule that emphasizes early, lower-shift prefixes. Across mathematical reasoning with Python and search environments over multiple teacher and student model scales, ReOPD preserves or improves OPD-level accuracy, uses zero tool calls during student training, and is at least 4$\times$ faster per training step than OPD. ReOPD therefore turns expensive agent-environment interaction into a reusable offline resource, enabling scalable distillation across tools, tasks, and environments.

</details>


### [172] [Predicting Drafted Deck Strength for "Magic: the Gathering"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04782)
*Tomas Rigaux,Hisashi Kashima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究《万智牌》轮抽（Draft）赛制中的决策问题，提出一种基于编码器的模型，通过集合上下文化的卡牌嵌入编码轮抽决策序列，在真实大规模数据上优于线性基线，建立了首个MTG轮抽结果预测的学习基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实游戏缺乏固定的紧凑规则集，其动态由大量且不断演变的游戏组件间的交互定义，导致通用策略学习不切实际。MTG轮抽赛制隔离了卡牌选择过程与游戏对战，为研究基于组合协同效应的决策提供了可行且非平凡的环境。

Method: 提出一种基于编码器的模型，生成集合上下文化的卡牌嵌入（set-contextualized card embeddings），对轮抽决策序列进行编码。模型在真实大规模数据上与线性基线进行对比评估。

Result: 该模型在真实大规模数据上一致优于线性基线，建立了MTG轮抽结果预测的首个学习基准。

Conclusion: 所提出的编码器模型能有效捕捉卡牌间的组合协同效应，为轮抽这一约束性牌组构建环境中的决策建模提供了可行方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Many real-world games do not admit a fixed, compact rule set: instead, their dynamics are defined by interactions among a large and often evolving collection of game pieces, making general-purpose policy learning impractical. Magic: the Gathering (MTG) exemplifies this setting, where the cards themselves define and alter gameplay rules, strategic constraints, and long-term outcomes, while the pool of available cards is ever-changing. We study Draft, a constrained deck-building format of MTG in which eight players make 39-45 sequential selections from semi-random packs to construct a 40-card deck under partial information. By isolating the card selection process from gameplay, Draft provides a tractable yet non-trivial setting for studying decision-making driven by combinatorial card synergies. We propose an encoder-based model that produces set-contextualized card embeddings to encode the draft decision sequence, with a consistent improvement over linear baselines on large-scale real-world data, establishing a first learned benchmark for outcome prediction in MTG Draft. Our code is available at github.com/akulen/MtGDraftEncoder.

</details>


### [173] [A Physics-Regulated Neural Framework for Learning 3D Grain Growth Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04680)
*Zhihui Tian,Kang Yang,Michael Tonks,Amanda R. Krause,Joel B. Harley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出3D-PRIMME方法，仅用两个连续时间步数据训练，即可准确预测三维晶粒生长动力学，保持线性粗化定律和拓扑统计量，且无需重训练即可外推至更大系统（从100³/512晶粒扩展到1024³/550000晶粒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种数据驱动的替代模型，在保持物理不变量（如晶粒生长统计标度律）的同时，实现三维微观结构演化的可计算扩展性。

Method: 提出3D-PRIMME（物理正则化可解释机器学习），仅用两个连续时间步训练，学习尺度无关且时间稳定的局部演化规则。

Result: 模型准确复现线性粗化定律并保持拓扑统计量；从100³网格（512晶粒）训练后，可直接应用于1024³网格（550000晶粒），动力学和拓扑特征一致。

Conclusion: 3D-PRIMME学到的局部演化规则具有尺度无关性和时间稳定性，能够高效、鲁棒地实现大规模三维微观结构演化的代理预测。

Abstract: Grain growth is governed by the reduction in grain boundary energy and exhibits well-established statistical scaling laws. Developing data-driven surrogates that preserve these physical invariants while remaining computationally scalable remains challenging, especially in 3D. We present 3D-PRIMME (Physics-Regulated Interpretable Machine Learning for Microstructure Evolution) for learning three-dimensional grain growth dynamics. The model is trained using only two consecutive time steps yet accurately reproduces the linear coarsening law and preserves topological statistics over extended time scales. Despite being trained on a $100^3$ grid points with 512 grains, the learned evolution operator is applied to domains up to $1024^3$ grid points with 550000 grains without retraining, maintaining consistent kinetics and grain topology across orders-of-magnitude increases in system size. These results demonstrate that 3D-PRIMME learns a scale-independent and temporally stable local evolution rule, enabling efficient and robust large-scale surrogate prediction of 3D microstructure evolution.

</details>


### [174] [Pretraining Curricula Enable Selective Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04846)
*Sebastian A. Bruijns,Jirko Rubruck,Mia H. Whitefield,Kai J. Sandbrink,Fazl Barez,Christopher Summerfield*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，不平衡的预训练课程（先学习一个任务再学习另一个）相比平衡课程（均匀采样），能促进上下文学习、提升任务表征解耦程度（分离神经回路），从而增强微调的选择性，对AI安全中的精确行为抑制有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索显式预训练课程如何影响学习、泛化以及微调的选择性，尤其是对于AI安全中通过微调选择性抑制不当行为的需求。

Method: 对比平衡课程（均匀采样）与不平衡课程（一个任务先学，另一个后学）在两类任务上（冲突复制任务、合成语言学习任务）的效果，使用消融实验和激活修补分析神经表征的解耦程度。

Result: 不平衡课程促进上下文学习，提升拒绝微调的选择性；任务被解耦到可分离的神经回路中，而平衡课程将两个任务路由到共同通路。在语言任务中，不平衡课程同样导致更局部化、更少纠缠的规则表征，带来更稳健的规则遵循行为。

Conclusion: 不平衡预训练课程可作为促进解耦表征的重要工具，直接影响安全微调的精确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Transformers follow implicit curricula whereby some tasks are learned before others. However, how explicit pretraining curricula influence learning, generalization, and the selectivity of fine-tuning is unclear. This is important for AI safety, where fine-tuning is used to selectively suppress misaligned behaviors. Here, we compare curricula that pretrain tasks in a balanced (sampled uniformly) or an imbalanced (one task early, the other late) fashion. We show that imbalanced learning of two conflicting copy tasks promotes in-context learning and improves the selectivity of refusal fine-tuning. Ablations and activation patching show that this occurs because imbalanced pretraining encourages tasks to be disentangled in separable neural circuits, whereas balanced training routes both tasks through a common pathway. We extend these findings to a synthetic language learning task involving rule-consistent and rule-violating data, where imbalanced curricula similarly lead to more localized, less entangled rule representations, resulting in more robust rule-following behavior. Together, these results suggest that imbalanced pretraining curricula may be an important tool for promoting disentangled representations, with direct consequences for the precision and reliability of safety fine-tuning.

</details>


### [175] [Input Pathways Shape Few-Shot, Not Zero-Shot, Binding in Tiny Transformers: A Fully-Enumerable Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04926)
*Yoshiyuki Ootani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在穷举枚举的有限因子化世界上训练~6-10K参数的小型Transformer，系统研究了信息传递路径（符号token、干净oracle编码、纠缠感知向量）如何影响组合性绑定。发现：(1) 端点不变性：所有路径在零样本组合任务上均无法达到收敛（止步于或低于随机水平）；(2) 双因素解释小样本绑定：输入路径参数共享和代码可读性共同决定样本效率；(3) 双重分离：分布式编码在训练早期经历短暂的超随机阶段，而样本效率由路径共享驱动；(4) 失败解剖学：符号路径在读出层丢失答案，索引路径错误绑定，纠缠路径继承输入的不可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究信息传递路径（符号/token、oracle编码、感知向量）如何影响Transformer对信息的组合性绑定能力，揭示不同表征格式下的归纳偏差。

Method: 在穷举枚举的有限因子化世界上训练小型Transformer（~6-10K参数），信息匹配不同输入路径（符号、oracle、纠缠），零采样方差覆盖整个输入空间，进行零样本和小样本组合性绑定测试。

Result: (1) 所有路径零样本组合失败（端点不变性）；(2) 小样本绑定由参数共享和代码可读性双因素解释；(3) 分布式编码早期训练出现超随机短暂阶段，小样本效率跟踪路径共享；(4) 不同路径的失败模式不同。

Conclusion: 信息传递路径的表征格式显著影响Transformer的组合性绑定能力，核心贡献是双因素解释框架，端点不变性和失败解剖学提供了诊断性约束。

Abstract: How does the way information reaches a transformer -- as symbolic tokens, a clean per-factor "oracle" code, or an entangled perceptual vector -- shape whether it binds that information compositionally? We study ~6-10K-parameter transformers on finite factored worlds enumerated exhaustively, so every measurement covers the whole input space (zero sampling variance) and the informative routes are information-matched (exact Bayes ceiling 1.0). We report four findings. (1) Endpoint invariance: on held-out binding queries no informative route reaches converged zero-shot composition -- each ends at or below chance despite a ceiling of 1.0, so within a bounded sweep the failure reflects inductive bias under a lookup-sufficient objective, not missing information. (2) A two-factor account of few-shot binding: sample efficiency is best explained by input-pathway parameter sharing and code readability; a dimension-matched control and a graded readability sweep isolate readability from input dimension, and the clean oracle is not the most sample-efficient readable route. (3) A double dissociation: early in training, distributed -- but not index-like -- codes pass through a transient above-chance phase (tracking code format), while few-shot efficiency tracks pathway sharing. (4) Failure anatomy: symbolic routes lose the answer at the readout; index routes mis-bind (the answer stays decodable, yet an input intervention shows the output tracks the wrong slot); entangled routes inherit their input's readability. The central claim is the two-factor account; the endpoint and anatomy results are diagnostic constraints. All code, manifests, and per-seed logs are released for exact reproduction.

</details>


### [176] [F-ACVAE: A Federated Adaptive Conditional Variational Auto-Encoder for Privacy-Preserving Intrusion Detection in IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04698)
*Mohammad Ansarimehr,Somayeh Changiz,Ehsan Baghishani,Ali Mousavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出F-ACVAE，一种联邦自适应条件变分自编码器框架，用于解决IoT入侵检测中的高维数据、类别不平衡和非独立同分布数据问题。通过选择性参数聚合和约束动量高斯聚合策略，在N-BaIoT数据集上达到99%的平均准确率和Macro F1分数，同时通信开销降低约62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备快速激增导致攻击面扩大，亟需稳健且保护隐私的入侵检测系统。集中式学习方法因高维流量数据、极端类别不平衡以及异构边缘设备间高度非独立同分布数据而性能严重下降。

Method: 提出F-ACVAE联邦自适应条件变分自编码器框架，包含两个核心机制：(1)选择性参数聚合——本地编码器保持私有，全局共享组件同步以保留判别性潜在结构；(2)约束动量高斯聚合(CMGA)——结合更新钳制与动量平滑，缓解极端非独立同分布下的客户端漂移问题。

Result: 在N-BaIoT数据集上，平均准确率和Macro F1分数均达到99%，优于现有基线方法；选择性聚合机制使通信开销降低约62%。

Conclusion: F-ACVAE在保证隐私保护和通信效率的前提下实现了高检测性能，特别适用于资源受限的IoT环境。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Internet of things (IoT) devices has significantly expanded the cyber-attack surface, necessitating robust and privacy-preserving intrusion detection systems (IDS). However, centralized learning approaches often suffer from severe performance degradation due to high-dimensional traffic data, extreme class imbalance, and highly non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data across heterogeneous edge devices. To address these challenges, this paper proposes F-ACVAE, a federated adaptive conditional variational autoencoder framework that enables collaborative model training across distributed IoT devices without sharing raw data. F-ACVAE incorporates selective parameter aggregation, where local encoders remain private while globally shared components are synchronized to preserve discriminative latent structures. To further enhance stability under extreme non-IID settings and feature distribution shifts, we introduce a novel constrained momentum Gaussian aggregation (CMGA) strategy that combines update clamping with momentum-based smoothing to mitigate client drift. Extensive experiments on the N-BaIoT dataset demonstrate that F-ACVAE achieves an average accuracy and macro F1-score of 99\%, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Moreover, the selective aggregation mechanism reduces communication overhead by approximately 62\%, making the framework particularly suitable for resource-constrained IoT environments. These results highlight the effectiveness of F-ACVAE in achieving high detection performance while ensuring privacy preservation and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [177] [The Map Behind the Flow: Finite-Step Gradient Descent as a Dynamical System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04993)
*Thomas Hofmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了固定步长梯度下降作为离散动力系统的行为，在可精确求解的层次化模型中揭示了深度学习中边缘稳定性、震荡、平衡等现象本质上是训练映射的分岔行为，并证明学习率不仅是数值稳定性参数，更是决定训练动力学吸引子和表征选择的结构性参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的许多现象是动力学的，关注梯度下降如何达到、避免或选择不同的极小值，而小步长梯度流极限无法捕捉边缘稳定性、尖锐度震荡、弹射阶段、平衡化等训练映射本身的效果。

Method: 将固定步长梯度下降视为离散动力系统，在保留深度学习基本结构（深度、因子分解、宽度、数据耦合、激活、随机性）的可精确求解层次化模型中研究。从深度线性链的标量约化出发，得到四次损失和三次梯度映射，在自然的大深度缩放下收敛到通用的Ricker型映射。

Result: 边缘稳定性是训练映射的第一次分岔而非优化崩溃；有限步长打破梯度流的守恒律并收缩因子分解不平衡；残差震荡使参数向更平坦、更平衡的表征移动；更宽的线性网络产生谱边阶梯，最优学习率可位于第一个边之后；数据耦合、非线性激活和随机目标都遵循相同的组织原则。

Conclusion: 学习率不仅是数值稳定性参数，更是训练动力学的结构参数，决定其吸引子并塑造梯度下降选择的表征。

Abstract: Many phenomena of deep learning are dynamical: they concern not only which minima exist, but how gradient descent reaches, avoids, or selects among them. Edge-of-stability behavior, sharpness oscillations, catapult phases, balancing, and movement toward flatter representations are effects of the training map itself, and are poorly captured by the small-step gradient-flow limit.
  This paper studies fixed-step gradient descent as a discrete dynamical system in a hierarchy of exactly solvable models retaining basic structures of deep learning: depth, factorization, width, data coupling, activation, and stochasticity. The starting point is the balanced scalar reduction of a deep linear chain, giving a quartic loss and a cubic gradient map whose post-edge behavior is explicit. Under the natural large-depth scaling, this dynamics converges to a universal Ricker-type map. The edge of stability is therefore not a breakdown of optimization, but the first bifurcation of the training map.
  Embedding the scalar dynamics back into factored models turns these regimes into learning phenomena. Finite steps break conservation laws of gradient flow and contract factorization imbalance; residual oscillations move parameters toward flatter, more balanced representations. Wider linear networks produce a ladder of spectral edges, so the optimal learning rate can lie beyond the first edge. Data coupling, nonlinear activations, and stochastic targets preserve the same organizing principle: finite-step oscillations drive alignment, balancing, and representation selection. Thus the learning rate is not merely a numerical stability parameter. It is a structural parameter of the training dynamics, determining its attractors and shaping the representations gradient descent selects.

</details>


### [178] [Hierarchical Scaffolding Enables Human-Like Cognitive Selectivity under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04709)
*Juhyoung Park,Jaehyuk Bae,Hyeonbo Yang,Se-Bum Paik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCALA是一个受认知心理学启发的分层学习框架，通过从粗粒度概念结构到细粒度识别的引导，在数据稀缺条件下实现了类似人类的样本效率和鲁棒类别泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习系统需要大量数据，而人类即使在数据有限的情况下也能高效学习（通常先获取宽泛类别结构再细化区分）。受此对比启发，作者希望缩小机器与人类在样本效率上的差距。

Method: 提出SCALA（Scaffolded Cognitive Architecture for Learning under limited dAta），一种受认知心理学启发的分层学习框架，从粗粒度概念结构引导模型逐步过渡到细粒度识别。该框架具有类似人类的认知选择性，能有效优先处理任务相关特征并抑制背景干扰。

Result: 在严重数据稀缺条件下实现了显著的准确率提升；促进对未见类别的鲁棒泛化；加速新类别的获取；特征表示发生了根本性转变：加速了聚类形成、减少了类内离散度、增强了语义可分离性。

Conclusion: SCALA是一种在数据约束环境下实现人类级样本效率和鲁棒类别泛化的强大框架。

Abstract: Modern machine learning systems demand extensive datasets for visual recognition. Conversely, humans learn with high efficiency despite severe data limitations, often by acquiring broad categorical structures before refining finer distinctions. Inspired by this contrast, we introduce SCALA (Scaffolded Cognitive Architecture for Learning under limited dAta), a hierarchical learning framework grounded in cognitive psychology that guides models from coarse conceptual structures to fine-grained recognition. Our model exhibits human-like cognitive selectivity by effectively prioritizing task-relevant features while suppressing background distractors, a mechanism that induces a fundamental shift in representation learning. This shift is characterized by accelerated cluster formation, reduced intra-class dispersion, and enhanced semantic separability. Empirically, SCALA achieves significant accuracy improvements under severe data scarcity. Furthermore, this hierarchical scaffolding promotes robust generalization to unseen classes and accelerates the acquisition of novel categories. Collectively, our results establish SCALA as a powerful framework for achieving human-level sample efficiency and resilient category generalization in data-constrained environments.

</details>


### [179] [ImputeECG: Deep Learning Reconstruction of Complete 12-Lead Electrocardiograms from Incomplete Recordings for Cardiac Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05009)
*Xiaocheng Fang,Haoyu Wang,Jieyi Cai,Qinghao Zhao,Jun Li,Shanwei Zhang,Guangkun Nie,Yujie Xiao,Shun Huang,Jiarui Jin,Hongmin Liu,Guodong Wang,Shuohua Chen,Liming Lin,Shouling Wu,Hongyan Li,Shenda Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ImputeECG，一种基于掩码条件的一维Transformer自编码器，用于补全不完整的12导联10秒心电图信号。在PTB-XL、CPSC2018和真实世界Kailuan临床队列（43,633例）中，该方法显著优于基线，在缺失区域MAE降低41.7-51.9%，下游诊断性能接近完整ECG水平，零样本性别预测AUROC从82.6%提升至85.8%，年龄预测MAE从10.72降至9.87年。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多临床心电图记录（尤其是从图像数字化得到的记录）因显示格式短、波形数字化不完整、导联缺失或信号损坏而不完整，阻碍了AI心血管评估的应用。

Method: 提出ImputeECG，一种掩码条件的一维Transformer自编码器，在保留所有观测样本的同时补全12导联10秒ECG；训练于PTB-XL，在模拟不完整设置下评估，并在真实Kailuan临床队列中进行图像数字化后的验证。

Result: PTB-XL上缺失区域MAE降低41.7-51.0%，MSE降低54.0-63.7%；CPSC2018上MAE降低49.7-51.9%；下游分类任务中恢复至接近完整ECG性能；Kailuan队列中性别预测AUROC提升至85.8%，年龄预测MAE降至9.87年。

Conclusion: ECG补全是将不完整心电图记录转化为AI可用的12导联10秒数字信号的有效策略，可扩展心电图档案在心脏数字评估中的可用范围。

Abstract: Complete digital 12-lead electrocardiograms (ECGs) are essential for AI-enabled cardiovascular assessment, yet many clinical ECG records, particularly those digitized from ECG images, remain incomplete because of short display formats, incomplete waveform digitization, lead loss, or signal corruption. We developed ImputeECG, a mask-conditioned one-dimensional Transformer autoencoder that completes 12-lead, 10-s ECGs while retaining all observed samples. The model was trained on PTB-XL and evaluated on PTB-XL and CPSC2018 under simulated incomplete settings, with additional real-world validation in a 43,633-record Kailuan clinical cohort after ECG image digitization. Metrics were computed over originally missing regions, with analyses of morphology and downstream diagnostic utility. On PTB-XL, ImputeECG reduced missing-region MAE by 41.7-51.0% and MSE by 54.0-63.7% versus the strongest baseline, with lower errors in R-peak timing, RR interval, QRS duration, QT interval, and P-wave, QRS-complex, and T-wave reconstruction. On CPSC2018, ImputeECG reduced MAE by 49.7-51.9%, supporting external generalization. In downstream multi-label classification, ImputeECG restored performance to 92.28% AUROC and 33.88% AUPRC in the most incomplete PTB-XL setting, approaching complete-ECG performance. On CPSC2018, completed ECGs achieved 94.75-95.89% AUROC and 78.83-81.86% AUPRC across settings. In Kailuan, ECG completion improved zero-shot sex prediction AUROC from 82.6% to 85.8% and reduced age prediction MAE from 10.72 to 9.87 years after image-based ECG digitization. These findings support ECG completion as a practical strategy for converting incomplete ECG records into AI-ready 12-lead, 10-s digital signals and extending the usable scope of ECG archives for digital cardiac assessment.

</details>


### [180] [Hyperparameter Transfer in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05017)
*Gage DeZoort,Boris Hanin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图神经网络（GNN）的超参数迁移问题，提出了适用于SGD、Adam和AdamW优化器的迁移参数化方案，通过理论缩放分析和实验验证，该方法能实现稳定的特征更新、学习率迁移，并随着宽度和深度增加提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数设置对深度学习模型性能至关重要，超参数迁移旨在使最优超参数设置在不同模型规模间保持一致，从而通过调优小模型来代理优化大模型。这一原理在密集神经网络中已被充分研究，但在图神经网络中尚缺乏探索。

Method: 通过理论缩放分析和受控实验，提出适用于GNN的迁移参数化方案。针对SGD，提出图依赖的第一层修正因子；针对Adam，探索不同消息传递归一化对早期和后期训练迁移行为的影响；针对AdamW，提出联合迁移权重衰减和学习率的参数化方法。

Result: 提出的参数化方案实现了稳定的特征更新、学习率迁移，并且随着GNN的宽度和深度增加，模型性能得到提升。SGD中图依赖的第一层修正因子可加速稀疏词袋输入图的早期训练。消息传递归一化的选择对训练行为有重要影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为在不同学习任务和训练场景下缩放GNN提供了实用方案，填补了GNN超参数迁移研究的空白。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models crucially depends on the settings of hyperparameters like learning rate, initialization scale, and weight decay. Hyperparameter transfer aims to make near-optimal hyperparameter settings consistent across model scale, so that large models can be optimized by proxy tuning their smaller, cheaper-to-optimize counterparts. While transfer principles are well-studied in the context of dense neural networks in language and vision tasks, they remain comparatively under-explored for graph neural networks (GNNs). We develop and validate a transfer parameterization for GNNs trained with SGD, Adam, and AdamW. Through theoretical scaling analyses and controlled experiments, we show that the proposed parameterization yields stable feature updates, learning rate transfer, and improved performance as width and depth increase. For SGD, we identify graph-dependent first-layer correction factors and show that their use can accelerate early training in graphs with sparse bag-of-words inputs. For Adam, we explore how different message passing normalizations affect early- and late-training transfer behavior, illustrating the importance of message passing normalization and advocating for an associated hyperparameter. For AdamW, we adapt a parameterization that allows for the joint transfer of weight decay and learning rate. Together, these results provide a practical recipe for scaling GNNs across a variety of learning tasks and training scenarios.

</details>


### [181] [Grokking Is Conditional and Fragile: A Fully-Tractable, Multi-Seed Study at 12K Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05104)
*Yoshiyuki Ootani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在完全可解析的11,856参数Llama风格Transformer（Glimmer-1-Base）上研究"grokking"（泛化延迟现象），发现其为有条件的脆弱相变，受训练集覆盖率门控，且对浮点数值环境高度敏感。研究表明，该现象的实证单位必须是多种子率（multi-seed rate），并需固定数值环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的grokking研究通常在大模型上进行、报告单次运行结果，缺乏可解析性和统计可靠性。本文旨在一个完全可枚举的小型模型上，以多种子率替代单次结果，系统测量grokking的条件性与脆弱性，揭示其真实性质。

Method: 采用公开的~11,856参数Llama风格Transformer（Glimmer-1-Base），在模算术任务上枚举所有权重、注意力和完整的输入-输出映射。以多种子率（multi-seed rate）测量grokking，而非单次结果。通过控制训练集覆盖率、权重衰减和浮点数值环境（CPU线程数、CPU vs GPU执行）来系统分析该现象。

Result: (1) Grokking是有条件的脆弱相变，其门控条件是训练集覆盖率，且阈值主要跟踪输出基数（模数）而非任务结构。(2) 权重衰减在12K参数尺度上复现了Omnigrok的倒U形曲线，作为速率测量的阳性对照。(3) Grokking对数值环境极为敏感：CPU线程数（归约顺序）和CPU vs GPU执行均能翻转相同种子的少数结果，而不改变聚合速率。(4) 子任务专家分解主要通过降低覆盖成本而非增加监督来帮助模型。

Conclusion: Grokking的实证单位必须是固定数值环境下的多种子率，并尽可能与模型的直接解读对照验证。多种子控制可推翻单次运行中的种子混杂导致的戏剧性叙事。

Abstract: Grokking -- the delayed onset of generalization long after a network has fit its training set - -is usually studied in models too large to read completely and reported from single training runs. We instead study a publicly released ~11,856-parameter Llama-style transformer (Glimmer-1-Base) on modular arithmetic, small enough to enumerate its weights, attention, and full input-output map, and we measure grokking as a multi-seed rate rather than a single outcome. In this fully-tractable regime grokking is a conditional, fragile phase transition. It is gated by training-set coverage, whose threshold tracks output cardinality (the modulus) more than task structure, an ordering that holds above the transition and across a ten-fold change in domain size. Weight decay reproduces the Omnigrok inverted-U at 12K parameters, a positive control on the rate measurement. Grokking also sits on a numerical knife-edge: two perturbations of the floating-point environment -- CPU thread count (reduction order) and CPU-versus-GPU execution -- each flip a minority of same-seed outcomes without a detectable shift in the aggregate rate. Decomposition into sub-task specialists helps chiefly by making coverage cheap rather than by adding supervision. Methodologically, multi-seed control under a fixed numerical environment overturns three dramatic single-run narratives in our own data, each a seed confound. The unit of evidence for grokking must therefore be a multi-seed rate under a pinned numerical environment, checked where possible against a direct reading of the model.

</details>


### [182] [Localized LoRA-MoE: Block-wise Low-Rank Experts With Adaptive Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05114)
*Babak Barazandeh,Subhabrata Majumdar,Vinay Prithyani,George Michailidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Localized LoRA-MoE框架，通过局部化空间阻塞与动态上下文路由解决LoRA的梯度竞争和静态拓扑问题。设计了两种架构：集中式宏路由（Block-Wise）和去中心化微路由（Cell-Wise），实验表明后者实现了统计意义上的全局最优，且具备抗故障传播的"梯度防火墙"能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LoRA方法受限于单一瓶颈结构，在多任务交织场景下易发生梯度竞争，导致适配器权重退化为非专业化平均值；现有空间分区方法虽引入块级隔离，但拓扑结构静态，无法适应动态任务切换或传感器故障。

Method: 提出Localized LoRA-MoE统一框架：1) Block-Wise LoRA-MoE（集中式宏路由）：通过单一上下文信号调制整个结构网格；2) Cell-Wise LoRA-MoE（去中心化微路由）：赋予矩阵网格中每个坐标单元自主的局部专家门控能力。

Result: 在高维SVD矩阵模拟、真实世界表格变换及传感器退化下的空间视觉感知等基准测试中，两种架构均解决了静态基线的优化死锁问题。去中心化单元级门控实现了与全知全局协调器完全一致的统计性能，形成保护存活路径免受故障传播损害的"梯度防火墙"。

Conclusion: Localized LoRA-MoE框架持续优于静态基线，为跨细粒度坐标场和动态运行场景的模型自适应提供了可扩展、参数高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and high-dimensional perception networks increasingly rely on parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) to adapt to diverse operational contexts. However, standard methods like LoRA are structurally limited by a monolithic bottleneck, making them highly susceptible to gradient warfare. Interleaved multi-task streams may trigger destructive optimization feedback, collapsing adapter weights into unspecialized averages. While recent spatial partitioning methods have introduced block-wise isolation, they remain trapped in static topologies, unable to adapt to dynamic task-switching or environmental sensor failure. In this work, we introduce Localized LoRA-MoE, a unified framework that fuses localized spatial blocking with dynamic, context-conditioned routing. We propose and evaluate two novel architectural paradigms: Block-Wise LoRA-MoE (Centralized Macro-Routing), which modulates the entire structural grid via a monolithic context signal, and Cell-Wise LoRA-MoE (Decentralized Micro-Routing), which empowers every coordinate cell in the matrix grid with autonomous, localized expert gating. Through a comprehensive suite of benchmarks, ranging from high-dimensional SVD matrix simulations and real-world tabular transformations to spatial vision perception under sensor degradation, we demonstrate that both architectures resolve optimization deadlocks inherent in static baselines. Our empirical results establish that decentralized cell-level gating achieves complete statistical parity with an omniscient global coordinator, providing a robust "gradient firewall" that protects surviving pathways from fault-propagated corruption. Our proposals consistently outperform static baselines, offering a scalable and parameter-efficient solution for dynamic model adaptation across granular coordinate fields and shifting operational regimes.

</details>


### [183] [MARLIN: De Novo Molecular Structure Elucidation from Tandem Mass Spectra without a Ground-Truth Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04774)
*Xujun Che,Xiuxia Du,Depeng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MARLIN提出了一种无需分子式输入的从头结构解析方法，通过自监督编码器预测分子指纹，结合块扩散语言模型生成候选结构，并利用质量壳约束确保与测量质量一致，在NPLIB1基准上实现了无需真实分子式的最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非靶向串联质谱能检测数千种小分子，但绝大多数因不在谱库中而无法鉴定。这些未表征的代谢物和天然产物恰是药物发现、生物标志物研究和暴露组学的关键。计算从头结构解析可弥补这一空白，但现有方法几乎都假设已知分子式，而这对真正的新化合物而言并不现实。

Method: MARLIN包含三个核心模块：(1) 自监督编码器从原始质谱峰预测分子指纹；(2) 块扩散语言模型以分子指纹和仪器测量的前体质量为条件生成候选结构；(3) 质量壳约束（mass-shell constraint）确保每个候选结构与测量质量一致，无需固定原子组成。此外采用对称噪声目标吸收编码器误差，以及候选多样性机制防止结果坍缩为单一结构。

Result: 在NPLIB1基准上，MARLIN在精确匹配准确率、结构距离和指纹相似度三个指标上，均是无真实分子式输入方法中的最优者。同时，它作为副产品恢复的正确分子式频率与专用分子式预测器相当。

Conclusion: MARLIN实现了在分子式不可用的现实发现场景中可靠的从头结构解析，为质谱未知化合物的鉴定提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Untargeted tandem mass spectrometry (MS/MS) detects thousands of small molecules per biological sample, yet most go unidentified because they are absent from spectral libraries. These uncharacterized metabolites and natural products are precisely the compounds that matter for drug discovery, biomarker research, and exposomics. Computational de novo structure elucidation could close this gap, but almost all state-of-the-art methods assume the ground-truth molecular formula is known, an oracle that does not exist for genuinely novel compounds and is itself predicted with substantial error. We present MARLIN, a de novo method that elucidates structures directly from a spectrum with no molecular formula at any stage. A self-supervised encoder predicts a molecular fingerprint from the raw peaks, and a block-diffusion language model generates candidate structures conditioned only on the fingerprint and the instrument-measured precursor mass. A provably safe mass-shell constraint keeps every candidate consistent with the measured mass without fixing the atom inventory, and candidates are accepted by exact parts-per-million mass agreement. A symmetric noise objective absorbs encoder error, and a candidate-diversity mechanism keeps the candidates from collapsing to a single structure. On the NPLIB1 benchmark, MARLIN is the strongest method evaluated without a ground-truth formula across exact-match accuracy, structural distance, and fingerprint similarity, and it recovers the correct molecular formula as a byproduct about as often as a dedicated predictor without ever using one. MARLIN enables reliable de novo structure elucidation in the realistic discovery regime where the molecular formula is unavailable.

</details>


### [184] [PDEFlow: Autonomous Agentic PDE Pipelines for Neural Operator Learning and Solver-Free Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05134)
*Akshat Jani,Prathamesh Gadekar,Sakhinana Sagar Srinivas,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PDEFlow是一个自主智能体框架，可将用户层面的ODE/PDE描述转化为基于求解器的神经算子流水线，支持从问题规范、数据生成到模型训练和推理的全流程自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学计算和工程应用中，许多物理配置需要反复进行问题定义、数值模拟、学习建模和查询预测，现有流程需要大量人工干预，缺乏一个端到端的自动化框架来衔接这些环节。

Method: PDEFlow包含四个模块：1) 状态化输入图，将多轮自然语言输入和用户编辑转化为验证过的问题规范；2) 数据生成模块，通过FEniCSx有限元后端采样参数并求解方程，生成算子就绪张量；3) 基于注册表的训练和推理接口，支持不同神经算子无缝切换；4) 当前实例化为多分支贝叶斯DeepONet。

Result: 在基准ODE和PDE任务上的实验表明，PDEFlow能够构建有效的规范、生成基于求解器的数据集、在稳态和瞬态问题类别上训练神经算子，并从保存的检查点提供无求解器的预测。

Conclusion: PDEFlow设计用于可重复的科学与工程工作流，能够以最小人工干预完成多个相关物理配置的规范定义、仿真、学习和查询。

Abstract: We present PDEFlow, an autonomous agentic framework that turns user-level ODE and PDE descriptions into solver-backed neural-operator pipelines. The workflow links problem specification, data generation, operator training, and checkpoint-based inference. A stateful input graph converts multi-turn natural-language input and user edits into validated problem specifications. The data-generation module then samples parameters, solves the configured governing-equation with FEniCSx finite-element backend, and stores the solutions as operator-ready tensors. The training and inference stages use a registry-based interface, allowing different neural operators to be trained and deployed without changing the surrounding pipeline. In the current implementation, we instantiate this interface with a multi-branch Bayesian DeepONet. Experiments on benchmark ODE and PDE tasks show that PDEFlow can construct valid specifications, generate solver-backed datasets, train neural operators across steady and transient problem classes, and provide solver-free predictions from saved checkpoints. The framework is designed for repeatable scientific and engineering workflows where many related physics configurations must be specified, simulated, learned, and queried with minimal manual intervention.

</details>


### [185] [Towards Personalized Differentially Private Learning for Decentralized Local Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04777)
*Longzhu He,Peng Tang,Chaozhuo Li,Jinhu Fu,Litian Zhang,Li Sun,Philip S. Yu,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对去中心化图数据中现有本地差分隐私方法忽视用户个性化隐私需求导致数据效用下降的问题，提出PPGNN框架，通过个性化扰动机制(PPM)和加权校准策略(FlexProp)实现个性化的隐私-效用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化图数据收集与分析面临隐私挑战，现有LDP图学习方法假设所有用户具有统一的隐私需求，忽略了真实系统中用户异质化、个性化的隐私偏好，导致噪声注入不灵活、图数据严重失真、下游分析效用下降。

Method: 提出PPGNN框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 个性化扰动机制(PPM)，允许用户设置个性化隐私预算进行本地扰动；(2) 加权校准策略FlexProp，处理异构隐私水平和噪声失真问题。

Result: 在六个真实图数据集上的大量实验表明，PPGNN能够在去中心化图学习场景中有效平衡个性化隐私保护与数据效用。

Conclusion: PPGNN通过个性化隐私预算分配和加权校准策略，解决了传统统一隐私预算方法在去中心化图数据中的局限性，实现了隐私保护与数据效用的有效权衡。

Abstract: Graph-structured data is increasingly generated and stored in decentralized environments, such as social platforms, mobile applications, and edge networks, where users maintain control over their local graph data. However, collecting and analyzing such decentralized graph data for downstream learning tasks raises significant privacy concerns, as nodes and their attributes often contain sensitive personal information. Local Differential Privacy (LDP) has emerged as a promising solution for privacy-preserving data collection without relying on trusted servers. Nevertheless, existing LDP-based graph learning methods typically assume uniform privacy requirements across users, ignoring the heterogeneous and personalized privacy preferences commonly observed in real-world systems. This uniform treatment leads to inflexible noise injection at the data collection stage, resulting in substantial distortion of graph data and degraded utility in subsequent analysis. To address this limitation, we propose PPGNN, a personalized differentially private framework for decentralized graph data. PPGNN enables user-specific privacy budgets during local perturbation while preserving analytical utility. To handle heterogeneous privacy levels and noise distortion, we design a two-stage solution consisting of a Personalized Perturbation Mechanism (PPM) and a weighted calibration strategy, FlexProp. Extensive experiments on six real-world graph datasets demonstrate that PPGNN effectively balances personalized privacy protection and data utility in decentralized graph learning scenarios.

</details>


### [186] [Relational Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Pricing in High-Speed Railway Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05179)
*Enrique Adrian Villarrubia-Martin,David Muñoz-Valero,Luis Rodriguez-Benitez,Giovanni Montana,Luis Jimenez-Linares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实体图建模的多智能体强化学习框架，用于解决铁路定价中部分可观测环境下的竞争策略学习问题，通过图卷积网络和注意力机制处理市场拓扑结构，在铁路定价仿真中实现了更高的收益和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放松管制的铁路系统中，运营商必须在部分可观测环境下动态定价，且由于监管禁止竞争者之间直接通信以避免合谋，智能体只能从可观测市场数据中推断策略互动。标准多智能体强化学习方法通常将观测视为非结构化向量，忽略了决定策略互动的市场拓扑结构，因此需要一种能够利用市场拓扑信息的建模方法。

Method: 提出实体图建模方法，将环境表示为运营单元（而非决策智能体或静态基础设施）的图结构，编码竞争、协调和连通关系；在此基础上扩展多智能体双延迟深度确定性策略梯度（MATD3）算法，通过多层关系图卷积网络处理实体特征，并通过学习到的注意力机制进行聚合。

Result: 在铁路定价强化学习环境中，该框架在两种不同市场复杂度的设定下，相比具有代表性的关系型和非关系型基线方法，实现了更高的收益和更好的稳定性。

Conclusion: 基于实体图建模的关系图卷积网络结合注意力机制的强化学习方法能有效利用市场拓扑结构，提升铁路定价场景下的策略学习性能。

Abstract: In liberalised railway systems, operators must set prices dynamically in an environment with partial observability, as they retain private information about their objectives and performance, where regulatory constraints prohibit communication or direct information exchange between competitors to prevent explicit collusion. Consequently, agents must learn to infer strategic interactions only from observable market data which presents a significant challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning, where standard approaches typically treat observations as unstructured vectors, ignoring the underlying market topology that governs strategic interactions. To address this, an entity graph modelling approach is proposed, which represents the environment as a graph of operational units, rather than decision-making agents or static infrastructure, encoding competition, coordination, and connectivity relations between entities. Then, an extension of the multi-agent twin delayed deep deterministic policy gradient algorithm with graph-based representation learning processes the features of the entities through a multi-layer relational graph convolutional network and aggregates them via a learnt attention mechanism. Experimental results in a rail pricing reinforcement learning environment show that this novel framework achieves higher revenue and stability in two different settings of increasing market complexity compared to a representative selection of relational and non-relational baselines. The code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Kinrre/RelationalRailPricing-RL

</details>


### [187] [Noisy-Channel Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05198)
*Yusuke Sakai,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于噪声信道分解的最小贝叶斯风险解码方法，通过引入双向效应（假设到参考和参考到假设）来解决传统MBR解码中评估指标不对称的问题，将解码分解为四个交互组件，提供了统一的解释框架和更好的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MBR解码在假设选择时计算的是基于给定伪参考的期望效用分数，而常用评估指标（如BLEU、COMET）具有不对称性。这种设计上的不一致性导致解码结果可能不够准确，因此需要一种能够自然地纳入双向效应的方法来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 本文对MBR解码进行噪声信道分解，将其分解为四个交互组件：假设到参考的似然、参考到假设的似然、假设先验和参考先验。这种分解方式自然地将双向效应纳入MBR解码中，为现有的MBR变体提供了统一的解释框架。

Result: 通道级别的贡献在不同评估指标下表现出不同的特征，但在不同任务中保持一致性。适当的通道加权可能优于原始MBR解码方法。

Conclusion: 噪声信道分解为MBR解码提供了统一解释，增强了指标和任务层面的可解释性。通过分解各通道的贡献，可以实现更精细的解码优化，提示合理调整通道权重可能进一步提升解码性能。

Abstract: Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding yields more robust and higher-quality text generation than maximum a posteriori (MAP) decoding by selecting hypotheses that maximize expected utility over sampled pseudo-references. However, there exists a discrepancy in the design: hypothesis selection calculates expected utility scores conditioned on given pseudo-references, while commonly used evaluation metrics, e.g., BLEU and COMET, are asymmetric. Therefore, it is important to consider both hypothesis-to-reference and reference-to-hypothesis directional effects. In this study, we introduce a noisy channel decomposition of MBR decoding that naturally incorporates bidirectional effects to account for these asymmetries. We decompose MBR decoding into four interacting components: hypothesis-to-reference likelihood, reference-to-hypothesis likelihood, hypothesis prior, and reference prior. This decomposition provides a unified interpretation of existing MBR variants and enables metric- and task-specific interpretability by isolating the contribution of each channel. Our comprehensive analysis reveals that channel-wise contributions exhibit distinct characteristics across metrics while remaining consistent across tasks, and suggests that appropriate channel weighting may lead to improvements over original MBR decoding.

</details>


### [188] [Compressed Computation under $L^4$ Loss is likely Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04800)
*Francisco Ferreira da Silva,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络在超位置（superposition）中进行计算的能力，通过一个压缩计算任务（50个神经元计算100个稀疏输入特征的ReLU）发现，使用L4损失函数训练能诱导出超位置计算解决方案，并通过逆向工程揭示了网络使用稀疏二进制码字编码特征、用伪逆解码的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络中表征超位置已得到较好理解，但计算超位置（即用少于函数数量的神经元计算多个函数）尚未被充分研究，且缺乏通过训练自然涌现（而非手工设计）的玩具模型。

Method: 设计单隐藏层ReLU网络（50神经元），输入100个稀疏特征，任务是对每个特征计算ReLU。使用L4损失（误差四次方的均值）而非传统L2损失进行训练，诱导超位置计算解决方案，并逆向分析网络内部机制。

Result: 网络为每个特征分配稀疏二进制码字（编码器），并使用伪逆矩阵解码。仅需三个标量参数即可描述该编码方案，且能恢复大部分网络性能；通过手工设计编码构建的等价网络验证了该机制的有效性。

Conclusion: L4损失能有效诱导计算超位置的出现，揭示了神经网络在超位置中执行计算的机制——稀疏编码加伪逆解码，为理解神经网络如何在有限神经元下计算更多函数提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Neural networks are thought to represent concepts as directions in their activation space, and superposition lets them encode more concepts than they have dimensions. It is natural to ask whether they can also compute more functions than they have neurons, i.e., perform computation in superposition. In this regime many functions of sparse inputs are evaluated by a layer with fewer neurons than there are functions to compute. Representation in superposition is by now fairly well understood, but computation in superposition is not, and there are few toy models of it arising through training rather than being hand designed. As a toy model of computation in superposition we study the compressed-computation setup: a single-hidden-layer ReLU network with 50 neurons that must compute the ReLU of each of 100 sparse input features. We show that training it under an $L^4$ loss (the mean fourth power of the error), rather than the usual $L^2$, elicits a solution that appears to compute all features in superposition. We then reverse-engineer this solution. We find that the network assigns each feature a sparse binary codeword over neurons and decodes it with a pseudoinverse of the encoder. Given these codewords, a description with only three scalars recovers most of the network's performance, and we validate it by building equivalent networks from hand-designed codes.

</details>


### [189] [CanniUplift: A Holistic Framework for Mitigating Seller and Incentive Cannibalization in E-commerce Uplift Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05242)
*Zuwang He,Shihao Shu,Yuli Qu,Hanyu Gao,Ziliang Zhang,Diwei Chen,Xiangda Yan,Buyu Gao,Tanchao Zhu,Yumeng Li,Junxiong Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CanniUplift框架，通过平台级全局对齐（PGA）和赎回分解去噪（RDD）解决电商多卖家场景下的卖家层面和激励层面双重蚕食效应，在离线和在线实验中均显著优于基线方法，并实现4.08%的增量GMV提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统增量建模在复杂多卖家电商环境中违背SUTVA假设，面临卖家层面蚕食（激励导致用户在不同店铺间转移消费而非平台整体增长）和激励层面蚕食（自然转化或替代奖励引入噪声）两大挑战，亟需统一框架来缓解双重蚕食效应。

Method: 提出CanniUplift统一框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 平台级全局对齐（PGA），通过全局GMV一致性约束捕获跨店铺替代效应；2) 赎回分解去噪（RDD），利用赎回行为在全体空间框架内分解处理结果并降低归因噪声；3) Treat-Attention机制，建模用户历史行为与当前处理选项之间的复杂交互。

Result: 在合成数据和大规模工业数据集上，CanniUplift显著优于SOTA基线；消融实验证实PGA和RDD的集成一致提升wAUUC和wQINI；在线A/B测试中实现4.08%的平台增量GMV相对提升并改善ROI。

Conclusion: CanniUplift有效缓解了多卖家场景下的双重蚕食效应，能够驱动全局平台增长，已在线上成功部署。

Abstract: Personalized incentive allocation is vital for e-commerce, where uplift modeling is the standard for estimating Individual Treatment Effects (ITE). However, traditional models often fail in complex multi-seller environments with violations of the Stable Unit Treatment Value Assumption (SUTVA). We identify two critical challenges: Seller-level Cannibalization, where incentives shift expenditure between shops without growing the platform, and Incentive-level Cannibalization, where organic conversions or alternative rewards introduce significant noise into incrementality estimation. In this paper, we propose CanniUplift, a unified framework to mitigate these dual-source cannibalization effects. Specifically, we design Platform-level Global Alignment (PGA) to capture cross-shop substitution through global GMV consistency constraints. To tackle incentive-driven noise, we introduce Redemption-based Decomposition Denoising (RDD), which uses redemption behavior to decompose treated outcomes and reduce attribution noise within an entire-space framework. Furthermore, a Treat-Attention mechanism is designed to model intricate interactions between users' historical behaviors and current treatment options. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and large-scale industrial datasets demonstrate that CanniUplift significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Ablation studies confirm that the integration of PGA and RDD consistently improves wAUUC and wQINI. Successfully deployed online, our framework achieved a 4.08% relative increase in platform-wide incremental GMV (Delta GMV) over the production baseline and improved ROI in online A/B tests, proving effective in driving global platform growth.

</details>


### [190] [Layer-Parallel Inference Reduces Encrypted Nonlinear Depth in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04819)
*Ligong Han,Kai Xu,Hao Wang,Ruijiang Gao,Akash Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化牛顿层并行（SNLP）方法，将Transformer的L层顺序非线性深度压缩为少量求解器迭代加线性结构化修正，在0.5B IDN模型上减少2.65倍符号自举（53→20），仅增加+1.2%困惑度，跨8个模型4个架构家族验证SNLP误差放大均低于顺序推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同态加密（FHE）下的Transformer推理被大量非线性块的顺序组合所瓶颈，需要减少层间顺序非线性深度以提升FHE效率。

Method: 提出结构化牛顿层并行（SNLP），通过将L层顺序非线性压缩为少量牛顿求解器迭代加上线性结构化校正，使每个Transformer块的非线性操作（softmax、RMSNorm）可并行化处理。

Result: 在8个模型4个架构家族上验证：0.5B IDN模型符号自举从53降至20（2.65x），困惑度仅增+1.2%；误差放大1.36x vs 顺序推理1.42x；所有测试模型SNLP误差放大均更低。

Conclusion: SNLP能显著降低FHE下Transformer推理的计算负担，且与块级FHE友好算子设计互补；softmax近似主导误差预算，CKKS算术噪声可忽略。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) enables computation on encrypted data, but practical encrypted Transformer inference is bottlenecked by the sequential composition of many nonlinear blocks. We study whether Structured Newton Layer Parallelism (SNLP) can make this inter-layer composition more FHE-friendly: each Transformer block still requires polynomial approximations for operations such as softmax and RMSNorm, but SNLP reduces the layerwise sequential nonlinear depth from L stages to a small number of solver iterations plus linear structured corrections. Using a simulation framework based on Chebyshev polynomial approximations, we measure error accumulation under sequential versus SNLP inference across 8 models and 4 architecture families. On a 0.5B IDN-trained model, SNLP reduces symbolic bootstraps from 53 to 20 (2.65x) with only +1.2% perplexity degradation, while lowering error amplification (1.36x vs. 1.42x). Across all tested models, SNLP has lower amplification than sequential inference. Ablations show that softmax approximation dominates the error budget and CKKS arithmetic noise is negligible in our setting, suggesting that SNLP is complementary to block-level FHE-friendly operator design rather than a replacement for it.

</details>


### [191] [KinEMbed: Decoding Kinematics from Electromyography via Cross-Modal Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04820)
*Sofia Gilardini,Chenfei Ma,Kianoush Nazarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KinEMbed，一种跨模态对比学习框架，用于从表面肌电信号回归手部运动学参数。该方法在NinaPro DB8数据集上优于PCA、PLS、自编码器和CEBRA等基线方法，尤其对拇指关节的估计提升最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表面肌电信号的表征学习方法主要聚焦于离散手势分类，而对连续运动学回归的关注不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索对比学习在连续手部运动学回归中的应用。

Method: 提出KinEMbed框架，采用跨模态对比学习联合训练双编码器——分别处理窗口化EMG特征和运动学（关节角度）目标。训练后的嵌入继承了运动学空间的几何结构，且在推理时无需运动学信号输入。

Result: 在NinaPro DB8数据集（含健全受试者和肢体差异受试者，N=11）上，KinEMbed在留出会话评估中优于PCA、PLS、自编码器和CEBRA等基线方法，其中对最具挑战性的拇指关节自由度的改进最为显著。

Conclusion: 本文将这项工作定位为从结构化可穿戴生物信号进行手部运动学回归的对比表征学习的第一步，展示了跨模态对比学习在该任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Decoding hand kinematics from surface electromyography (EMG) is a core challenge in wearable biosignal processing with clinical relevance for prosthetic control and motor rehabilitation. Most representation learning approaches for EMG focus on discrete gesture classification, and few focus on continuous regression. We present KinEMbed, a cross-modal contrastive learning framework for hand kinematics regression that jointly trains dual encoders -- one for windowed EMG features and one for kinematic (joint angle) targets. The resulting embeddings inherit the geometric structure of the kinematic space without requiring kinematic signals at inference time. Evaluating on the NinaPro DB8 dataset that includes both able-bodied users and subjects with limb difference (N=11), KinEMbed outperforms PCA, PLS, autoencoder and contrastive (CEBRA) baselines on held-out sessions, with largest gains on the most challenging thumb degrees of articulation. We position this work as a first step toward contrastive representation learning for regression of hand kinematics from structured wearable biosignals.

</details>


### [192] [Air Quality Downscaling with Station-Guided Pseudo-Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05292)
*Guorun Wang,Simone Foti,Andreas D. Demou,Leonidas Kotoulas,Theodoros Christoudias,Alexandros Koliousis,Mihalis Nicolaou,Stefanos Zafeiriou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于站点引导的框架，利用多尺度Transformer网络和时空无关传播策略，将粗分辨率CAMS大气成分场超分辨率（×40，≈1 km）并纠正偏差，以精细估算欧洲PM2.5浓度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗分辨率大气场与局部PM2.5观测之间存在空间支撑不匹配问题：像素代表区域平均值，而地面真值观测是连续空间信号中的离散、未对齐样本，亟需弥合这一差距。

Method: 采用多尺度Transformer网络，融合CAMS粗分辨率场与多模态辅助信息（人类活动、土地覆盖、海拔、卫星气溶胶观测、风场）；引入时间无关的传播策略，利用OpenAQ观测的空间高斯混合插值对稀疏原位数据进行稠密监督。

Result: 在欧洲范围内的定性和站点级评估表明，模型能够恢复细粒度空间结构，并有效缓解局部CAMS偏差。

Conclusion: 所提出的站点引导框架无需时序建模即可实现对PM2.5的高分辨率降尺度与偏差校正，为区域到局地的污染物空间估算提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Super-resolving coarse atmospheric fields to local PM$_{2.5}$ variations is uniquely challenged by a mismatch in spatial support: while pixels represent regional averages, ground-truth observations are discrete, unaligned samples of a continuous spatial signal. To bridge this gap, we present a station-guided framework for high-resolution PM$_{2.5}$ downscaling over Europe. Taking coarse CAMS atmospheric composition fields alongside heterogeneous side information (i.e., human activity, land cover, elevation, satellite aerosol observations, and wind fields) our framework jointly super-resolves ($\times 40$, $\approx$ 1 km) and bias-corrects CAMS rasters, without relying on temporal sequence modelling. To address the challenge of densely supervising our multi-scale transformer network with sparse in-situ data, we introduce a time-agnostic propagation strategy that utilises spatial Gaussian blending of interpolated OpenAQ observations. Extensive qualitative and station-level evaluations across Europe demonstrate that our model recovers fine-grained spatial structures and effectively mitigates localised CAMS biases.

</details>


### [193] [Probably Correct Optimal Stable Matching under Two-Sided Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04824)
*Andreas Athanasopoulos,Anne-Marie George,Christos Dimitrakakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了双边市场中稳定匹配的序列学习问题，提出了一种利用部分偏好信息识别最优稳定匹配的纯探索算法，并扩展到了遗憾最小化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多假设偏好已知或仅考虑单边不确定性，本文旨在处理双边不确定性并利用部分偏好信息来高效识别最优稳定匹配。

Method: 提出了"泛稳定匹配"（pervasive stable matching）概念，设计了基于淘汰（elimination-based）的算法，其停止准则利用了学习到的部分偏好结构，并提供了精细的样本复杂度分析。

Result: 在纯探索设置下实现了样本复杂度上界改进；在遗憾最小化设置中获得了不依赖最小奖励间隙Δ_min的遗憾界。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入泛稳定匹配概念和淘汰算法，有效解决了双边不确定性下的最优稳定匹配识别问题，并拓展到遗憾最小化场景，消除了对最小奖励间隙的依赖。

Abstract: We study a sequential learning problem for stable matchings in two-sided markets where preferences on both sides are initially unknown. We focus on a centralized setting where an algorithm matches agents at each time step and receives noisy rewards that reflect the preferences of the matched agents, following a semi-bandit feedback structure. We adopt a pure exploration perspective, aiming to efficiently identify the optimal stable matching with high probability. Our work extends prior results by handling \emph{two-sided uncertainty} and by exploiting \emph{partial preference} information. A central ingredient is the notion of \textbf{pervasive stable matching}, which enables the identification of optimal stable matchings under partial preferences. We propose elimination-based algorithms whose stopping criteria exploit the structure of the learned partial preferences, and provide a refined sample-complexity analysis. Beyond pure exploration, we extend our approach to regret minimization and establish regret bounds with respect to the \emph{optimal} stable matching that avoid dependence on the minimum reward gap $Δ_{\min}$.

</details>


### [194] [TREK: Distill to Explore, Reinforce to Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05339)
*Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Kayhan Behdin,Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Hejian Sang,Xiaomin Li,Wenhui Zhu,Xinchen Du,Aida Rahmattalabi,Ran He,Sen Na,Zhipeng Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TREK提出了一种两阶段训练框架：先通过前向KL蒸馏从教师模型（外部或自身）中提取已验证的推理轨迹以扩展学生策略支持集，再切换回标准GRPO强化学习。在数学推理和智能体任务上均显著优于基线GRPO，尤其对硬提示（on-policy支持外）效果突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO在硬提示上停滞，因为正确解模式不在当前on-policy支持内；现有蒸馏方法只做模仿，未用于探索支持扩展。

Method: 1）识别学生通过率低的硬提示；2）从教师（黑盒/白盒/自上下文）获取候选解并保留学生似然最高的top-r条；3）短阶段前向KL训练将这些模式拉入学生支持；4）切回on-policy GRPO精炼。

Result: 数学推理：Qwen3-8B在AIME 2025从36.9→40.3，AIME 2024从47.9→51.1（avg@16）；自上下文变体分别达38.5和49.6。智能体任务：ALFWorld 75.8→82.8，ScienceWorld 12.5→26.7，且困难任务早期即达高成功率。

Conclusion: TREK是一种通用、高效且无需教师模型内部信息的强化学习增强框架，通过蒸馏扩展探索支持而非模仿，显著提升GRPO对硬提示的求解能力。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is effective when the current policy already samples useful reasoning trajectories, but it stalls on hard prompts whose correct solution modes lie outside the student's on-policy support. We propose TREK (Teacher-Routed Exploration via Forward KL), a simple staged procedure that uses distillation not for imitation but for exploration support expansion. A key advantage of TREK is its generality: because it only consumes verified output trajectories, it can use an external black-box teacher, a white-box teacher, or the same model given additional inference-time context, and it can efficiently identify which hard-prompt samples are most worth consolidating even when teacher internals are unavailable. TREK first identifies prompts where the unaided student has very low pass rate, queries a proposal source to produce verified candidate solutions, keeps the top-$r$ proposals ranked by current student likelihood, applies a short forward-KL phase to pull those verified modes into the student's support, and then returns to standard on-policy GRPO refinement. On mathematical reasoning, TREK with DeepSeek-V4 proposals improves Qwen3 models across all tested scales on AIME 2024 and AIME 2025; for Qwen3-8B, it improves AIME 2025 from 36.9 to 40.3 and AIME 2024 from 47.9 to 51.1 (avg@16), while the self-context variant reaches 38.5 and 49.6 without an external teacher. On agentic tasks, TREK raises ALFWorld success rate from 75.8 to 82.8 and ScienceWorld success rate from 12.5 to 26.7; notably, on the hardest task types, TREK achieves high success rates early in training while unaided GRPO requires substantially more optimization steps to reach comparable levels.

</details>


### [195] [Representing and Detecting Label Ambiguity in IMU-Based Exercise Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04842)
*Andreas Spilz,Heiko Oppel,Michael Munz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对家庭物理治疗中因无人监督导致动作执行不正确的问题，本文提出一种从IMU数据自动生成每个重复动作标签分布的方法，采用KL散度目标训练网络，在不损失分类精度的情况下检测动作的模糊性及相关类别，并在四个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭物理治疗中动作执行缺乏监督，现有IMU动作评估系统将每个重复动作硬分类到单一类别，但大量边界样本即使专家也难以一致标注；传统分类器将模糊样本强制归入单一类别，丢失了不确定性信息。

Method: 提出一种无需大规模标注者即可自动为每个重复动作生成标签分布的方法；训练网络以KL散度为损失函数拟合完整标签分布（模糊方法），与使用独热编码+交叉熵的基线进行对比；从网络输出判断重复动作是否模糊及哪些类别相关。

Result: 在四个IMU运动数据集上，模糊方法在分类性能上达到或超越基线；在模糊性检测和相关类别识别上更加可靠；在训练目标中引入标签分布信息不会降低分类精度。

Conclusion: 通过标签分布表示训练目标可以在不牺牲分类精度的前提下，提供额外的模糊性信息，适用于边界样本的处理。

Abstract: Home-based physiotherapy is performed without supervision, which leads to incorrect execution and motivates systems that assess movement automatically from inertial measurement units (IMUs). Such systems assign each repetition to a category, yet a relevant share of repetitions falls near a class boundary, where even trained raters disagree. Classifiers trained with one-hot labels collapse these borderline repetitions onto a single class and discard this ambiguity. We address this with a method that automatically generates a label distribution per repetition without a large rater pool. We train a network to reproduce the full distribution with a Kullback-Leibler objective, the ambiguity approach, and compare it against a one-hot cross-entropy baseline on four IMU exercise datasets. From the network output we further determine whether a repetition is ambiguous and which classes are relevant to it. The ambiguity approach matched or exceeded the baseline classification on all four datasets, and detected ambiguity and the relevant classes more reliably. Representing the label distribution in the training target therefore adds information about ambiguity at no cost to classification.

</details>


### [196] [What Does a Discrete Diffusion Model Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05381)
*Rodrigo Casado Noguerales,Bernhard Schölkopf,Thomas Hofmann,Aran Raoufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格推导了连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）的证据下界（ELBO），证明其负值等于数据熵加上从oracle逆向过程到学习过程的路径KL散度；其唯一优化器是真实逆向跳跃率关于当前噪声状态的条件期望，不可约代价是前向过程破坏信息速率$-\tfrac{d}{dt}I(Z_0; Z_t)$；对于序列的token分解噪声，优化器有三种精确坐标形式（去噪器、空穴（桥插件）和分数），并给出了封闭形式的相互转换；框架统一了MDM、UDM、SEDD、GIDD等现有方法，解释了掩码扩散与均匀扩散的区别，并通过精确可解模型数值验证了所有结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型中的去噪器、分数比和桥插件预测器本质上是同一对象在不同坐标系下的表示，使用错误的坐标训练和采样会导致过程发生变化。本文旨在严格推导连续时间马尔可夫链的ELBO，揭示其精确的优化目标，并厘清不同参数化形式之间的关系与差异。

Method: 从任意噪声过程的连续时间马尔可夫链ELBO的严格推导出发（包含边界项），证明Oracle Distance定理（负ELBO等于数据熵加上路径KL散度）；推导唯一优化器的条件期望形式；对于token分解噪声，给出优化器的三种精确坐标形式及转换公式；通过精确可解模型进行数值验证。

Result: 负ELBO精确等于数据熵加上路径KL散度，而非仅是下界；唯一优化器为条件期望；不可约代价为$-\tfrac{d}{dt}I(Z_0; Z_t)$；所有噪声过程的最佳负ELBO相同（数据熵）；给出了去噪器、空穴（桥插件）和分数三种坐标及其转换；统一了MDM、UDM、SEDD、GIDD等框架；解释了为什么掩码扩散中denoiser和cavity一致而均匀扩散中不一致；证明了均匀扩散的去噪器参数化在初始化时ELBO发散而桥插件保持有限。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的不同参数化形式本质上是同一优化目标的等价表示，选择错误的坐标会导致训练和采样的过程不匹配。本文的理论框架为理解和设计离散扩散模型提供了统一视角，并校准了ELBO的精确实现。

Abstract: What does a discrete diffusion model learn: a denoiser, a score ratio, or a bridge plug-in predictor? At the level of jump rates, these are one object in different coordinates, and reading a neural network in the wrong coordinate changes the process being trained and sampled. Starting with a rigorous derivation of the continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) ELBO for any noising process, boundary terms included, we prove the \emph{Oracle Distance} theorem: the negative ELBO is exactly equal to the data entropy plus the path KL from the oracle reverse process to the learned one, not merely a bound. Its unique optimizer is therefore the conditional expectation of the true reverse jump rate given the current noisy state, and its irreducible cost is the rate at which the forward process $Z_t$ destroys information about the clean data $Z_0$, $-\tfrac{d}{dt}I(Z_0; Z_t)$, so every noising process shares the same best achievable negative ELBO: the data entropy. For sequences with token-factorizing noise, the oracle projection yields three exact coordinates for the optimizer: denoiser, cavity (bridge plug-in), and score, with closed-form conversions among them. This framework identifies which law each loss in the literature actually optimizes, recovering MDM, UDM, SEDD, and GIDD as special cases; explains why denoiser and cavity coincide for masked diffusion but not for uniform diffusion; proves that a denoiser parameterization makes the uniform ELBO diverge at initialization while the bridge plug-in stays finite; and calibrates ELBO implementations exactly at initialization. Every identity is verified numerically, without approximation, on an exactly solvable model.

</details>


### [197] [Weak-to-Strong Generalization via Direct On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05394)
*Shiyuan Feng,Huan-ang Gao,Haohan Chi,Hanlin Wu,Zhilong Zhang,Zheng Jiang,Bingxiang He,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Direct-OPD方法，通过将弱模型RL前后的策略差异作为隐式奖励信号，蒸馏到强模型上，实现跨规模的RL效果复用，在AIME 2024上显著提升Qwen3-1.7B性能（48.3%→62.4%），仅需8张A100 GPU训练4小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能提升语言模型推理能力，但在每个新强模型上重复运行成本极高（需大量rollout）；随着模型规模增大，后训练本身成为瓶颈。因此探索弱到强（weak-to-strong）替代方案：在便宜的小模型上运行RL，然后将学习成果复用到强模型上。

Method: Direct-OPD：对比弱模型RL前后的检查点，将其对数概率比作为密集隐式奖励信号，在强模型自己的on-policy状态上应用该信号进行蒸馏。不训练显式奖励模型，也不在目标模型上运行稀疏奖励RL。

Result: Qwen3-1.7B在AIME 2024上从48.3%提升至62.4%，仅需8×A100 GPU训练4小时；优于在强模型上直接运行RL；支持多个策略偏移的顺序组合。

Conclusion: RL成果可以作为隐式奖励信号跨模型规模复用（而非仅作为最终模型进行模仿），Direct-OPD有效实现了弱到强的RL迁移。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a powerful recipe for improving language-model reasoning, but it is expensive to repeat on every new strong model because the target model must generate many rollouts during training. As models scale, post-training itself becomes a bottleneck. We study a weak-to-strong alternative: run RL on a smaller model where rollouts are cheaper, then reuse what that RL run learned to improve a stronger target model. Directly distilling the post-RL weak teacher is not enough, because the teacher's final policy mixes useful RL gains with the limitations of the smaller model. We propose Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD), which transfers the teacher's RL-induced policy shift instead. Direct-OPD compares the post-RL teacher with its own pre-RL reference and treats their log-ratio as a dense implicit reward for the student. In plain terms, the checkpoint pair tells us which actions RL made the weak model more or less likely to take, and Direct-OPD applies that signal on the stronger student's own on-policy states. This directly reuses the weak model's RL supervision signal without training an explicit reward model or running sparse-reward RL on the target model. Empirically, Direct-OPD consistently leverages weaker teachers to improve stronger target models; notably, it boosts Qwen3-1.7B from 48.3% to 62.4% on AIME 2024 in just 4 hours on 8 A100 GPUs. It outperforms step-matched direct RL and enables the sequential composition of multiple policy shifts. Our results show that RL outcomes can be reused across model scales as implicit reward signals, not merely as final models to imitate.

</details>


### [198] [Framework for Grouping Local Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04856)
*Viki Peeva,Wil M. P. van der Aalst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对局部过程模型（LPM）发现中的模型爆炸和重复问题，提出了一种基于分组的框架，通过选取每组代表性LPM来构建最优样本，替代传统仅依赖最高评分LPM的采样方式，实验证明该方法能有效降低重复性并提高覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LPM发现算法会产生大量结构或行为相似的模型，分析师无法逐一审查，而当前默认使用最高评分LPM作为最优样本的方式在不同应用场景下效果不佳，尤其是当目标是理解流程时，高评分样本存在严重重复问题。

Method: 提出一个分组框架，通过过程模型相似度度量或基于事件日志中数据属性的上下文相似度对LPM进行分组，然后从每组中选取一个代表性LPM构成最优样本。通过在多个事件日志上比较最高评分样本与代表性样本的重复性和覆盖率来验证有效性。

Result: 实验表明，相比传统最高评分LPM样本，基于分组选取的代表性样本在多个事件日志上能显著降低模型重复性，同时提高对流程的理解覆盖率。

Conclusion: 当目标是通过LPM理解流程时，使用最高评分模型作为最优样本是糟糕的选择；本文提出的分组框架能有效解决模型重复问题，为不同应用场景提供更合适的最优样本。

Abstract: Local Process Models (LPMs) are an underexplored concept in process mining. LPMs describe patterns in event data considering sequence, choice, concurrency, and loop. In recent years, process mining has proved successful in the analysis and improvement of operational processes. More often than not, surprising findings are found when one does not consider the full process, making LPMs and their discovery highly valuable. However, similar to other pattern mining approaches, LPM discovery algorithms face the problems of model explosion and model repetition, i.e., the algorithms may create hundreds if not thousands of LPMs, and subsets of them are close in structure or behavior. Practically, no analyst would be able to comb through thousands of LPMs leading to using a sample of LPMs that are easily accessible. The current sentiment is that the top-scoring LPMs form the optimal sample to be presented. However, different applications should demand a different optimal sample. With this work, we show that if the goal of the mined LPMs is to understand a process, using the top-scoring LPMs as an optimal sample is a poor choice because of high repetition. We propose a framework for grouping LPMs and creating an optimal sample by taking one representative LPM for each group. We measure similarity between models via established process model similarity measures or by comparing the context in which an LPM appears. The context is formed using data attributes available in the underlying event logs. We demonstrate the usefulness of grouping on multiple event logs by comparing repetition and coverage between samples comprised of the top-scoring models and the representatives of discovered groups.

</details>


### [199] [Enhancing the Forecasting Capability of Multi-Model Blending Algorithms for Extreme Precipitation via Joint Use of Station and Gridded Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04862)
*Yu Wang,Yong Cao,Kan Dai,Yue Shen,Xiaoqing Zeng,Ruixia Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于U-Net的两阶段框架（概率分类+数值重建），用于融合六种主要数值天气预报（NWP）模型的降水预报。通过引入2411个国家级气象站观测数据构建站-格点联合监督机制，显著提升了极端降水预报的准确率，在2025年汛期测试中，暴雨TS评分较最优NWP提升38.4%，极端降水（≥100mm）TS提升至0.1以上，达到业务可用水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值天气预报模型在极端降水预报中存在系统性强度低估和空间位移问题，传统多模型融合算法基于逐像素加权融合会导致降水区域扩张和极值平滑，亟需一种能够同时校正空间偏差和强度偏差的融合方法。

Method: 提出U-Net两阶段框架：第一阶段进行概率分类，第二阶段进行数值重建；创新性地引入站-格点联合监督机制，将中国2411个国家气象站观测数据融入损失函数，同时约束空间结构和峰值强度。

Result: 在2025年汛期独立样本测试中，暴雨（≥50mm）TS较最优NWP提升38.4%；极端降水（≥100mm）TS提升至0.1以上；模型具备数据驱动的空间校正能力，可有效对齐雨带；加入站点观测使暴雨TS额外提升10.4%，并平衡了偏差。

Conclusion: 多源联合监督机制能有效增强模型对极端降水事件的捕捉能力，将原本缺乏参考价值的预报提升至具备一定业务实用性的水平。

Abstract: Accurate extreme precipitation forecasting is critical for disaster mitigation but remains challenging for numerical weather prediction (NWP) models due to systemic intensity underestimation and spatial displacement. Traditional precipitation multi-model blending algorithms perform pixel-by-pixel blending on the forecast field based on weights, which may lead to the expansion of precipitation areas and the smoothing of extreme values. This study proposes an U-Net based two-stage framework: probability classification followed by value reconstruction, to blend forecasts from six major NWP models. A novel station-grid joint supervision mechanism is introduced by integrating observations from 2411 national meteorological stations in China into the loss function, simultaneously constraining spatial structures and peak intensities. Evaluations using independent samples from the 2025 flood season demonstrate that our model significantly outperforms both individual NWPs and current operational products. For rainstorms (>=50 mm), the Threat Score (TS) improved by 38.4% compared to the best NWP. Notably, for extreme events (>=100 mm) driven by extratropical cyclones and the subtropical high, the model successfully elevated the TS to above 0.1, transforming forecasts from having negligible reference value into those with certain operational utility. Furthermore, the model exhibits data-driven spatial correction capabilities, effectively realigning systematic rainbelt displacements with actual precipitation centers. The inclusion of station observations specifically enhanced the TS for rainstorms by 10.4% and effectively balanced the Bias. These results highlight the efficacy of multi-source joint supervision in enhancing the capture of extreme precipitation events.

</details>


### [200] [Active Learning on Adversarially Corrupted Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04869)
*Marco Bressan,Nicolò Cesa-Bianchi,Tommaso d`Orsi,Emmanuel Esposito,Silvio Lattanzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性环境下主动学习算法检测图中被恶意篡改的顶点集的问题。攻击者可在图中添加边以隐藏腐败顶点，其能力通过腐败顶点在原始图中的邻域大小衡量。作者设计了一种高效的主动学习算法，利用少量标签查询近似恢复腐败顶点，查询复杂度与攻击者能力和原始图的顶点扩展（vertex expansion）成多项式关系。核心贡献是通过适配平方和算法（sum-of-squares），首次证明顶点扩展在结构对抗攻击下对主动学习查询复杂度起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中，恶意实体可能通过添加边来篡改现有网络结构，隐藏一批腐败顶点。现有主动学习算法缺乏对此类结构对抗攻击的鲁棒性分析，因此需要设计能够高效检测腐败顶点集的算法。

Method: 定义对抗模型，攻击者可添加腐败顶点之间及腐败顶点与原始图之间的边，其能力由腐败顶点在原始图中的邻域大小衡量。提出主动学习算法，核心是一个多项式时间算法，通过适配平方和（sum-of-squares）算法来近似求解带基数约束的最小顶点扩展问题，从而实现腐败顶点集的近似恢复。

Result: 算法能够近似恢复腐败顶点集，查询复杂度与攻击者能力和原始图的顶点扩展成多项式关系。

Conclusion: 首次揭示了顶点扩展在结构对抗攻击下对主动学习查询复杂度起关键作用，为鲁棒主动学习算法设计提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Motivated by real-world scenarios where malicious entities tamper with existing networks, we define a model where an adversary seeks to hide a set of \emph{corrupted vertices} inside a graph $G^*$. To this end, the adversary can add edges between the corrupted vertices, as well as edges between the corrupted vertices and $G^*$, and its power is then measured by the size of the \emph{neighborhood} of the corrupted vertices in $G^*$. Our goal is to design an active learning algorithm that efficiently finds the subset of corrupted vertices using a small number of label queries. We devise an efficient algorithm that approximately recovers the corrupted vertices with a query complexity that depends polynomially on both the power of the adversary and the \emph{vertex expansion} of $G^*$, a fundamental measure of graph connectivity. At the heart of this result is a polynomial-time algorithm, obtained by carefully adapting sum-of-squares algorithms for approximating minimum expansion, that finds a set with small vertex expansion subject to cardinality constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time that the vertex expansion is shown to play a key role in determining the query complexity of active learning algorithms robust to structural adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [201] [RL-Ballast: Ship Ballast Water Path Planning and Clog Prediction via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04906)
*Ming-Kuan Lin,Yi-Chung Lai,Ming-Hsin Chiang,Tsung-Wei Pan,Jung-Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RL-Ballast，一种基于图深度强化学习的框架，用于自适应压载水路径规划和传感器节俭的堵塞候选评分，在单堵塞场景下平均决策步骤从61.0降至41.5，Top-3命中率100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航运4.0范式下，自主化和少船员船舶需要智能内部系统来维持运营安全和结构稳定性。传统基于规则或手动方法的压载水控制对液压异常（如阀门故障和管道堵塞）适应性有限，且通常依赖密集的压力或流量传感器进行诊断。

Method: 将阀门排列问题转化为54条可行流体传输路径（基于图论和深度优先搜索）；采用帧堆叠的液位信息和动作结果近似部分可观测的压载环境；在确定性推理中使用失败动作记忆和动态动作掩码避免重复无效动作并支持即时重路由；累积失败转移历史对可疑阀门或管段进行排序评分。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟显示：RL-Ballast完成所有意外单堵塞场景，平均决策步数从61.0降至41.5；失败历史评分方案实现100% Top-3命中率、66.7%严格Top-1命中率、83.3% Top-1平局命中率。

Conclusion: RL-Ballast在有限传感条件下实现了自适应重路由和面向维护的堵塞诊断。

Abstract: Under the Shipping 4.0 paradigm, autonomous and reduced-crew vessels require intelligent internal systems to maintain operational safety and structural stability. Ballast-water control is essential for ship trim and integrity, but conventional rule-based or manual approaches have limited adaptability to hydraulic anomalies such as valve failures and pipe blockages, and often depend on dense pressure or flow sensors for diagnosis. To address these limitations, this paper proposes RL-Ballast, a graph-based deep reinforcement learning framework for adaptive ballast-water path planning and sensor-frugal blockage candidate scoring. The valve-permutation problem is transformed into 54 feasible fluid-transfer routes generated using graph theory and depth-first search. The partially observable ballast environment is approximated with frame-stacked tank levels and action outcomes, allowing the agent to infer hidden blockage effects without explicitly modeling a high-dimensional POMDP. During deterministic inference, episode-level failed-action memory and dynamic action masking prevent repeated ineffective actions and support immediate rerouting. Failed transfer histories are further accumulated to rank suspicious valves or pipe segments without dense instrumentation. Monte Carlo simulations show that RL-Ballast completes all unexpected single-blockage scenarios and reduces average decision steps from 61.0 to 41.5 compared with a Dijkstra rule-based baseline. For diagnostic support, the failure-history scoring scheme achieves a 100% Top-3 hit rate, a 66.7% strict Top-1 hit rate, and an 83.3% Top-1 tie-hit rate under serially indistinguishable blockage conditions. These results suggest that RL-Ballast enables adaptive rerouting and maintenance-oriented blockage diagnosis under limited sensing conditions.

</details>


### [202] [When Do Foundation Models Pay Off? A Break-Even Analysis of Pretrained Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04919)
*Nicholas Tan Jerome,Frank Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了时间序列基础模型（Chronos, Moirai, Lag-Llama）与传统方法（Naive, ETS, ARIMA, XGBoost）在30个基准数据集上的盈亏平衡点，提出了两阶段决策框架：当训练样本<700且季节性强时直接使用零样本基础模型，否则仅在必要时进行5-10%的小规模试点测试。LoRA微调在短序列上可能损害性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时间序列基础模型需要GPU基础设施和工程成本，且不一定优于XGBoost等经典方法。目前缺乏系统的盈亏平衡分析来指导 practitioners 何时值得投入这些成本。

Method: 在30个基准数据集上，使用6种训练集比例（2%-100%），对比零样本和LoRA微调的基础模型（Chronos, Moirai, Lag-Llama）与经典基线（Naive, ETS, ARIMA, XGBoost），并通过数据集长度和季节强度两个特征构建两阶段决策框架。

Result: 15/30数据集上基础模型无条件优于经典方法；6/30数据集上经典方法仅需2%数据即超越零样本基础模型；9/30数据集盈亏平衡点在24-8361样本之间。LoRA微调在短序列上可能退化性能。提出的决策规则（n_train<700且季节性强则直接用零样本FM）可立即解决10/30的部署决策。

Conclusion: 基础模型并非总是优于经典方法，部署决策应基于数据集特征。两阶段决策框架可帮助实践者在投入完整基础设施前做出判断，但可靠的全自动预测仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Deploying a time series foundation model requires GPU infrastructure, engineering overhead, and carries no guarantee of improvement over XGBoost. We provide the first systematic break-even analysis answering when this investment pays off. Across 30 benchmark datasets, we compare zero-shot and LoRA fine-tuned foundation models (Chronos, Moirai, Lag-Llama) against classical baselines (Naive, ETS, ARIMA, XGBoost) at six training set sizes from 2% to 100% of available data. Foundation models outperform classical methods at every evaluated training fraction on 15 of 30 datasets -- GPU deployment is unconditionally justified on these regardless of data volume. On 6 datasets, classical methods surpass zero-shot foundation models with as little as 2% of training data (21-2,768 samples); on the remaining 9, break-even ranges from 24 to 8,361 samples. One robust deployment rule requires no model training: if n_train < 700 and seasonality is non-negligible, use FM zero-shot and skip fine-tuning -- this resolves 10 of 30 deployment decisions immediately. Contrary to common practice, LoRA fine-tuning can actively degrade performance on short series. We operationalise these findings as a two-step decision framework -- compute dataset length and seasonality strength, run a brief 5-10% pilot only if needed -- enabling practitioners to make the FM-versus-classical decision before committing to full infrastructure. Four dataset features motivate mechanistic hypotheses for the remaining cases, though reliable automated prediction at this benchmark scale remains an open problem. Code, benchmark, and decision tools are available at https://github.com/nicolaisi/fm-breakeven.

</details>


### [203] [Lightweight ML-Based Automatic Sleep Staging Framework with Constrained CNN and Mamba for Small-Sample EEG Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04934)
*Zihao Wei,Yulin Gong,Yudan Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GamSleepNet轻量级睡眠分期框架，结合改进Gabor核、Mamba架构和对比学习，仅3万参数便在Sleepedf数据集上达87.86%准确率，有效解决小样本过拟合、难分期识别差和部署困难三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动睡眠分期方法存在三个主要问题：参数量大易在小型数据集上过拟合；N1、REM等困难分期识别精度低；难以确定最优训练集大小导致部署困难。需要一种轻量、低延迟且高精度的解决方案。
