Method: 提出GamSleepNet框架，包含三个核心设计：1）FEB模块，将改进的Gabor核与可学习滤波器结合进行特征提取；2）基于Mamba架构构建时序分类网络；3）引入新型对比损失和两阶段训练策略，并通过实验验证单通道EEG睡眠分期模型的最优数据集大小。

Result: 在Sleepedf数据集上，模型仅30.86k参数即达87.86%总体准确率，所有指标均达SOTA水平，显著提升了困难睡眠阶段的识别准确率。

Conclusion: GamSleepNet以极低的参数量实现了优异的睡眠分期性能，同时解决了过拟合、难分期识别和部署困难的问题，为便携式EEG设备的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automatic sleep staging is a key technology for precise diagnosis and treatment of sleep disorders as well as long-term home sleep monitoring. Portable electroencephalogram (EEG) devices have become the focus of research due to their convenience in data collection. However, current methods still face three major challenges: large parameter sizes that easily lead to overfitting on small datasets, low accuracy in classifying difficult stages such as N1 and REM, unclear optimal training dataset size, and difficulty in deployment. This paper proposes GamSleepNet, a lightweight and low-latency automatic sleep staging framework for single-channel EEG. The framework features the FEB module, which combines improved Gabor kernels with learnable filters for feature extraction, uses the Mamba architecture to build a temporal classification network, introduces a novel contrastive loss and a two-stage training strategy, and experimentally validates the optimal dataset size for single-channel EEG sleep staging models. On the Sleepedf dataset, this model achieves an overall accuracy of 87.86 percent with only 30.86 thousand parameters, with all metrics reaching SOTA levels and significantly improving the identification accuracy of challenging sleep stages.

</details>


### [204] [Sensitivity Sampling with Predictions for k-Means Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04949)
*Cristian Boldrin,Fabio Vandin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用预测来近似k-means聚类中敏感性采样的重要性权重，从而在保证理论保证的前提下大幅降低运行时间，特别适用于同分布数据集序列的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于敏感性采样的k-means聚类核心集方法虽然能提供质量保证，但计算每个输入点相对于整个数据集在所有可能中心选择下的重要性（敏感性）是难以精确计算的，现有近似方法的运行时间在大数据集上仍然不切实际。

Method: 首先从理论上证明核心集构建的现有结果在敏感性近似更粗略的情况下仍然成立；然后提出一个自然预测器，利用同分布序列中前一个数据集训练出的低误差中心作为后续数据集的敏感性采样预测；最后通过实验验证。

Result: 理论证明了对敏感性进行更粗略的近似仍能保证核心集质量；实验表明在数据集序列场景下，所提方法在聚类代价与运行时间的权衡上显著优于均匀采样和现有敏感性采样方法。

Conclusion: 利用预测可以显著加速基于敏感性的k-means核心集构建，且理论保证仍然成立，为大规模序列化聚类场景提供了实用的加速方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of k-means clustering on large datasets. The state-of-the-art for the problem is given by coresets-based approaches, which build small weighted summaries of the input and derive approximate solutions with rigorous quality guarantees from them. One of the most popular and advanced approaches to derive coresets for k-means is sensitivity sampling. However, sensitivity sampling requires to compute the importance of each input point with respect to the whole dataset over all possible choices of centers. Since the exact computation of such quantities is unfeasible, current approaches work by approximating the sensitivity values. Nevertheless, the runtime of such approaches is still impractical for large datasets.
  In this work, we propose to reduce the runtime of sensitivity-based approaches for k-means by leveraging predictions to approximate the importance of input points. We first formally prove that current theoretical results on coresets construction via sensitivity sampling hold for coarser approximations of sensitivities compared to the one required by existing approaches. This implies that even fairly noisy predictors can be leveraged for sensitivity-sampling approaches. We then propose a natural predictor, which applies to the common scenario where clustering is performed (over time) on a sequence of datasets from the same problem. We prove that when the datasets in the sequence come from the same (unknown) distribution, centers resulting in a low error on one dataset can be used as predictions for sensitivity sampling in subsequent datasets, with guarantees on their quality. We perform an extensive experimental evaluation showing that our approach significantly improves, in terms of clustering cost vs runtime, over uniform sampling and state-of-the-art sensitivity sampling approaches when applied to sequences of datasets.

</details>


### [205] [Train Smarter, Not Longer: Memorization-Guided Data Reuse for Efficient LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04969)
*Jingwei Zuo,Cong Zeng,Ilyas Chahed,Maksim Velikanov,Dhia Eddine Rhaiem,Pasquale Balsebre,Abhay Kumar,Younes Belkada,Hakim Hacid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"记忆引导的数据重用"训练范式，通过观察模型损失保留动态和下游评估分数中的"记忆窗口"信号，自适应地决定何时及如何重用数据，实验表明在远超常规四轮限制的重复训练中性能仍持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练已从单次训练转向多轮训练，虽然重复使用有限的高质量数据能提升性能和样本效率，但过度重复会导致过拟合和收益递减，现有研究缺乏确定何时以及如何有效重用数据的指导原则。

Method: 通过分析模型损失保留动态（loss retention dynamics）和下游评估分数，提出"记忆窗口"（Memorization Window）信号，据此设计记忆引导的数据重用训练范式，自适应决定训练轮次和数据回放调度。

Result: 初步实验发现存在一致的记忆驱动区间：模型性能在远超当前实践中常见的四轮限制后仍持续提升。

Conclusion: 该发现为记忆感知的训练调度奠定了基础，有助于确定数据重用预算，推动在有限高质量数据下"更聪明而非更长"地训练大语言模型。

Abstract: The training paradigm of large language models has shifted from traditional one-pass training to multi-epoch training, as reasonable reuse of limited high-quality data can improve both model performance and sample efficiency. Meanwhile, excessive repetition introduces the risk of overfitting and diminishing returns. Determining when and how to reuse data effectively thus emerges as a natural but under-explored question. Through a novel observation of model's "Memorization Window" signals derived from loss retention dynamics and downstream evaluation scores, we propose "Memorization-guided Data Reuse", a training paradigm that adaptively determines when and how data should be reused, enabling principled decisions on the number of training epochs and the scheduling of data replays. Our preliminary experiments reveal a consistent memorization-driven regime: performance continues to improve with repetition far beyond current practice (e.g., the commonly cited four-epoch limit). While a full scheduler remains future work, these insights provide a foundation for memorization-aware training schedules, helping to determine reuse budgets and move toward training LLMs smarter rather than longer with limited high-quality data.

</details>


### [206] [Geometry-Aware Bayesian Quantification via Compositional Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04977)
*Alejandro Moreo,Pablo González,Juan José del Coz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Aitchison几何和对数比率表示的核密度估计方法，用于多类标签偏移下的类别 prevalence 估计（量化）。通过对分类器后验概率的几何感知建模，结合收缩正则化和最大似然/贝叶斯推断，在42个数据集上表现出与最先进方法相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于KDE的量化方法使用欧几里得高斯核，忽略了后验向量位于概率单纯形上的组合数据几何结构，将概率质量错误地分配到单纯形边界之外，导致估计不准确。

Method: 提出基于对数比率表示和Aitchison几何的几何感知KDE模型，引入收缩正则化增强边界附近的鲁棒性，结合最大似然解释推导点估计和贝叶斯推断两种类别 prevalence 估计流程。

Result: 在42个涵盖表格、文本和图像领域的数据集上，该方法与最先进的量化方法竞争力强，通常优于标准KDE基线，在贝叶斯量化方法中也表现出色。

Conclusion: 几何感知的KDE模型能有效利用后验概率的单纯形几何结构，提升多类量化任务的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurately estimating the unknown target label distribution is the critical first step for adapting to label shift. This task, widely known as quantification or class prevalence estimation, has recently seen significant advances through continuous KDE-based methods which model the density of multiclass classifier posteriors. Posterior vectors might be regarded as compositional data, since they lie on the probability simplex. However, existing KDE-based quantifiers typically rely on Euclidean Gaussian kernels, which ignore simplex geometry and incorrectly assign probability mass outside its boundaries. We introduce a geometry-aware KDE model for multiclass quantification based on log-ratio representations and Aitchison geometry, together with a shrinkage regularization that improves robustness near the simplex boundary. Combined with a maximum-likelihood interpretation of KDE-based quantification, we derive both point-estimation and Bayesian inference procedures for class prevalences. Experiments on 42 datasets across tabular, text, and image domains show that the proposed method is competitive with state-of-the-art quantifiers, often improving over standard KDE-based baselines, while also yielding strong results among Bayesian quantification methods.

</details>


### [207] [Qantara: Bridge-Flow Training for Multi-Paradigm JEPA Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04978)
*Ruslan Rakhimov,George Bredis,Yuriy Maksyuta,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Qantara 是一种端到端联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），通过布朗桥插值与流匹配的联合训练目标，使同一检查点同时支持潜在规划、行为克隆动作采样和逆动力学三种推理范式，无需重新训练。在 LeWM 控制套件上达到 91.2 SR，在 OGBench-Cube 上创下新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA世界模型在训练时只能承诺单一推理范式（轨迹优化或行为克隆），无法在部署时根据约束（rollout成本、观测可及性）灵活选择。作者希望训练一个检查点同时支持多种推理范式，将选择推迟到推理阶段。

Method: 提出Qantara，其联合训练目标包含两部分：状态轴上对连续干净潜在变量之间的布朗桥插值，以及动作轴上的噪声到数据流匹配。通过视频-逆组合（先预测无动作条件的下一潜在变量，再从中提取动作）实现逆动力学查询。训练时将质量集中在（动作时间，状态时间）噪声平方的边缘区域，以匹配推理时预测器的查询分布。

Result: Push-T规划任务达90.1 SR，替换为均匀内部采样则降至53.3 SR。LeWM控制套件三种子平均91.2 SR。OGBench-Cube上+7.7 SR超过DINO-WM，+19.7 SR超过LeWM。同一权重的行为克隆和视频-逆路径在Push-T上达82-83 SR，Cube上达71-73 SR。

Conclusion: Qantara将JEPA世界模型从单范式规划器推进到多范式控制器，使同一检查点无需重新训练即可灵活切换推理范式。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) underpin a growing family of latent world models for control from raw pixels, but every existing JEPA world model commits at training time to a single inference paradigm: either trajectory optimisation in a learned dynamics model, or direct behaviour cloning. A single checkpoint that serves both would defer this choice to inference, when deployment constraints (rollout cost, observation accessibility) determine which path wins. We present Qantara, an end-to-end JEPA whose joint training objective pairs a Brownian-bridge interpolant between consecutive clean latents on the state axis with noise-to-data flow matching on the action axis. The same checkpoint serves three inference paradigms without retraining: latent planning, behaviour-cloning action sampling, and inverse dynamics, which we query through a video-inverse composition that first predicts the next latent without action conditioning, then extracts the action. Training concentrates mass on the edges of the (action-time, state-time) noise square, where inference queries the predictor: replacing it with uniform interior sampling drops Push-T planning from 90.1 to 53.3 SR at matched compute. On the LeWM control suite, Qantara reaches a 91.2 SR three-train-seed average and sets new SOTA on OGBench-Cube (+7.7 SR over DINO-WM, +19.7 over LeWM). From the same weights, the behaviour-cloning and video-inverse paths reach 82-83 SR on Push-T and 71-73 SR on Cube. These results move JEPA world models from single-paradigm planners to multi-paradigm controllers.

</details>


### [208] [Data-Driven Soft Labeling Scales DNA Read Classification to Whole-Body Cell-Type Deconvolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04987)
*Dmytro Rizdvanetskyi,Nathan Ross,Pavlo Lutsik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于软标签（soft labels）的读段级分类去卷积框架Syto，解决了现有方法在多细胞类型场景下因硬标签冲突和非判别性读段主导导致的收敛问题，在39种人类细胞类型数据集上均方误差降低2.56倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DNA甲基化的细胞类型去卷积方法大多使用聚合的甲基化估计，丢弃了单读段（read-level）的模式信息；而现有的读段级方法仅能处理少数几类细胞类型，无法扩展到大规模类别，因为非判别性读段占主导且硬标签（hard labels）与甲基化模式与细胞类型之间的多对多映射存在冲突，导致分类器无法收敛。

Method: 提出数据驱动的软标签（soft labels）来估计每个读段的条件细胞类型分布，取代传统的硬标签；将该机制整合到Syto框架中，这是一个模块化的读段级分类去卷积框架。

Result: 在包含39种人类细胞类型的全身图谱上，Syto的均方误差比现有最优方法降低2.56倍；该性能优势可迁移至包含16个组织的分布外数据集。

Conclusion: Syto为建模日益庞大的细胞类型面板奠定了基础，在生物学和医疗健康中有改进的应用前景；所提出的软标签方案还可推广至任何具有多对多信号到标签映射的场景。

Abstract: Cell-type deconvolution, the task of estimating the proportions of constituent cell types in a heterogeneous biological sample, is a core problem in computational biology. Methods that rely on epigenetic marks such as DNA methylation typically operate on aggregated methylation estimates, discarding the pattern-level information carried by individual DNA reads. Existing read-level approaches that exploit this information are scarce, and all remain restricted to few-class settings; scaling them further is an open problem because, at scale, non-discriminative reads dominate and hard labels conflict with the many-to-many mapping between methylation patterns and cell types, preventing classifier convergence. To overcome this, we propose data-driven soft labels that estimate the conditional cell-type distribution for each read, and integrate this scheme into Syto, a new modular framework for read-level classification-based deconvolution. On a whole-body atlas of 39 human cell types, Syto reduces MSE by 2.56$\times$ over SoTA, with gains transferring to an out-of-distribution dataset spanning 16 tissues. Syto lays the foundation for modeling increasingly large cell-type panels, with improved applications in biology and healthcare. The proposed soft-labeling scheme is further translatable to any setting with a many-to-many signal-to-label mapping.

</details>


### [209] [Non-Convex Sparse Reinforcement Learning via Non-Monotone Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04990)
*Kyohei Suzuki,onstantinos Slavakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将非凸PMC惩罚项引入LSTD策略评估以改进强化学习中的特征选择，并发展了前向-反射-后向分裂（FRBS）方法在非单调包含问题上的收敛理论，实验表明该方法在含噪特征多的情况下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正则化方法在强化学习特征选择中存在估计偏差，且常规凸正则化无法有效处理噪声特征带来的稀疏性问题。

Method: 在LSTD策略评估中引入弱凸的非凸PMC惩罚项，将原单调不动点问题转化为更广泛的非单调包含问题（单调Lipschitz算子与次单调算子之和）；采用FRBS方法求解，并发展该方法的收敛条件。

Result: 在弱Minty变分不等式假设下建立了FRBS迭代的精确收敛性，在更温和条件下证明了Lyapunov稳定性和极限点存在性；基准数据集上的数值测试表明所提方法在含噪特征场景下显著优于现有特征选择方法。

Conclusion: 非凸正则化结合FRBS求解为RL特征选择提供了有效方案，同时扩展了非单调包含问题的可求解范围。

Abstract: This work delivers two key contributions: one to efficient feature selection in reinforcement learning
  (RL), the other to the theory of non-monotone inclusions. On the RL side, the estimation bias inherent
  in conventional regularization schemes is addressed by augmenting classical least-squares
  temporal-difference (LSTD) policy evaluation with the sparsity-inducing, non-convex projected minimax
  concave (PMC) penalty. Because the PMC penalty is weakly convex, the resulting fixed-point problem is
  no longer monotone; instead, it falls under a broader class of non-monotone inclusions involving the
  sum of a monotone Lipschitz operator and a hypomonotone operator. On the theory side, novel convergence
  conditions are developed for the forward-reflected-backward splitting (FRBS) method applied to this
  broader class of non-monotone inclusion problems. Under mild conditions, Lyapunov stability and the
  existence of a limit point of the sequence of FRBS iterates are established; alternatively, under the
  weak Minty variational inequality assumption, exact convergence is guaranteed. Numerical tests on
  benchmark datasets show that the proposed FRBS iterates, applied to the non-convexly regularized LSTD
  problem, substantially outperform state-of-the-art feature-selection methods, especially when many
  noisy features are present.

</details>


### [210] [Beyond Modality Fusion: Deep Ensembles for Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05019)
*Ilya Burenko,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在多模态分类中，无需显式融合，仅使用单模态网络的深度集成即可取得优于现有晚期融合方法的效果。作者提出了选择每模态模型数量的启发式方法，并在极端模态不平衡等场景下验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晚期融合方法在多模态不平衡时表现不佳，需依赖正则化等技术平衡学习过程。本文旨在探索是否可以不通过显式模态融合，仅用单模态网络的集成来实现有效分类。

Method: 系统比较了深度集成与晚期融合网络（参数数相同）；提出了一种启发式方法选择每模态模型数量，避免穷举搜索；构建了可控制模态数量和预测强度的合成多模态框架，用于系统分析优化挑战；拟合标度律估计集成的渐近性能。

Result: 深度集成在同等参数下持续优于最先进的晚期融合方法及中间融合方法；极端不平衡时，小规模集成仅用强模态模型更优，大规模集成则加入弱模态模型有益；合成与真实数据集结果一致。

Conclusion: 无需显式模态融合，深度集成可作为多模态分类的有效替代方案；提出的启发式方法能高效指导集成组成选择。

Abstract: In multimodal classification, late-fusion approaches classify concatenated modality-specific features extracted by unimodal neural networks.
  When modality imbalance is pronounced, various regularization techniques have been proposed to balance the learning process and overcome the inferior performance of late-fusion networks.
  In contrast, this work demonstrates that multimodal data can be effectively classified without any explicit modality fusion, using deep ensembles of unimodal networks.
  We systematically compare deep ensembles to late-fusion networks at equal parameter count and show that ensembles consistently outperform state-of-the-art late-fusion methods designed to address modality imbalance.
  This advantage also holds over intermediate-fusion techniques we evaluated and over hybrid methods that combine unimodal and multimodal predictions.
  We propose and empirically validate a method for selecting the number of models per modality in an ensemble, avoiding computationally expensive exhaustive search.
  Under extreme modality imbalance and small ensemble sizes, the heuristic indicates that ensembles of unimodal models trained solely on the stronger modality are preferable; as the ensemble scales up, incorporating models from the weaker modality becomes beneficial.
  Both predictions align with our empirical findings.
  To systematically explore the challenges of optimizing multimodal models, we propose a synthetic multimodal framework that allows control over both the number of modalities and their predictive strength; our findings are consistent across synthetic and real-world datasets.
  Finally, by fitting scaling laws to bimodal datasets, we estimate the asymptotic performance of ensembles.

</details>


### [211] [Uncertainty-aware damage identification in short-span bridges via physics-informed variational autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05025)
*Ana Fernandez Navamuel,A. Javier Omella,Diego Zamora-Sanchez,David Pardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息高斯Copula变分自编码器（PI-GCVAE）框架，通过将可微特征值求解器嵌入VAE、用高斯Copula替代独立潜变量假设，实现了结构健康监测中具有不确定性量化的损伤识别，在合成简支梁桥数据上达到77.2%的后验覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于振动的损伤识别是病态逆问题，受噪声、稀疏传感器和环境变化影响；确定性深度学习方法缺乏可靠的不确定性量化且可能产生物理不一致的结果。

Method: 1) 将可微数值特征值求解器直接嵌入VAE架构，确保潜变量满足结构动力学控制方程；2) 用高斯Copula替代独立潜变量假设，建模相邻结构单元间物理相关的空间互相关性；3) 在合成简支梁桥数据集上验证，加入随机高斯噪声模拟实际场景。

Result: PI-GCVAE准确恢复了真实后验分布，实现了77.2%的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该框架为运营桥梁的早期损伤诊断提供了一种可靠、可扩展的工具，兼具物理一致性与不确定性量化能力。

Abstract: Vibration-based damage identification in civil infrastructure is a challenging, ill-posed inverse problem due to measurement noise, sparse sensor arrays, and environmental variability. While deep learning is powerful for system identification, deterministic approaches lack reliable uncertainty quantification and can yield physically inconsistent results. This work proposes a robust probabilistic Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) framework: a physics-informed Gaussian copula variational autoencoder (PI-GCVAE) for structural health monitoring (SHM). First, we eliminate the need for data-driven surrogates by embedding a differentiable numerical eigenvalue solver directly into the VAE architecture. This ensures that latent space samples satisfy the governing equations of structural dynamics, reducing the trainable parameter space and improving generalization. Second, we replace the conventional independence assumption of latent variables with a Gaussian copula. This model captures complex, physics-dependent spatial cross-correlations between adjacent structural elements, defining feasible solutions while accounting for inherent system variability and measurement errors. Third, compared with alternatives such as Gaussian mixtures, our copula-based VAE provides an efficient distributional model for high-dimensional, strongly correlated latent spaces. We validate the approach using a synthetic dataset of a simply supported bridge subjected to various damage scenarios and corrupted with stochastic Gaussian noise. Synthetic data enables exhaustive validation against ground-truth stiffness values unavailable in practice. Results demonstrate that the PI-GCVAE accurately recovers the true posterior distribution, achieving 77.2% coverage. The proposed framework provides a reliable, scalable tool for early-stage damage diagnosis in operating bridges.

</details>


### [212] [CollabEval: Statistically Efficient Collaborative Model Evaluation via Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05046)
*Adam Fisch,Daniel Deutsch,Joshua Maynez,Alekh Agarwal,Jonathan Berant,William Cohen,Amir Globerson,Jacob Eisenstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CollabEval方法，通过将模型评估建模为矩阵补全问题，利用历史运行结果间的依赖关系，在少量标注下实现无偏估计和有效置信区间，显著提升统计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型的评估在开发过程中需要反复进行，但这一过程资源消耗巨大，现有方法未充分利用不同模型在同一任务上历史运行结果之间的依赖关系来提升统计效率。

Method: 将模型评估建模为M×N评分矩阵的补全问题（M为模型数，N为评估提示数），对目标模型仅标注p比例的数据；利用预测驱动推断（prediction-powered inference）构建低秩近似矩阵，将重构值作为控制变量，保证评估指标均值的无偏估计和统计有效的置信区间。

Result: 在多个数据集、模型和稀疏度p下，CollabEval相比基线方法显著降低了平均置信区间大小和点估计的均方误差。

Conclusion: CollabEval是一种简单、有效且原理严谨的方法，能在相同标注预算下大幅提升模型评估的统计效率。

Abstract: Evaluating generative AI models is a routine, but resource-intensive, process that is conducted over and over again during the course of model development. In this work, we propose Collaborative Evaluation (CollabEval), a simple, effective, and principled method for exploiting dependencies between historical runs of different models on the same tasks to improve statistical efficiency. Specifically, our approach treats model evaluation as a matrix completion problem over an $M \times N$ matrix of evaluation scores, where $M$ is the total number of models and $N$ is the total number of evaluation prompts. We assume that a subset of these $M$ models are targeted for evaluation. For these target models only a small fraction, $p$, of prompts has been annotated with evaluation scores. Leveraging recent results in prediction-powered inference, we build a low-rank approximation of the score matrix, and use the reconstructed values as control variates in a manner that guarantees unbiased estimates of the true evaluation metric mean, in addition to statistically valid confidence intervals. Empirically, across a wide range of datasets, models, and sparsity levels $p$, we find that CollabEval substantially reduces the mean confidence interval size, and the mean squared error of the point estimate, compared to baseline methods at the same annotation budget.

</details>


### [213] [KVpop -- Key-Value Cache Compression with Predictive Online Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05061)
*Lukas Hauzenberger,Niklas Schmidinger,Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,David Stap,Thomas Schmied,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KVpop提出了一种基于未来注意力监督的学习型KV缓存淘汰策略，通过直接监督保留/丢弃决策，在75%和88%压缩率下分别保留98%和97%的全注意力性能，显著优于现有启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码中KV缓存持续增长导致内存和带宽开销随上下文长度线性扩展，现有淘汰方法依赖静态启发式或代理分数，难以准确预测未来token的效用，在相关性变化时表现脆弱。

Method: 1) 学习固定预算的KV淘汰策略，直接监督保留-丢弃决策；2) 基于未来注意力目标训练评分器，无需实例化密集注意力图即可高效计算；3) 引入延迟记忆评分器，在固定步数后评分以利用近期上下文信息。

Result: 在AIME和HMMT数学推理任务上，Qwen3-4B在75%压缩率下保持98%全注意力性能，88%压缩率下保持97%；Qwen3-8B达到接近全注意力教师性能。

Conclusion: 通过未来注意力信号监督KV淘汰可以有效降低内存成本同时维持生成质量，学习型方法优于传统启发式淘汰基线。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache growth is a major bottleneck in autoregressive decoding, as memory and bandwidth scale linearly with context length. Existing KV eviction methods often rely on static heuristics or proxy scores, which poorly track future token utility and cause brittle eviction as relevance shifts. To address this, we introduce KVpop, which learns a fixed-budget KV eviction policy by directly supervising the keep-or-drop decision. The scorer is trained against a novel future-attention target, computed efficiently without materializing dense attention maps. We further introduce a delayed memory-based scorer that, uniquely among learned eviction methods, defers scoring for a fixed number of steps to exploit near-future context. On AIME and HMMT mathematical reasoning, KVpop retains 98% of full-attention performance on Qwen3-4B at 75% KV cache compression and 97% at 88% compression, consistently outperforming established eviction baselines. Qwen3-8B shows even stronger results, reaching near-full teacher performance. These results show that supervising eviction with future-attention signals cuts memory costs while maintaining quality.

</details>


### [214] [Computing Monetary Risk Measures in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05078)
*Palash Agrawal,Gersi Doko,Maeve Burwell,Marek Petrik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了QuickVaR和QuickDivergence两种新算法，分别用于在期望线性时间内计算离散随机变量的VaR和φ-散度风险度量（包括CVaR），相比现有方法在大规模域上可实现数量级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VaR和CVaR等风险度量计算算法在大规模离散随机变量域上效率不足，亟需更高效的算法来满足实际应用中对风险度量的快速计算需求。

Method: QuickVaR算法改进了经典Quickselect算法来计算离散随机变量的VaR；QuickDivergence算法则基于QuickVaR并结合拟阵优化（polymatroid optimization）算法，用于计算包括CVaR在内的φ-散度风险度量。

Result: 数值实验表明，新算法在大规模域上相比现有方法实现了数量级的加速，并提供开源实现。

Conclusion: 所提出的QuickVaR和QuickDivergence算法能够高效计算离散随机变量的VaR和φ-散度风险度量，显著提升了计算效率，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Monetary risk measures have gained popularity for expressing decision-makers' risk aversion. Value-at-Risk (VaR) and Conditional-Value-at-Risk (CVaR), in particular, are used commonly for this purpose. This paper proposes new efficient algorithms to compute these risk measures for a discrete random variable in expected linear time with respect to the size of its domain. First, we propose a QuickVaR algorithm that computes the VaR of a discrete random variable. Then, we leverage QuickVaR to propose QuickDivergence, an algorithm for computing a class of $\varphi$-divergence risk measures, including the popular CVaR risk measure. The QuickVaR algorithm adapts the well-known Quickselect algorithm, while QuickDivergence builds on polymatroid optimization algorithms. Numerical results show that our new algorithms offer an order-of-magnitude speedup for large domains, and a library implementation of the algorithms is available at https://github.com/RiskAverseRL/RiskMeasures.jl.

</details>


### [215] [FAST: A Holistic Framework for Optimizing Memory-I/O, Computation, and Sampling in Temporal GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05095)
*Yushu Cai,Qingrui Zhu,Lei Liu,Kai Sheng,Hao Chen,Xin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FAST是一个端到端加速TGNN训练的统一框架，通过联合优化采样、内存I/O和计算，在大型动态图上实现了平均2.1倍（最高4.7倍）的加速，且不牺牲模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TGNN训练系统面临内存I/O、不规则计算和时序邻居采样三个相互交织的性能瓶颈，且现有系统孤立优化各阶段，导致性能提升有限。

Method: 提出SlimCache（批内压缩+跨批缓存减少数据搬运）、线程高效的图算子（提升GPU缓存局部性）、拓扑感知采样策略（提升CPU缓存局部性）三方面联合优化。

Result: 在真实大规模动态图上，平均2.1倍加速，最高4.7倍加速，且不损失模型精度。

Conclusion: FAST通过全局联合优化而非孤立优化各阶段，能有效突破TGNN训练的性能瓶颈，实现显著的端到端加速。

Abstract: Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNNs) are widely used for learning from dynamic graphs in applications such as recommendation, social network analysis, and traffic forecasting. However, scaling TGNN training to large dynamic graphs remains challenging due to three intertwined bottlenecks: memory I/O, irregular computation, and temporal neighbor sampling. Existing systems often optimize these stages in isolation, leaving substantial performance headroom on the table. We present FAST, a holistic framework that accelerates end-to-end TGNN training by jointly optimizing sampling, memory I/O, and computation. FAST introduces SlimCache, which exploits within-batch compression and cross-batch caching to reduce host-device data movement under limited GPU memory budgets. It further designs thread-efficient graph operators tailored to sparse temporal subgraphs, improving GPU cache locality and reducing the latency of aggregation and edge softmax. In addition, FAST employs a topology-aware sampling strategy that improves CPU cache locality and accelerates temporal neighbor sampling. Extensive experiments on real-world large dynamic graphs show that FAST achieves an average of 2.1x (up to 4.7x) speedup over state-of-the-art systems without sacrificing model accuracy.

</details>


### [216] [Functional Bilevel Optimization for Predictive Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05098)
*Ieva Petrulionyte,Julien Mairal,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对连续高维敏感属性的均值群体公平性（DPVar）问题，提出了两种算法（FBO和ITD），在60个表格回归数据集上实现了最低或接近最低的公平-准确权衡遗憾，优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当敏感属性是连续且高维的（如人口统计评分向量、属性后验、年龄或收入分布）时，强制完全的统计独立性往往过于严格，而现有的松弛方法依赖于间接的依赖性惩罚或对抗机制，不能直接针对公平-准确权衡进行优化。

Method: 通过DPVar（给定敏感属性条件均值预测的方差）来定义均值群体公平性，将其优化转化为函数双层问题。提出了两种算法：FBO（利用平方损失情况的闭形式伴随矩阵获得精确超梯度）和ITD（通过展开内部步骤进行微分，可扩展到平方损失之外）。

Result: 在合成数据和基于60个表格回归数据集构建的半合成基准上，两种方法均实现了最低或接近最低的聚合公平-准确遗憾，一致优于或匹配HSIC、对抗性、线性依赖和广义DP等基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的DPVar优化方法能有效处理连续高维敏感属性的公平性约束，在保持高准确性的同时实现更好的公平性，且FBO和ITD两种算法在不同场景下各有优势。

Abstract: When sensitive attributes are continuous and high-dimensional $-$ demographic score vectors, posteriors over attributes, age or income profiles $-$ enforcing full statistical independence is often too restrictive, and existing relaxations rely on indirect dependence penalties or adversarial schemes that do not directly target the fairness-accuracy trade-off. We instead consider mean demographic parity through DPVar, the variance of the conditional-mean prediction given the sensitive attribute, and show that optimizing it yields a functional bilevel problem. We propose two algorithms for this problem: FBO, which uses a closed-form adjoint we derive for the squared-loss case to obtain an exact hypergradient, and ITD, which differentiates through unrolled inner steps and extends beyond squared loss. On synthetic data and a new semi-synthetic benchmark built from 60 tabular regression datasets, both methods achieve the lowest or near-lowest aggregate fairness-accuracy regret, and consistently match or outperform strong HSIC, adversarial, linear-dependence, and generalized-DP baselines.

</details>


### [217] [Counterfactual Methods for Detecting Unfairness in Anti-Money Laundering Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05101)
*Lea Multerer,Michele Inchingolo,David Kletz,Adrian Cosma,Alessandro Antonucci,Martina Gogova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了反洗钱（AML）系统中机器学习模型的公平性问题，发现基于反事实路径分析，模型预测性能的提升往往伴随着更严重的公平性违规，揭示了预测精度与公平性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AML系统中广泛使用基于敏感客户信息的机器学习算法，但这类系统的公平性问题尚未得到充分研究，现有反事实分析方法也未在该领域得到应用。

Method: 使用IBM AMLSim合成交易数据集，构建账户所在国家和平均行为等额外特征，训练从基础决策树到图神经网络的多种模型，并通过反事实路径特定效应分析评估公平性。

Result: 扩展特征提升了模型预测性能，但性能提升越大的模型其公平性违规越严重，二者之间存在明显的权衡关系。

Conclusion: AML领域亟需系统性的公平性分析，因为预测精度与公平性之间存在实质性冲突，尤其是在引入额外敏感特征时。

Abstract: The application of machine learning-based predictive algorithms to Anti-Money Laundering (AML) has grown rapidly, driven by the vast volume of financial transaction data available to banks. These algorithms are typically trained not only on transactional data but also on sensitive client information, which may raise fairness concerns. Despite this, AML detection systems remain largely underexplored from a fairness perspective, even though deeper analytical methods based on counterfactuals are now available. Such techniques enable the decomposition of the direct and indirect effects of potentially sensitive features on model predictions, thereby supporting the evaluation of whether their influence is acceptable from a fairness perspective. Closing this gap, we consider the synthetic IBM AMLSim transaction dataset and construct additional features of the country of an account and its average behaviour. This improves the predictive performance of diverse machine learning models, ranging from baseline decision trees to state-of-the-art graph neural networks. We assess the potential unfairness associated with these features through a counterfactual, path-specific effect analysis. This reveals that fairness violations tend to be more pronounced for models whose predictive performance benefits the most from the extended features. Such a finding highlights a concrete instance of the trade-off between predictive accuracy and fairness in AML applications, thus underscoring the urgency of a systematic fairness analysis in such critical domains.

</details>


### [218] [Choosing a parallel heterogeneous ensemble method for tabular classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05103)
*Vassili Maillet,Gustavo,Angulo,Pierre Jouvelot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在56个OpenML CC18表格分类任务上比较了并行集成方法，基于观察得出一套"最佳实践"建议，并在28个额外任务中验证，该建议显著优于单一最佳模型且不逊于单个集成方法。两个关键发现：Blending和Stacking表现不一致但相互独立；Hard Voting概率分类较弱，而Robust Soft Voting在多分类任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为并行集成方法在小到中型表格分类任务上提供系统性的实验比较和最佳实践建议，填补该领域缺乏统一指导的空白。

Method: 在56个OpenML CC18的小到中型表格分类任务上对比多种并行集成方法，基于观察结果提炼最佳实践建议，并在28个额外任务（使用TabArena预计算数据）上验证建议的有效性。

Result: 1) Blending和Stacking各自在不同任务上表现不一致，且两者不一致性相互独立；2) Hard Voting因使用投票比例作为后验概率估计导致概率分类较弱，而Robust Soft Voting在多分类场景下概率分类表现尤为成功；3) 所提最佳实践建议显著优于单一最佳模型，与个体集成方法持平或更优。

Conclusion: 提出了一套经过验证的并行集成方法最佳实践建议，揭示了不同集成策略的互补特性和概率估计的优劣差异。

Abstract: Parallel ensemble methods were compared on $56$ small-to-medium tabular classification tasks drawn from OpenML CC18. A set of ``best practice'' recommendations on the use of ensemble methods was derived from these observations. It was later validated on 28 additional tasks using TabArena's precomputed data, where the recommendation set significantly outperformed Single Best and matched or exceeded individual ensemble methods.
  Two key observations were made. First, Blending and Stacking are inconsistent, but their inconsistencies are independent and happen on different tasks. Second, while Hard Voting's probabilistic classification is rather weak, a consequence of using vote proportions as posterior estimates, Robust Soft Voting's probabilistic classification is particularly successful, especially in the multiclass case.

</details>


### [219] [Physiological Noise Augmentation Improves Non-Invasive Brain-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05165)
*Benjamin Ballyk,Teyun Kwon,Miran Özdogan,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生理噪声增强（PNA）方法，通过ICA分解脑记录为干净数据与噪声伪迹，再缩放混合生成生物物理真实、保留标签的训练样本，使解码器对任务无关伪迹（如眼动、心跳）具有不变性。在MegNIST数据集上，结合10次试验平均，EEGNet解码准确率绝对提升4.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MEG和EEG的非侵入式脑到语音解码方法，因信号噪声比低导致词错误率高，亟需提升对任务无关伪迹的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出生理噪声增强（PNA）：使用独立成分分析（ICA）将脑记录分解为干净数据和噪声伪迹，通过缩放和重新混合生成生物物理真实、保留标签的训练样本，近似各向异性正则化，惩罚沿伪迹主导方向的解码器敏感性。

Result: 在MegNIST 12k试次想象数字MEG数据集上，PNA结合10次试验平均使EEGNet解码准确率绝对提升4.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 伪迹感知增强与试验平均是提升非侵入式语音BCI鲁棒性的互补工具。

Abstract: Non-invasive brain-to-speech decoding aims to restore communication to patients suffering from neurodegenerative disease, without the risks of neurosurgery. Existing MEG- and EEG-based methods, while scalable, continue to suffer from high word error rates driven by relatively low signal-to-noise ratios compared to invasive recordings. We propose physiological noise augmentation (PNA), a data augmentation method that explicitly trains decoders to become invariant to task-agnostic artifacts (e.g. ocular and cardiac activity). PNA draws inspiration from automatic speech recognition systems, where environmental noise (e.g. dogs barking, city traffic) is added to clean speech to improve robustness. Analogously, we decompose brain recordings into clean data and noise artifacts using independent component analysis (ICA), before scaling and remixing to generate biophysically realistic, label-preserving training examples. We show that PNA approximates anisotropic regularization, penalizing decoder sensitivity along artifact-dominated directions. On MegNIST, a 12k-trial imagined-digit MEG dataset, PNA with 10-trial averaging improves EEGNet decoding accuracy by 4.7 percentage points (absolute) over training on real data alone. Our results suggest that artifact-aware augmentation and trial averaging are complementary tools for improving robustness in non-invasive speech BCIs.

</details>


### [220] [MeGA-MP: Metric Graph Advection Message Passing -- A Physics-Informed Message Passing Operator for Advection-Dominated Metric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05167)
*Janine Strotherm,Luca Hermes,André Artelt,Barbara Hammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息消息传递算子，将度量图上的线性平流作为归纳偏置编码，在纯平流设置中无需训练即可恢复精确动力学，并扩展到平流-反应动力学，在不同图拓扑上实现零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多真实世界系统（如水网、电网、交通流）本质上是度量图上的时空动力学系统，传统PINN和算子学习方法受限于全局欧几里得空间假设，且平流等传输动力学需要处理图上反对称和长程依赖关系，现有方法难以直接适用。

Method: 提出一种新颖的物理信息消息传递算子，将线性平流在度量图上的动力学方程编码为归纳偏置；纯平流场景下推导了理论离散误差，无需训练即可精确恢复动力学；结合可训练MLP组件，扩展至平流-反应动力学场景。

Result: 在纯平流场景下，无训练即可精确恢复动力学；在配水系统的平流-反应动力学任务中，性能优于基线方法，并能零样本泛化到不同图拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 提出的物理信息消息传递算子有效解决了度量图上平流动力学的建模挑战，兼具理论保证和实用泛化能力，为图上的物理信息学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Many real-world systems are organized as networks where spatio-temporal dynamics unfold along connections and not discretely between nodes. Examples include utility networks such as water distribution systems or gas networks, electrical grids, and traffic flow networks. Such systems are naturally modeled as metric graphs, where edges correspond to one-dimensional Euclidean subspaces connected at vertices. Metric graphs are independent of an underlying global Euclidean space, limiting direct application of typical PINNs and operator-learning methods. Especially transport dynamics like advection require a methodology able to capture antisymmetric and long-range dependencies on graphs, which is itself a challenge. We propose a novel physics-informed message passing operator that encodes linear advection on metric graphs as an inductive bias. In the purely advective setting, the operator provably recovers the exact dynamics up to a theoretically derived discretization error without any training. Combined with trainable components like MLPs, our message passing operator extends to realistic advection-reaction dynamics in water distribution systems, where we achieve superior performance compared to baselines and zero-shot generalization across different graph topologies.

</details>


### [221] [Platonic Projection Structures: Operator-Induced Observability in Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05175)
*Kazuo Ishii,Bishnu Prasad Gautam,Jieling Wu,Javaid Saher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出柏拉图投影结构（PPS），一个基于算子理论的框架，通过自伴半正定观测算子和商几何来表征表示学习中的可观性，揭示观测等价类、量子测量的结构对应性，以及输出可解释性的固有限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习方法缺乏一个统一的数学框架来刻画在部分观测条件下潜在表示的可访问性（observability），尤其是在可解释性、知识蒸馏和表示迁移等任务中，哪些表示成分可以被观测到是一个核心但未被充分形式化的问题。

Method: 提出柏拉图投影结构（PPS），将系统建模为三元组$(H, Π, O)$，其中$H$为潜在表示空间，$Π\succeq 0$为自伴半正定观测算子，$O(v)=\langle v,Πv\rangle$定义诱导标量可观测量。可观性通过商几何$H/\ker(Π)$刻画，即核空间中的不可区分等价类。进一步将表示迁移和知识蒸馏建模为$ΦΠ_T \approx Π_S Φ$的近似保存关系。

Result: 通过控制实验验证了核不变可观性、投影诱导的归因缺口以及秩控制的可观几何。揭示了输出可解释性的结构局限：$ker(Π)$中的潜在成分无法从诱导可观测量中访问，对归因和解释方法施加了内在约束。同时表明量子测量和线性观测模型下的表示推断共享相同的算子理论结构，区别仅在于观测算子的代数性质不同。

Conclusion: PPS提供了一个通过算子诱导商几何显式刻画可观性的统一框架，为表示可访问性、可解释性和投影介导推断提供了理论视角。

Abstract: We characterize observability in representation learning through Platonic Projection Structures (PPS), an operator-theoretic framework for analyzing representation accessibility under partial observation. Rather than treating observable outputs as direct reflections of latent representations, PPS models observation through a self-adjoint positive semidefinite operator acting on a latent representation space. A system is represented as a triple $(H, Π, O)$, where $H$ is a latent representation space, $Π\succeq 0$ is an observation operator, and $O(v)=\langle v,Πv\rangle$ defines an induced scalar observable. Observability is characterized by the quotient geometry $H/\ker(Π)$, representing equivalence classes of latent states indistinguishable under observation. We show that quantum measurement and representation inference under linear observation models share this operator-theoretic structure while differing in the algebraic properties of their observation operators; the correspondence is structural rather than physical. Representation transfer and knowledge distillation can likewise be interpreted as approximate preservation of observable geometry through $ΦΠ_T \approx Π_S Φ$. PPS also reveals a structural limitation of output-based interpretability: latent components in $\ker(Π)$ are inaccessible from induced observables, imposing intrinsic constraints on attribution and explanation methods. Controlled empirical validations demonstrate kernel-invariant observability, projection-induced attribution gaps, and rank-controlled observable geometry in latent representation spaces. PPS thus provides an explicit characterization of observability through operator-induced quotient geometry and a unified perspective on representation accessibility, interpretability, and projection-mediated inference.

</details>


### [222] [SMART: A Machine Learning and Monte Carlo Framework for Rapid Analysis of Stochastic Transistor Aging and Process Variation in Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05187)
*Arash Esshaghi,Siavash Es'haghi,Gholamreza Shahabadi,Alireza Moradi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对纳米尺度下BTI和工艺变化导致的电路可靠性分析效率瓶颈，提出SMART框架，结合机器学习（随机森林回归）与蒙特卡洛仿真，通过贝叶斯优化自动调参，在ISCAS85基准测试中实现分析时间减少94.54%，平均精度误差仅1.63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMOS技术进入深纳米时代后，BTI和工艺变化的随机效应严重威胁数字电路可靠性；传统基于密集仿真或查找表的分析方法在大规模设计中效率低下，成为设计空间探索的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出SMART框架：1）采用随机森林回归直接预测门延迟分布，绕过耗时的原子模型参数提取；2）结合蒙特卡洛仿真进行快速高保真可靠性分析；3）利用贝叶斯优化自动调整超参数，确保跨不同工艺库的预测鲁棒性。

Result: 在ISCAS85基准电路上，相比最先进方法，分析时间降低94.54%，平均精度误差仅1.63%。

Conclusion: 通过将计算复杂度转移到离线训练阶段，SMART为设计抗老化、高可靠性的数字系统提供了可扩展且精确的解决方案。

Abstract: As CMOS technology scales into the deep nanometer regime, digital circuit reliability is increasingly threatened by the combined stochastic effects of Bias Temperature Instability (BTI) and Process Variation (PV). Traditional reliability analysis methods, which rely on computationally intensive simulations or extensive lookup tables, fail to scale efficiently for large designs, creating a critical bottleneck in design space exploration. To address this, we propose SMART, a novel framework that integrates Machine Learning (ML) with Monte Carlo simulation to enable rapid, high-fidelity reliability analysis. SMART employs Random Forest regression to predict gate delay distributions directly, bypassing time-consuming atomic model parameter extractions. Crucially, the model utilizes Bayesian Optimization for automated hyperparameter tuning, ensuring maximum predictive robustness across diverse libraries. Experimental validation on ISCAS85 benchmark circuits demonstrates that SMART achieves a 94.54% reduction in analysis time compared to state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining a remarkable average accuracy error of just 1.63%. By shifting computational complexity to an offline training phase, the proposed framework offers a scalable, accurate solution for designing resilient, reliability-aware digital systems.

</details>


### [223] [Latent Programming Horizons in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05188)
*André Silva,Han Tu,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现编码智能体（coding agent）中语言模型的残差流能线性编码程序属性（如是否能通过测试、是否引入回归），且这些表征能“超前预测”智能体未来约25步内的编辑结果，作者称之为“潜在编程视界”（latent programming horizon）。探针无需重新训练即可跨基准迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在执行软件工程任务时，会经历多步推理、编辑和测试，但人们对其内部语言模型如何表征正在演化的程序几乎一无所知。本文旨在探究LM在编码智能体场景下，其残差流是否编码了关于程序状态的结构化信息。

Method: 使用逻辑回归探针（logistic-regression probe）在语言模型的隐藏状态（hidden states）上进行解码，预测程序是否可解析、是否通过测试套件、失败测试数是否减少以及是否引入回归。实验覆盖两个模型和两个基准。此外，训练探针预测尚未实施的未来编辑结果，衡量“潜在编程视界”。

Result: 探针在正确性预测上AUC可达0.83；对未来编辑结果的预测在约25步内优于随机水平；探针无需重新训练即可跨基准迁移。

Conclusion: 语言模型的残差流中编码了关于演化程序的结构化信息，且这些表征具有前瞻性。该发现为编码智能体的机械可解释性研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: A coding agent solving a software-engineering task spends dozens of steps reasoning, editing code, and running tests, yet little is known about what the underlying language model internally represents about the program it is working on. We show that the residual streams of language models under coding agents linearly encode properties of the evolving program: a logistic-regression probe on hidden states is able to decode whether the current code parses, passes its test suite, reduces the number of failing tests, and introduces regressions, reaching AUC up to 0.83 for correctness across two models and two benchmarks. Our second finding is more surprising: these representations run ahead of the agent's own edits. Probes trained to predict the outcome of future edits (before they are materialized and written on disk) achieve performance above chance up to roughly 25 steps in advance. We call this the agent's latent programming horizon. As a proof of external validity, we show that the probes transfer across benchmarks without retraining. Our positive results open calls for more research in mechanistic interpretability of coding agents.

</details>


### [224] [FlatManifold: Robust Continual Learning under Severe Label Noise and Domain Shifts via Intrinsic Manifold Flattening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05201)
*Rai Hisada,Kanji Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlatManifold框架，利用Nyström流形展平映射和正交化RKHS投影，在非平稳流式环境中同时应对持续学习中的灾难性遗忘和严重标签噪声问题，通过结构线性化本身作为数学屏障抵御分布式标签 corruption。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平稳流式环境中，同时适应复杂的非线性域偏移（持续学习）并缓解严重、未校准的标签噪声带来的灾难性影响，是一个根本性的数学挑战。现有依赖于复杂、易出错的样本过滤管道的方法效果有限。

Method: 提出FlatManifold框架，采用基于核技巧的Nyström流形展平映射，将特征分布投影到正交化RKHS中的固定正交目标拓扑上，结合岭正则化器；同时引入利用过去经验协方差矩阵的持续拓扑刹车项来防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在真实世界多会话机器人数据集上的广泛评估表明，即使在40%对称标签噪声的严重条件下，FlatManifold成功缓解了梯度污染；在跨会话极端域偏移（不同季节和光照条件）下，该框架建立了高泛化能力，显著优于标准顺序优化基线。

Conclusion: 结构线性化本身可作为抵御分布式标签污染的强有力数学屏障，证明了在非平稳流式环境中通过数学变换而非复杂过滤管道来同时解决持续学习和标签噪声问题的可行性。

Abstract: In non-stationary streaming environments, simultaneously adapting to complex, non-linear domain shifts via continual learning while mitigating the catastrophic effects of severe, uncalibrated label noise poses a fundamental mathematical challenge. In this paper, we propose \FlatManifold{}, a novel, streamlined robust continual learning framework that utilizes a Nyström manifold flattening map based on the kernel trick and projection onto an orthogonalized Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS).
  Unlike traditional methods that rely on complex, error-prone sample-filtering pipelines, the proposed approach exploits the intrinsic mathematical robustness of the flattened space itself. By mapping feature distributions onto a fixed orthogonal target topology with a ridge regularizer, the framework naturally smoothes and counteracts the influence of extreme label noise during the optimization process. Concurrently, catastrophic forgetting is prevented via a continual topology brake term that leverages the covariance matrix of past experiences.
  Extensive evaluation on real-world multi-session robotics datasets demonstrates that even under severe conditions featuring 40\% symmetric label noise, \FlatManifold{} successfully mitigates gradient corruption. Under extreme cross-session domain shifts spanning various seasons and lighting conditions, the proposed framework establishes high generalization capabilities, significantly outperforming standard sequential optimization baselines and proving that structural linearization itself serves as a powerful mathematical barrier against distributed label corruption.

</details>


### [225] [Video-based detection of cessation of breathing in pre-term infants using machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05230)
*Dineo Serame,Lionel Tarassenko,Mauricio Villarroel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用非接触式视频监测技术从30名早产儿的躯干运动提取呼吸信号，结合残差网络和阻抗呼吸描记等信号，实现了对呼吸暂停相关呼吸停止（COBE）的检测。仅使用视频信号时平衡准确率为76.9%，而视频与阻抗呼吸描记结合后达到90.6%，表明视频信号可作为传统监护的补充手段，提升新生儿呼吸监测的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早产儿因呼吸控制发育不成熟易发生呼吸暂停，但现有接触式监测（如电极、传感器）易受运动伪影、传感器移位和皮肤脆弱性影响，亟需一种可靠的非接触式呼吸监测方法。

Method: 采集30名早产儿的视频与临床记录，从动态追踪的躯干区域提取呼吸运动信号；采用残差网络（ResNet）训练仅基于视频的检测模型，并将视频信号与阻抗呼吸描记（IP）、心电图衍生呼吸（EDR）、光电容积描记呼吸包络（PPG）结合构建混合模型。

Result: 仅视频模型的平衡准确率为76.9%，视频+IP混合模型达到90.6%，优于任一单一模态，证明视频提供了标准生理信号之外的额外呼吸信息。

Conclusion: 视频衍生信号包含临床相关的呼吸特征，与常规生理信号结合可显著提升COBE检测性能，非接触式视频监测有望作为新生儿呼吸监护的补充模态，提高监测系统的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Pre-term infants are susceptible to potentially harmful apnoea-related cessations of breathing due to immature respiratory control. However, reliable respiratory monitoring in the neonatal intensive care unit (NICU) remains challenging because motion artefacts, sensor displacement, and skin fragility can compromise contact-based measurements. Non-contact video monitoring offers a complementary approach that does not depend on adhesive sensors while providing additional respiratory information.
  We investigated whether camera-based signals can detect apnoea-related cessation of breathing (COBE) and provide complementary information to routinely acquired physiological signals. Using video and clinical recordings from 30 pre-term infants, respiratory motion was extracted from dynamically tracked torso regions to generate camera-derived time-series signals. Camera-only models were trained using residual network (ResNet) architectures, while hybrid models combined video-derived signals with impedance pneumography (IP), ECG-derived respiration (EDR), and the PPG-derived respiratory envelope.
  Camera-only models achieved a balanced accuracy of 76.9%, demonstrating the feasibility of non-contact COBE detection. Combining video-derived features with IP improved balanced accuracy to 90.6%, outperforming either modality alone and indicating that video provides respiratory information beyond standard physiological signals.
  These findings show that video-derived signals contain clinically relevant respiratory features and enhance COBE detection when combined with conventional physiological signals. This supports non-contact video as a complementary modality for automated COBE detection and highlights its potential to improve the robustness of neonatal respiratory monitoring.

</details>


### [226] [FUSE: FK-Steered Multi-Modal Flow Matching for Efficient Simulation-Based Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05252)
*Weichen Qin,Yufan Xie,Peihao Wang,Chia-Jui Chou,Minghui Du,Peng Xu,Ziren Luo,Yi Yang,Jingyi Yu,Bo Liang,Jiakai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FUSE，一种基于Feynman-Kac引导的双轨流匹配方法，用于高效的多模态仿真推演推断，在标准SBI基准和真实系外行星轨道估计任务中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SBI方法在处理多模态建模时存在不足，通常采用暴力融合策略，忽视了参数与观测之间的结构差异，限制了估计保真度。

Method: 采用双轨架构（dual-track architecture）以保留多模态输入的不同特征，同时促进动态交互；提出FK（Feynman-Kac）引导采样策略，利用中间观测似然引导生成轨迹，提升推理阶段样本质量。

Result: 在标准SBI基准上优于现有最先进基线，生成的后验分布与真实MCMC高度吻合；在真实系外行星轨道估计任务中，成功解决了现有方法难以处理的参数简并问题。

Conclusion: FUSE在高效仿真推演推断方面具有显著优势，有望加速天体物理学及其他领域的复杂科学发现。

Abstract: Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) is critical for scientific discovery, with generative models offering a promising path toward efficient inference. However, existing methods struggle with effective multimodal modeling. They often rely on brute-force fusion strategies that ignore the structural disparities between parameters and observations, thus limiting estimation fidelity. In this work, we introduce FUSE (Feynman-Kac steered mUlti-modal flow matching for efficient Simulation-based posterior Estimation). Unlike prior work, FUSE employs a dual-track architecture that preserves the distinct features of multimodal inputs while facilitating dynamic interaction. Additionally, we propose an FK-steered sampling strategy that leverages intermediate observation likelihoods to guide the generative trajectories, effectively improving the sample quality during inference. Our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on standard SBI benchmarks, producing posteriors that closely match ground-truth MCMC. Furthermore, in a real-world exoplanet orbital estimation task, FUSE successfully resolves complex parameter degeneracies that challenge existing methods, highlighting its potential to accelerate complex scientific discoveries in astrophysics and beyond.

</details>


### [227] [GeoFlow: Geo-Aware Modeling of Inter-Area Relationships in Origin-Destination Flow Prediction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05257)
*Zherui Huang,Guanjie Zheng,Hao Xue,Linghe Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoFlow是一个融合地理空间属性的OD流建模框架，通过几何-内在融合编码器和轴向-全局注意力解码器，结合流匹配模型，提升了预测准确性和生成逼真度与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的OD流建模方法忽视了关键的地理空间属性，限制了其建模长距离和多区域依赖关系的能力。

Method: (i) 用相对位置、k跳距离和测地距离增强区域表示；(ii) 设计几何-内在融合编码器，结合图注意力和坐标感知编码器；(iii) 采用轴向-全局注意力解码器捕获OD特定竞争依赖；(iv) 与流匹配模型结合生成OD流。

Result: 在预测准确性上表现优异，同时显著提升了生成逼真度和多样性。消融和分析研究确认了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: GeoFlow通过融合地理空间属性与专门设计的编码器-解码器架构，有效改进了OD流建模的预测与生成能力。

Abstract: Origin-destination (OD) flow modeling underpins urban planning and mobility analysis, but prevailing graph-based methods often neglect salient geographic attributes, limiting their ability to model long-range and multi-area dependencies. In this paper, we introduce GeoFlow, a novel framework that (i) augments area representations with geospatial attributes, including relative positions, k-hop and geodesic distances, (ii) employs a specialized geometric-intrinsic fusion encoder design that combines graph attention for intrinsic area signals with coordinate-aware encoders for global structure, and (iii) adopts an axial-global attention decoder to capture OD-specific competitive dependencies. For OD flow generation, GeoFlow is paired with flow matching models to produce more authentic and diverse mobility samples. Empirically, GeoFlow achieves superior performance in predictive accuracy, while substantially improving generative fidelity and diversity. Ablation and analytical studies confirm the contribution of each component. Code is available at https://github.com/ZheruiHuang/GeoFlow.

</details>


### [228] [Target-Guided Selective Reweighting for Physics-Informed Neural Network Inverse Problems: A Transfer Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05271)
*Qian Hu,Bin Fan,Yao Xiao,Zhicheng Lin,Meixin Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对PINN在逆问题迁移学习中的负迁移现象（源域与目标域物理机制、参数或噪声不同导致参数恢复错误），提出TGSR-PINN方法：仅迁移源域权重偏置，独立初始化目标物理参数，通过一阶泰勒敏感度和预激活方差计算神经元目标分数，用GMM将低分神经元转化为弱适应信号，并对这些神经元施加选择性软衰减。实验表明该方法在多个任务中提升目标参数恢复精度且保持场精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在PDE逆问题中存在不适定优化、损失竞争和参数补偿问题；直接微调迁移学习在源域与目标域物理机制、控制参数或观测噪声不同时会产生负迁移，导致场误差低但目标物理参数恢复错误，因此需要一种目标证据驱动的表征修正方法。

Method: TGSR-PINN: (1) 仅迁移源域PINN的权重和偏置，目标物理参数独立初始化；(2) 短目标适应后，使用一阶泰勒敏感度和预激活方差在固定评分批次上计算神经元目标分数；(3) 通过GMM（含秩回退）将低分神经元转化为连续弱适应信号；(4) 对低分神经元的输入权重行和偏置施加选择性软衰减（而非硬剪枝或随机重置）。

Result: 在高Péclet数二维对流扩散任务、Allen-Cahn到Burgers的跨PDE族迁移任务中提升目标参数恢复精度，同时保持相当的场精度；5%噪声的反应扩散任务为较温和源-目标失配提供了补充证据。消融研究表明神经元目标评分、弱适应信号估计、层保护和选择性软衰减均共同贡献。

Conclusion: TGSR-PINN通过目标证据驱动的神经元选择性软衰减，有效缓解了PINN逆迁移学习中的负迁移问题，在保持场精度的同时显著改善了目标物理参数的恢复效果。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) encounter ill-posed optimization, loss competition, and parameter compensation in partial differential equation (PDE) inverse problems. Transfer learning can reuse representations from source tasks, but direct fine-tuning may introduce negative transfer when dominant physical mechanisms, governing parameters, or observation noise differ between source and target domains: the model achieves low field error yet recovers incorrect target physical parameters. To mitigate, we propose Target-Guided Selective Reweighting PINN (TGSR-PINN), a target-evidence-driven representation correction method for PINN inverse transfer learning. TGSR-PINN transfers only the weights and biases from the source PINN, while target physical parameters are independently initialized; after a short target-adaptation phase, the method computes neuron target scores using first-order Taylor sensitivity and pre-activation variance on fixed scoring batches, and converts evidence associated with low-scoring neurons into continuous weak-adaptation signals via a Gaussian mixture model (GMM) with rank fallback. TGSR-PINN then applies selective soft decay to input weight rows and biases of low-scoring neurons instead of hard pruning or random resetting. In experiments, TGSR-PINN improves target parameter recovery while maintaining comparable field accuracy in the high-Péclet 2D advection-diffusion task and in the Allen--Cahn to Burgers cross-PDE-family transfer task; a 5%-noise reaction--diffusion case provides supplementary evidence under milder source-target mismatch. Ablation studies suggest that neuron target scoring, weak-adaptation signal estimation, layer protection, and selective soft decay jointly contribute to the benefits.

</details>


### [229] [Advances in Neural Controlled Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05280)
*Benjamin Walker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文通过三种互补方法（Log-NCDEs、线性NCDEs和结构化线性NCDEs）大幅提升神经控制微分方程（NCDEs）的训练效率和可扩展性，将每步训练时间降低最多三个数量级，同时在多种时间序列基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCDEs虽然具有最大表达能力和连续时间灵活性，但其非线性向量场导致前向传播计算昂贵且本质上是顺序的，限制了可扩展性和实际应用。

Method: (1) Log-NCDEs：基于神经粗糙微分方程，应用Log-ODE方法高效近似NCDE求解过程；(2) 线性NCDEs：用线性向量场替代非线性向量场，实现闭式解和并行计算；(3) 结构化线性NCDEs：使用结构化线性向量场进一步提升效率。

Result: 将NCDE每步训练时间降低最多三个数量级，同时在多样化时间序列基准上取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的三种方法在不牺牲理论表达能力和实证性能的前提下，显著提升了NCDEs的训练效率和可扩展性。

Abstract: Many real-world systems evolve continuously, yet most machine learning models interpret time series as discrete sequences. Continuous-time approaches instead treat time series as samples from an underlying input path, a formulation that naturally accommodates irregularly sampled or oversampled data. Among these, Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDEs) are a maximally expressive class of models that parametrise a vector field using a neural network and evolve their hidden state by solving a dynamical system driven by the input path. NCDEs typically use a non-linear vector field, so their expressive power and continuous-time flexibility come at the cost of a forward pass that is both computationally expensive and inherently sequential, limiting their scalability and practical applicability.
  This thesis advances the training and scalability of NCDEs through three complementary contributions. First, building on neural rough differential equations, Log-NCDEs apply the Log-ODE method to efficiently approximate an NCDE's solution during training, improving both computational speed and empirical performance. Second, Linear NCDEs replace the non-linear vector field with a linear one, enabling closed-form solutions and parallel-in-time computation without sacrificing theoretical expressivity. Third, Structured Linear NCDEs use structured linear vector fields to further enhance efficiency while maintaining theoretical expressiveness and empirical performance.
  Collectively, these methods reduce the time per training step for an NCDE by up to three orders of magnitude while achieving state-of-the-art performance across diverse time series benchmarks.

</details>


### [230] [Learning Only What Valid Adapters Can Express: Subspace-Constrained Adaptation Against Fine-Tuning Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05300)
*Fabien Polly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于信任适配器池子空间约束的参数高效微调方法，通过将模型更新限制在从已有LoRA适配器中估计出的低维共享子空间内，在保持性能的同时显著提升了对数据投毒和后门攻击的鲁棒性，但会牺牲在池子覆盖不佳任务上的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调（如LoRA）虽然更新参数少，但仍有足够自由度让恶意目标函数被表达和优化，存在安全隐患。本文旨在探索一种更安全的适配方式，通过将学习约束在可信适配器池的子空间内来限制可能的更新方向，从而防御投毒和后门攻击。

Method: 在Flan-T5-Large模型和196个公开LoRA适配器上，首先通过矩阵分解等技术估计适配器的共享低维子空间（30-38%的权重范数是冗余的）。然后提出约束学习器，将梯度更新限制在该子空间的128个坐标内，对比完整LoRA微调在干净数据、标签反转投毒、自适应后门攻击等场景下的表现。

Result: (1) 适配器的功能相关内容位于低维共享子空间；(2) 在干净分类数据上约束学习匹配全LoRA性能，在标签反转投毒下LoRA精确匹配率降至3-26%，而约束学习保持在62-96%；(3) 约束学习无法拟合污染数据，适应损失差异达120倍，提供天然OOD检测；(4) 对自适应后门攻击，当目标行为不在池子中时攻击成功率从100%降至8%，当目标与池中常见行为一致时降至85%。

Conclusion: 基于可信适配器池的子空间约束是一种有效的安全微调机制，能在池覆盖的任务上抵抗投毒和后门攻击，但缺点包括：(a) 在池覆盖不佳的任务上性能不如无约束微调；(b) 保护依赖于池本身的可信度；(c) 以降低峰值塑性为代价换取安全属性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning still leaves a broad space of behavior-changing updates reachable, so a poisoned objective can be represented and optimized. We study an alternative: adaptation constrained to the subspace estimated from a trusted pool of existing task adapters. On flan-t5-large with 196 public LoRA adapters, we show that (1) the functionally relevant content of an adapter lies in a low-dimensional shared subspace, 30 to 38 percent of its weight norm being redundant under the evaluated task distributions; (2) gradient adaptation restricted to 128 coordinates on this subspace matches full LoRA fine-tuning on clean classification data, while under targeted label inversion LoRA collapses to 3-26 percent exact match and the constrained learner keeps 62-96 percent on the tasks the pool covers; (3) the constrained learner cannot fit corrupted data, its adaptation loss separating clean from garbage by two orders of magnitude (120x), an out-of-distribution signal without an extra detector; and (4) against an adaptive backdoor attacker who optimizes within the subspace, the attack is blocked (8 percent success versus 100 for LoRA) on the task where its target behavior is unlike anything in the pool, and only partially blocked (85 percent) when the target coincides with a common pool behavior. On these two tasks the outcome is consistent with how close the target is to the pool's directions, which suggests but does not establish a pool-relative boundary. The mechanism trades peak plasticity for these properties: on tasks the pool covers poorly, unconstrained fine-tuning wins, and the protection assumes the pool itself is trusted. Code and data are public.

</details>


### [231] [Biologically Informed Deep Neural Networks for Multi-Omic Integration, Pathway Activity Inference and Risk Stratification in Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05306)
*Pedro Henrique da Costa Avelar,Le Ou-Yang,Min Wu,Sophia Tsoka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于通路活性自编码器（Pathway Activity Autoencoders）的多组学数据整合框架，通过通路先验知识约束架构，在保持高表征能力的同时实现模型可解释性。在乳腺癌生存预测和亚型分类任务中验证了多组学整合的积极效果，并分析了各组学层（基因、蛋白质、miRNA）的贡献，揭示了正则化对模型鲁棒性与性能的权衡，最终生成了具有生物学意义且临床可解释的潜在表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在整合复杂多组学数据时面临可解释性与表征能力之间的权衡：大多数方法要么依赖事后解释（post-hoc interpretation），要么使用线性模型而忽略复杂交互作用。

Method: 提出多组学设定下的通路活性自编码器（Pathway Activity Autoencoders），通过通路先验知识构建架构约束，将生物学知识嵌入模型结构以增强可解释性，同时保留深层表征能力。框架在乳腺癌数据上应用于生存预测和亚型分类任务。

Result: 多组学整合对性能有正向影响；基因、蛋白质和miRNA表达层贡献最强；dropout可提升鲁棒性与一致性，但过度正则化会降低预测性能；可视化展示框架的透明性与临床相关性。

Conclusion: 该通路活性自编码器框架能够生成既具有生物学意义又可直接转化为临床相关见解的优越潜在表征，为多组学整合提供了实用指导原则。

Abstract: Integrating complex, multi-omics data presents significant challenges. Existing approaches often face a trade-off between model interpretability and representational capacity, with most either relying on post-hoc interpretation or use linear models that may overlook complex interactions. We report Pathway Activity Autoencoders for the multi-omics setting, which embed prior knowledge via pathway-informed architectural constraints, fostering interpretability, while preserving representational power. Our multi-omic framework is applied in the context of breast cancer and is evaluated in survival prediction and subtype classification with results indicating a positive effect of integration. We conduct analysis of individual omics layer impact on end-task performance, revealing that gene, protein, and microRNA expression layers provide the strongest contribution. Repeatability studies indicate that, while dropout improves model robustness and consistency, excessive regularisation can reduce predictive performance. Finally, visualizations of the learned feature space illustrate the framework's intrinsic transparency and clinical relevance. The results underscore the value of multi-omic integration and delineate the impact of individual omics layers, establishing practical guidelines for integration within our framework. Overall, our pathway activity autoencoder frameworks yield superior latent representations that are biologically meaningful and are directly translatable into clinically relevant insights.

</details>


### [232] [How Far is Too Far? Defining the Distance Threshold for Verification Siamese Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05329)
*Heloísa Dias Viotto,Cauê Samonek,Lucas Garcia Pedroso,Marcos Sunye,André Abed Grégio,Paulo Lisboa de Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督方法，通过拟合孪生验证网络输出距离的双峰分布并取两峰间最低点作为阈值来自动确定验证阈值，无需标注数据，在四个数据集上达到94%的平均验证准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孪生验证网络在比较人脸、车辆或签名等对象时，虽然能学习到嵌入空间，但确定验证阈值通常需要标注数据，这是一个非平凡的任务。本文旨在消除对标注数据的依赖，实现阈值的自动确定。

Method: 假设孪生验证网络输出的距离分布可近似为双峰函数，通过识别两个模式之间的最小值点来自动确定验证阈值，该方法完全无监督，无需任何标注样本。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、LFW和PKLot四个数据集上评估，平均验证准确率达到94%，与需要标注数据的等错误率（EER）方法性能相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的无监督阈值确定方法能够在部署环境中直接更新验证阈值，避免了人工标注成本，同时保持与有监督方法可比的验证精度。

Abstract: Siamese verification networks are widely used to compare items such as faces, cars, or signatures. In these scenarios, the network is trained to learn an embedding space in which similar objects are mapped closer together, while dissimilar objects are mapped further apart. Two objects are considered to belong to the same class (e.g., the same person in two different images) when the distance between their embeddings falls below a predefined threshold. Defining this threshold, however, is a non-trivial task and typically requires labeled data. In this work, we assume that the distribution of distances produced by a siamese verification network can be approximated by a bimodal function. Based on this assumption, we propose an unsupervised method to determine the verification threshold by identifying the minimum point between the two modes. The proposed approach does not require annotated samples, enabling the verification threshold to be updated directly in the deployment environment without the cost of manual labeling. We evaluate our method on four datasets: MNIST, CIFAR-10, LFW, and PKLot. The results indicate that the proposed approach achieves an average verification accuracy of 94%, comparable to the Equal Error Rate method, while eliminating the need for labeled data.

</details>


### [233] [CompactionRL: Reinforcement Learning with Context Compaction for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05378)
*Yujiang Li,Zhenyu Hou,Yi Jing,Jie Tang,Yuxiao Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CompactionRL，一种结合上下文压缩的强化学习策略，用于训练长程智能体LLM，通过联合优化任务执行和摘要生成，在SWE-bench Verified和Terminal-Bench 2.0上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体LLM受限于有限上下文窗口，交互轨迹可能超出最大上下文长度导致任务无法完成。上下文压缩虽能缓解此问题，但将其融入强化学习训练尚未被充分探索。

Method: CompactionRL：采用token级损失归一化和跨轨迹广义优势估计（GAE），联合优化任务执行与摘要生成，使LLM智能体能从压缩后的长程轨迹中学习。

Result: 在GLM-4.5-Air (106B-A30B)上SWE-bench Verified Pass@1达66.8%（+7.0），Terminal-Bench 2.0达24.5%（+3.1）；在GLM-4.7-Flash (30B-A3B)上分别提升5.5和6.8个百分点；已部署于GLM-5.2 (750B-A40B)的RL训练流程。

Conclusion: CompactionRL能有效训练具有上下文压缩的长程智能体LLM，在多种规模的开放模型上取得一致的性能提升，并已实际应用于更大规模模型的强化学习训练中。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic LLMs are increasingly limited by finite context windows, as extended interaction trajectories can exceed the maximum context length before a task is completed. Context compaction offers a natural solution by summarizing previous interaction states and continuing the rollout under a compressed context, but incorporating compaction into reinforcement learning remains underexplored. We propose CompactionRL, a reinforcement learning strategy to train long-horizon agentic LLMs with context compaction. Our approach jointly optimizes task execution and summary generation with token-level loss normalization and cross-trajectory generalized advantage estimation. This design enables the LLM agents to learn from compacted long-horizon trajectories. We train CompactionRL on top of open models and observe consistent performance gains on agentic coding tasks. CompactionRL enables the open GLM-4.5-Air model (106B-A30B) to achieve Pass@1 scores of 66.8% on SWE-bench Verified and 24.5% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, with absolute gains of 7.0 and 3.1 points, respectively. Built upon GLM-4.7-Flash (30B-A3B), CompactionRL improves Pass@1 by 5.5 and 6.8 points, reaching 56.0% on SWE-bench Verified and 20.2% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, respectively. CompactionRL is thus deployed in the RL pipeline for training the open GLM-5.2 model (750B-A40B).

</details>


### [234] [TabPack: Efficient Hyperparameter Ensembles for Tabular Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05380)
*Yury Gorishniy,Akim Kotelnikov,Ivan Rubachev,Artem Babenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPack是一种高效的多层感知机集成方法，通过在一次运行中并行采样和训练不同超参数的MLP，并在训练过程中动态选择集成成员，实现了开箱即用的强大性能，大幅减少了对传统超参数调优的依赖和计算资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高效MLP集成方法对所有底层MLP使用相同超参数，需要大量超参数调优才能达到最佳性能，这增加了计算成本和人工干预。作者旨在减少这种调优负担。

Method: 在单次运行中并行采样和训练多个具有不同超参数的MLP，并在训练过程中动态选择集成成员。用户只需指定超参数采样范围而非精确值，降低了调优精度要求。

Result: 在中等至大规模公开数据集上，使用默认设置的TabPack性能与经过大量调优的先前方法相当；在MacBook上运行默认配置比在某些行业级GPU上调优基线方法耗时更短。

Conclusion: TabPack显著减少了在表格数据任务上获得有竞争力结果所需的调优努力和计算资源，实现了高效且开箱即用的强大性能。

Abstract: In deep learning for tabular data, efficient ensembles of multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) have recently emerged as effective and practical architectures. Existing methods of this kind use the same hyperparameters for all underlying MLPs, which requires hyperparameter tuning for achieving the best performance. In this work, we introduce TabPack, an efficient MLP ensemble with strong out-of-the-box performance and reduced reliance on traditional tuning. In a single run, TabPack samples and trains many MLPs with different hyperparameters efficiently in parallel and selects ensemble members on the fly during training. Thus, TabPack only requires specifying ranges from which to sample MLP hyperparameter rather than exact hyperparameter values, which naturally demands less precision for good performance. In experiments on medium-to-large public datasets, TabPack with default settings performs on par with extensively tuned prior methods, thus substantially reducing effort and compute resources needed to achieve competitive results on tabular tasks. Notably, running the default TabPack configuration on a modern MacBook took less time than tuning some baselines on an industry-grade GPU.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [GLM-5 Serving Parameter Tuning for OpenClaw: Single-Deployment MaaS Inference Optimization for Long-Context Agent Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02518)
*Minjie Hua,Ning Wang,Peijun Yang,Kai Wang,Shiguo Lian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对GLM-5在MaaS多模型推理优化架构下的服务参数调优，聚焦单节点优化块中的分块预填充、张量并行、流水线并行和请求并发度。在双节点16 GPU集群上，最佳配置为分块大小3072、tp=4、pp=4、最大并发请求24，相比基线，吞吐量提升约11.6%，TTFT降低25.5%，P90延迟降低18.9%，服务成本降低约10.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenClaw请求以长工具增强前缀（系统提示、对话历史、工具输出）为主，输入约28k-30k token、输出500 token，服务质量由吞吐量、TTFT和尾延迟共同决定，需要针对这一特定工作负载优化GLM-5的服务参数。

Method: 在MaaS多模型推理优化架构的单节点优化块中，对四个关键参数进行系统调优：分块预填充大小（chunked-prefill-size）、张量并行度（TP）、流水线并行度（PP）、请求最大并发数（max-running-requests）。实验在双节点16 GPU集群上运行，对比保守基线配置（2048/4/4/16）。

Result: 最佳配置（3072/4/4/24）：请求吞吐从0.43提升至0.48 req/s，token吞吐从9029.64提升至9993.23 tok/s，平均TTFT从8.98降至6.69 s，P90延迟从40.23降至32.64 s，估计服务成本降低10.4%/请求、9.6%/token。

Conclusion: 最优配置是工作负载特定的，更大的分块大小和更深的排队并不单调提升性能。推荐3072/tp4/pp4/max24作为OpenClaw部署的默认配置。

Abstract: OpenClaw requests are dominated by long, tool-augmented prefixes, including system prompts, conversation history, and tool outputs fed back into the context window. For this workload, with about 28k-30k input tokens and 500 output tokens per request, serving quality is governed by throughput, TTFT, and tail latency rather than short-prompt throughput alone.
  This report studies GLM-5 serving-parameter tuning within a MaaS multi-model inference optimization architecture. The scope is the Single-Node Optimization block of the inference-optimization layer, where chunked prefill, tensor parallelism (TP), pipeline parallelism (PP), and request concurrency are tuned for one GLM-5 serving deployment; in this report, "Single-Node Optimization" denotes the architecture block, while experiments run on a two-node, sixteen-GPU cluster.
  Within the tested space, the best configuration is chunked-prefill-size=3072, tp=4, pp-size=4, and max-running-requests=24. Compared with the conservative 2048/4/4/16 baseline, it increases request throughput from 0.43 to 0.48 req/s and total token throughput from 9029.64 to 9993.23 tok/s, while reducing average TTFT from 8.98 to 6.69 s and latency P90 from 40.23 to 32.64 s. Under the same hardware footprint, this corresponds to an estimated 10.4% lower serving cost per request and 9.6% lower cost per token. The results show that the optimum is workload-specific: larger chunk sizes and deeper queueing do not monotonically improve performance. We therefore recommend 3072 / tp4 / pp4 / max24 as the default OpenClaw deployment profile.

</details>


### [236] [Kotlin-MP: DSL and IR Transformer for Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02519)
*Ruikai Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对Kotlin缺乏低开销硬件并行原语的问题，提出了Kotlin-MP：一个结合编译器插件的领域特定语言(DSL)，通过编译期IR变换将OpenMP风格的并行指令直接降级为优化后的ForkJoinPool任务，避免了运行时抽象开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kotlin虽为异步并发（如协程）提供了优秀抽象，但缺乏用于真正硬件并行的低开销、指令式构造。开发者使用标准任务并行库处理计算密集型数学循环时，会因抽象层（如lambda分配、状态机生成）引入显著运行时开销，且难以保证代码的可读性、可靠性和可维护性。

Method: 提出Kotlin-MP，包含两部分：1) 一个原生Kotlin DSL，提供直观的、类OpenMP的语法（如并行区域、调度策略、临界区标记）；2) 一个作为Kotlin编译器插件的自定义中间表示(IR)变换器。该IR变换器在编译期拦截DSL，直接进行结构重写：将并行化部分降级为优化后的java.util.concurrent.ForkJoinPool任务，并自动管理循环分块、线程调度，注入硬件同步与互斥机制。

Result: 编译期IR变换避免了运行时包装器的开销，直接生成底层的ForkJoinPool任务，自动处理循环分块、线程调度以及硬件同步互斥的注入，从而降低并行计算的开销。

Conclusion: Kotlin-MP通过编译期DSL+IR变换的方案，为Kotlin提供了低开销的硬件并行能力，兼顾了OpenMP式的易用性与底层性能优化的效率。

Abstract: While Kotlin provides exceptional abstractions for asynchronous concurrency (like Coroutines for I/O), it lacks low-overhead, directive-based constructs for true hardware parallelism. When developers attempt to parallelize compute-bound mathematical loops using standard task-parallel libraries, they encounter significant runtime overhead such as lambda allocations and state-machine generation due to the abstraction layers, and face difficulties in ensuring readability, reliability and maintainability in implementing them.
  To address this, this project proposes Kotlin-MP, a native Kotlin Domain-Specific Language (DSL) paired with a custom Intermediate Representation (IR) transformer as a Kotlin compiler plugin. The DSL provides an intuitive, OpenMP-like syntax for developers to denote parallel regions, schedules, and critical sections. Instead of acting as a standard runtime wrapper, the IR transformer intercepts this DSL at compile-time and structurally rewrites the code. It lowers the parallelized sections directly into optimized \textit{java.util.concurrent.ForkJoinPool} tasks, automatically managing loop chunking, performing thread scheduling, and injecting hardware synchronization and mutual exclusion.

</details>


### [237] [Tile-Level Activation Overlap for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02521)
*Abhinav Jangda,Tyler Sorensen,Sebastian Burckhardt,Jianlan YE,Chaoyin Li,Atul Gupta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出两种基于CUTLASS的SM90融合核函数，将SwiGLU激活函数与GeMM在tile级融合，在H100上实现最高2.47倍加速，达到79.5% BF16峰值利用率，且数值精度优于cuBLAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SwiGLU是现代大语言模型中主流的MLP激活函数，但其中间张量物化开销占MLP执行时间的9-37%，亟需通过融合优化减少内存开销。

Method: 提出两种互补的CUTLASS-based SM90核函数：Kernel-1采用Pingpong warp-specialized调度，将Swish计算与Up-tile加载重叠；Kernel-2通过自定义Epilogue Visitor Tree将SwiGLU与tile存储交错执行。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5模型（0.5B-72B）和NVIDIA H100上，相比PyTorch最高加速2.47倍，达到79.5% BF16峰值利用率；torch.compile无法复现该融合（慢3-7倍）；数值零不匹配，而cuBLAS有4.5-11%的不匹配率。

Conclusion: 手工设计的tile级融合核函数能有效解决SwiGLU中间张量物化瓶颈，显著提升计算效率与数值精度，且自动编译工具无法替代这种精细优化。

Abstract: SwiGLU is the dominant MLP activation in modern large language models, yet its intermediate tensor materialization costs 9-37% of MLP execution time. We present two complementary CUTLASS-based SM90 kernels that fuse SwiGLU into GeMM at the tile level. Kernel-1 overlaps Swish computation on the Gate accumulator with Up-tile loading using the Pingpong warp-specialized schedule; Kernel-2 interleaves SwiGLU with tile stores via a custom Epilogue Visitor Tree. Evaluated on Qwen-2.5 models (0.5B-72B) on NVIDIA H100, our kernels achieve up to 2.47x speedup over PyTorch, shifting workloads from memory-bound to compute-bound and reaching 79.5% peak BF16 utilization. We demonstrate that torch.compile cannot replicate this fusion (3-7x slower than our kernels), validating the need for hand-crafted tile-level design. Our fused kernels are also numerically superior, achieving zero mismatches compared to 4.5-11% for cuBLAS.

</details>


### [238] [Multi-Turn Distributed Inference with Mixture of Experts for 6G Edge--Cloud Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02522)
*Bo Liu,Haiyuan Li,Yuelin Liu,Yulei Wu,Rasheed Hussain,Shadi Moazzeni,Dimitra Simeonidou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: StateFlow是一种面向6G边缘-云网络中MoE架构的分布式推理策略，通过将持久KV状态与稀疏计算解耦，在固定站点保留KV状态实现跨轮复用，同时优化专家调度和聚合放置。实验表明，相比基线方案，StateFlow可实现2倍以上的稳定对话并发量，并将多轮推理的p95延迟降低53.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构在边缘-云网络中面临KV状态局部性与弹性专家调度之间的根本矛盾：KV状态迁移开销大，而专家计算需要分散利用网络容量。多轮推理中每轮扩展的KV状态加剧了这一矛盾。

Method: 提出StateFlow策略，将持久KV状态与瞬态稀疏计算解耦：在固定"粘性"服务站点固定KV状态实现跨轮复用，并联合优化专家调度和聚合放置位置。基于真实测试床和内核级网络仿真进行实验验证。

Result: 相比分布式基线方案，StateFlow可维持2倍以上的稳定对话并发量，多轮推理场景下p95延迟降低53.0%。

Conclusion: 通过解耦KV状态与稀疏计算，StateFlow有效解决了MoE推理中状态局部性与弹性调度之间的冲突，显著提升了边缘-云网络中的多轮推理性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are increasingly deployed across 6G edge--cloud networks, where sparse activation reduces the computational footprint of each inference to only a fraction of the full expert set. However, MoE inference in edge-cloud networks creates a tension between KV state locality and elastic expert dispatch. KV state relocation incurs substantial transfer overhead, while expert computation benefits from spreading across the network to exploit available capacity. This tension is amplified in multi-turn inference, where each turn extends the KV state that must persist across the dialogue. To this end, we present StateFlow, a distributed inference policy that decouples persistent KV state from transient sparse computation. StateFlow pins KV state at a sticky serving site for cross-turn reuse and jointly optimizes expert dispatch and aggregation placement across the network. We further implement a real-world testbed with kernel-level network emulation and experimental results show that StateFlow sustains more than $2\times$ higher stable dialogue concurrency than the distributed baseline solutions and reduces turn-level p95 latency under multi-turn inference by 53.0\%.

</details>


### [239] [Edge-Deployable LLM Fine-Tuning on a Single GPU for Telecom Network Troubleshooting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02523)
*Chenhua Shi,Bhavika Jalli,John Zou,Gregor Macdonald,Wanlu Lei,Mridul Jain,Joji Philip*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对电信边缘站点部署大语言模型（LLM）面临的功耗、冷却、空间等资源限制问题，系统性地研究了在单一边缘级GPU上使用Unsloth框架进行LLM微调的性能特征。分析了最大序列长度、GPU内存利用、LoRA秩和生成数量对训练稳定性和资源效率的影响，并探讨了KV缓存、激活内存开销及编译优化的权衡。实验在电信故障排查数据集上进行，为电信边缘环境中稳定、高效且资源感知的LLM微调提供了实用配置指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信边缘站点故障排查需要低延迟模型响应和本地化模型适配，但边缘站点在GPU基础设施上存在功耗、冷却、空间和重量预算的严格限制。此外，人类模式化的无线接入网（RAN）流量导致GPU利用率低、投资回报差，确定性ASIC处理的电信架构与GPU导向的AI工作负载存在架构不匹配。因此，单GPU微调成为可扩展边缘AI部署的实际需求，而非仅仅是资源限制。

Method: 使用Unsloth框架在单一边缘级加速器上进行LLM微调的GPU性能分析。系统分析了最大序列长度、GPU内存利用、LoRA秩和生成数量对训练稳定性和资源效率的影响。研究了KV缓存使用、激活内存开销和电感编译器下的运行时稳定性权衡。对比了推理型与非推理型模型架构在监督微调（SFT）和强化微调（RFT）中的行为差异。实验使用了由问答对和top-3检索文档增强的电信故障排查数据集。

Result: 不同模型架构（推理型与非推理型）在SFT和RFT过程中因聊天模板结构、推理标签和控制标志的差异表现出显著不同的行为。研究提供了电信边缘环境中LLM微调的实际配置指导。

Conclusion: 研究结果为电信边缘环境中实现稳定、高效且资源感知的LLM微调提供了实用配置指南，有助于在资源受限的边缘站点实现可扩展的AI部署。

Abstract: Telecom troubleshooting at edge sites requires low-latency model responses and localized model adaptation to satisfy operational and data sovereignty requirements. However, deploying large language models (LLMs) at telecom edge sites is constrained by limited power, cooling, space, and weight budgets for GPU infrastructure. These challenges are further amplified by human-patterned Radio Access Network (RAN) traffic that often results in low GPU utilization and poor return on investment, as well as by architectural mismatches between deterministic ASIC-based telecom processing and GPU-oriented AI workloads.
