Consequently, single-GPU fine-tuning becomes a practical requirement for scalable edge AI deployment rather than merely a resource limitation. This paper presents a GPU profiling study of LLM fine-tuning using the Unsloth framework on a single edge-class accelerator. We systematically analyze the effects of maximum sequence length, GPU memory utilization, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) rank, and generation count on training stability and resource efficiency. We further investigate trade-offs in KV cache usage, activation memory overhead, and runtime stability under inductor compilation. In addition, we show that reasoning and non-reasoning model architectures exhibit substantially different behaviors during supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) because of differences in chat template structures, reasoning tags, and control flags. Experiments are conducted on a telecom troubleshooting dataset consisting of question-answer pairs augmented with top-3 retrieved contextual documents. The results provide practical configuration guidelines for stable, efficient, and resource-aware LLM fine-tuning in telecom edge environments.

</details>


### [240] [SWIFT: Spatio-temporal Wavelet Integrated Forecasting Framework for Workload Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02524)
*Zeyuan Ding,Lingfeng Zheng,Dian Ding,Guangtao Xue*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出SWIFT框架，通过可学习级联小波路径（将传统固定小波基改造为自适应卷积算子）和多变量交互模块（顺序建模变量间空间交互和变量内特征交互），实现高精度、低延迟的云工作负载预测，在SOTA基础上误差降低31.04%、延迟降低79.74%，且保持线性复杂度O(L)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云工作负载预测对资源管理至关重要，但工作负载高度波动且易突发。现有小波方法虽保留时域局部性，但固定基难以处理复杂模式，且孤立处理忽略了关键的空间依赖关系。

Method: 1) 可学习级联小波路径：将固定小波基重构为自适应卷积算子，实现数据驱动的特征剥离；2) 多变量交互模块：顺序建模变量间空间交互和变量内特征交互，稳定和细化含噪工作负载状态。

Result: SOTA精度，线性复杂度O(L)，预测误差降低31.04%，延迟降低79.74%。

Conclusion: 纯卷积框架SWIFT通过自适应小波算子和多变量交互建模，在提升精度的同时大幅降低了计算延迟，为云工作负载预测提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate cloud workload forecasting is pivotal for efficient resource management but remains challenging as workloads are highly volatile and prone to sudden bursts. Although wavelets preserve temporal locality, rigid fixed bases struggle with complex patterns and isolated processing neglects critical spatial dependencies. To address this, we propose SWIFT, a pure convolutional framework designed for high-efficiency workload forecasting. We introduce a Learnable Cascaded Wavelet Path that reformulates the traditional fixed wavelet bases into adaptive convolutional operators, enabling precise, data-driven feature peeling. Complementing this, our Multivariate Interaction Module sequentially models inter-variable spatial and intra-variable feature interactions to stabilize and refine noisy workload states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SWIFT achieves SOTA accuracy with linear O(L) complexity, reducing prediction error by up to 31.04% while cutting latency by 79.74%.

</details>


### [241] [PEEK: Predictive Queue-Informed KV Cache Management for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02525)
*Bing Xie,Zhipeng Wang,Masahiro Tanaka,Zheng Zhen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PEEK是一个轻量级调度与驱逐框架，通过在待处理队列上维护增量基数树，暴露前缀共享簇，结合双游走匹配、簇先驱优先调度、协同驱逐钩子和多通道步幅调度器，在SGLang和vLLM上实现高达3.0×/2.6×缓存命中率、7.9×/7.1× TTFT、6.7×/5.5×端到端延迟和3.6×/4.5×吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM serving引擎未能有效利用待处理请求之间的前缀共享结构，导致KV缓存利用率低，TTFT和吞吐量性能受限。

Method: 1) 在待处理队列上维护增量基数树以暴露前缀共享簇；2) 双游走算法将树与引擎前缀缓存匹配，为每个等待请求找到最长前缀匹配；3) 簇先驱优先调度，使同簇后续请求继承新鲜缓存；4) 协同驱逐钩子保护队列中需求祖先块；5) 多通道步幅调度器限制饥饿。

Result: 在SGLang和vLLM上，5个workloads（最大4×H100，DP=2 over TP=2）：缓存命中率提升最高3.0×/2.6×，TTFT提升最高7.9×/7.1×，端到端延迟提升最高6.7×/5.5×，吞吐量提升最高3.6×/4.5×；无前缀结构场景下与基线持平。

Conclusion: PEEK在保持对无前缀结构workload无负面影响的前提下，显著提升了有前缀共享场景下的LLM serving性能，且优势随KV缓存压力和推理并行度扩展而保持。

Abstract: We present PEEK, a lightweight scheduling and eviction framework for both online (streaming) and offline (batch) LLM serving; this paper focuses on the online regime. PEEK maintains an incremental radix tree over the pending queue, exposing
  prefix-sharing clusters no existing engine surfaces. A low-overhead dual-walk matches the tree against the engine's prefix cache to yield longest-prefix-match for every waiting request; PEEK then admits cluster pioneers first so siblings
  inherit the freshly cached prefix, a co-designed eviction hook protects blocks ancestral to queued demand, and a multi-lane stride scheduler bounds starvation. On SGLang and vLLM across five workloads up to 4$\times$H100 (DP=2 over TP=2),
  PEEK delivers up to 3.0$\times$/2.6$\times$ cache hit, 7.9$\times$/7.1$\times$ TTFT, 6.7$\times$/5.5$\times$ E2E, and 3.6$\times$/4.5$\times$ throughput gains over each engine's strongest stock baseline (SGLang/vLLM), while matching
  baselines within noise on workloads with no exploitable prefix structure. Wins hold as KV-cache pressure and inference parallelism scale.

</details>


### [242] [Operational Memory Architecture for Kubernetes: Evidence Horizon Taxonomy and Extended Causal Pattern Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02528)
*Shamsher Khan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文形式化了Kubernetes集群中五种证据边界（H1-H5）导致诊断上下文永久丢失的机制，并扩展了操作记忆架构（OMA），新增两个因果模式（P004）、两个Go观察器及两个存储表，以捕获跨边界的调度器证据链和临时容器调试上下文，验证于Minikube和AKS集群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes原生事件保留机制存在系统性缺陷，会在确定性调度后永久丢弃具有诊断价值的上下文信息，导致运维排障困难。现有方案未能全面应对这些证据销毁机制。

Method: 形式化定义证据边界分类（H1-H5）；扩展OMA架构，新增P004因果模式（调度决策溯源）、两个Go观察器（EventWatcher, EphemeralWatcher）和两个存储表（scheduler_events, ephemeral_exits）；在Minikube和AKS 1.32.10上验证。

Result: 在3节点Minikube和AKS集群上完成验证；原30轮统计延迟分析（242条边，周期内均值0.702ms）和压力评估（20 Pods时2.86事件/秒，8.8MB内存）被继承，并补充了H2、H3、H5的实验结果。

Conclusion: 通过跨证据边界的因果链捕获，OMA扩展架构能够有效保留Kubernetes集群中本会被永久丢失的诊断上下文，提升运维可观测性。

Abstract: Kubernetes clusters generate rich operational events during pod lifecycle transitions, yet the platform's native event retention model systematically discards the most diagnostically valuable context through multiple evidence destruction mechanisms operating on deterministic schedules. We formalize these mechanisms as an evidence horizon taxonomy: five distinct boundaries after which specific categories of diagnostic context become permanently unrecoverable from the Kubernetes API. H1(LastTerminationState rotation, ~90s) destroys container failure forensics; H2 (scheduler event pruning, 1hr/1000-event cluster limit) destroys placement rationale; H3 (ephemeral container exit, immediate) destroys debug session context; H4 (kubelet reconciliation gap) destroys in-memory operational state; and H5 (scrape-interval blind spot) renders sub-interval pod lifetimes invisible to poll-based observability tools.
  This paper extends the Operational Memory Architecture (OMA) to address the full evidence horizon taxonomy. Two new causal patterns are defined: P004 (Scheduler Decision Provenance) captures FailedScheduling predicate failures before kube-apiserver TTL pruning and demonstrates the first cross-horizon causal chain linking scheduler evidence to downstream OOMKill failures.
  Two new Go watchers (EventWatcher, EphemeralWatcher) and two new storage tables (scheduler_events, ephemeral_exits) extend the original architecture. Validated on Minikube (3-node) and AKS 1.32.10. The original 30-run statistical latency analysis (242 edges, intra-cycle mean 0.702ms) and stress evaluation (2.86 events/sec at 20 pods, 8.8MB RAM) are carried forward and augmented with H2, H3, and H5 results.

</details>


### [243] [RepliCore: Reproducible Parallel Simulation under Asynchronous Browser Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02529)
*Anqing Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RepliCore是一个面向浏览器的确定性并行模拟框架，通过分离异步运行时进度与逻辑状态可见性，确保在异步浏览器环境下大规模模拟的可重现性，实验证明在不同配置下均能产生比特级一致输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浏览器模拟依赖事件循环、渲染回调和Web Workers等异步运行时机制，导致模拟进度受运行时时序和回调调度行为影响，产生不确定性，亟需一种不依赖执行重放或显式调度控制的确定性并行模拟方案。

Method: 将异步运行时进度与外部可观测的逻辑状态可见性分离，仅在逻辑状态达到外部稳定后才暴露运行时可见的模拟状态，防止渲染回调和异步任务在并行推进期间观察到瞬态中间逻辑状态，从而保持并行与串行执行的一致性。

Result: 在真实浏览器环境中，跨不同的Worker配置、调度条件和渲染频率下均产生比特级一致的输出，且对大规模浏览器工作负载仍保持实用性；消融实验表明放松关键执行约束后系统性地出现状态发散。

Conclusion: 通过受控的逻辑状态可见性稳定化，无需执行重放或显式调度控制即可实现可重现的异步并行模拟。

Abstract: Browser-based simulations execute over asynchronous runtime mechanisms including event loops, rendering callbacks, and independently scheduled Web Workers, causing simulation progression to depend on runtime timing and callback scheduling behavior. RepliCore addresses this problem by separating asynchronous runtime progression from externally observable logical-state visibility. Runtime-visible simulation states are exposed only after logical-state progression becomes externally stable, preventing asynchronous runtime activities from observing partially updated simulation progression. The framework prevents rendering callbacks and asynchronous runtime tasks from observing transient intermediate logical states during parallel progression. This organization maintains consistency between parallel and sequential execution. Based on this model, we implement RepliCore, a browser-oriented deterministic parallel simulation framework for reproducible large-scale simulation under asynchronous browser runtimes. Experiments in real browser environments produce bitwise-identical outputs across varying worker configurations, scheduling conditions, and rendering frequencies while remaining practical for large-scale browser-oriented workloads. Additional ablation experiments show systematic state divergence after relaxing key execution constraints. These results indicate that reproducible asynchronous parallel simulation can be achieved through controlled logical-state visibility stabilization without relying on execution replay or explicit schedule control.

</details>


### [244] [Static PTX Metrics Track Structural Kernel Regressions but Miss Semantic Ones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02541)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出在GPU内核分析中，使用静态PTX指标（寄存器、溢出、指令数）的差异作为便携式预过滤器，可以明确区分结构性bug（改变内核工作量）和语义性bug（如常量替换），而测量运行时性能差异受硬件和噪声影响大，不能替代静态分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GPU内核性能分析中，运行时测量的Delta性能百分比受硬件平台和噪声影响大，缺乏一种便携、稳定的预过滤方法来区分结构性变化（改变工作量）和语义性变化（不改变工作量）。

Method: 将每个GPU内核的静态PTX指标（寄存器、溢出、指令数）与CUDA事件计时运行时数据配对，在五种GPU类别上进行测试，分析正确/错误内核对的Delta-regs、Delta-instrs和Delta-perf%，比较静态信号与测量信号。

Result: 静态PTX信号（Delta-regs和Delta-instrs）在所有五种GPU上一致，可清晰区分结构性bug（如gelu_triton_buggy减少8条指令和8个寄存器）和语义性bug（如softmax_triton_buggy编译为字节完全相同的PTX）。而测量Delta-perf%在RTX 3060上仅+3.2%，在亚毫秒级噪声带内不可靠。

Conclusion: 对于该语料库和工具链，静态PTX差异门控是一种便携式预过滤器，可分离结构性变化与语义性变化；在亚毫秒尺度上，测量运行时差异对硬件和噪声敏感，不能替代静态分析。

Abstract: We pair each GPU kernel's static PTX metrics (registers, spills, instruction count) with CUDA-event-timed runtime on five GPU classes: RTX 3060, A10, L40S, A100 SXM4, and H100 NVL. In this corpus and toolchain the static and measured signals separate cleanly along one axis. Per-pair Delta-regs and Delta-instrs are identical across all five GPUs for any given (correct, buggy) pair. Measured Delta-perf% is not. Structural bugs that change the kernel's work are unambiguous in the static signal. The gelu_triton_buggy variant, which drops a leading 0.5 factor, removes 8 instructions and 8 registers. The corresponding measured Delta-perf% on RTX 3060 is +3.2%, within the run-to-run noise band at the sub-millisecond scale these corpus kernels occupy. Semantic bugs that swap one constant for another are invisible to the static signal. The softmax_triton_buggy variant, which substitutes other=0.0 for -inf on the masked load, compiles to byte-identical PTX. The paper's bounded claim is that, for this corpus and toolchain, a static-PTX delta gate is a portable pre-filter that separates structural from semantic changes; measured runtime deltas at this scale are hardware- and noise-sensitive and are not a substitute.

</details>


### [245] [ETALE: Evolving Topology with Accelerated Lock-free Execution for Dynamic Graph ANN Search on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02543)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ETALE，一种支持流式插入和删除的GPU原生图ANN索引，通过无锁写时复制slab图结构实现高效动态更新，性能显著优于现有静态和动态索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU上的图ANN索引仅支持静态集合，更新需重建整个窗口；而CPU上的动态图索引无法利用GPU并行性。需要一种GPU原生支持流式插入和删除的ANN索引。

Method: 提出ETALE，核心是**无锁写时复制slab图**（lock-free copy-on-write slab graph），其删除状态与邻接表共享单个原子发布的字（atomically published word），实现了可证明的删除单调性不变性（deletion-monotonicity invariant），以及随累积删除次数亚线性增长的GPU内存有界回收。

Result: 在五个多模态数据集上，与Tagore、DIGRA、CAGRA、HNSW相比，在持续更新下ETALE每轮维护时间在数百毫秒内，召回率>0.95；比CAGRA快4.8-8.8倍，比Tagore快1.8-2.5倍，比CPU动态索引快3.3-147倍；内存占用有界，而仅使用墓碑的系统会无限增长。

Conclusion: ETALE是首批GPU原生支持流式插入和删除的图ANN索引，无需全局重建，在性能和内存效率上均优于现有方案。

Abstract: Graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) indexes on the GPU are originally built for static collections and must reconstruct the affected window to absorb any update, while the dynamic graph indexes that update incrementally run on the CPU and cannot exploit GPU parallelism. This paper presents a new ANN index, namely ETALE (Evolving Topology with Accelerated Lock-free Execution), which is among the first GPU-native graph ANN indexes to support streaming insertion and deletion without a global rebuild. The core of ETALE is a lock-free copy-on-write slab graph whose deletion state and adjacency share a single atomically published word, which yields a provable deletion-monotonicity invariant together with a bounded reclaim of GPU memory that is sublinear in accumulated deletions. We have implemented ETALE in CUDA and evaluate it on five diverse multimodal datasets against four state-of-the-art indexes: Tagore (GPU static, SIGMOD'26), DIGRA (CPU dynamic, SIGMOD'26), CAGRA (GPU static, ICDE'24), and HNSW (CPU dynamic, TPAMI'20). Under continuous churn, ETALE maintains the index in hundreds of milliseconds per round at recall above $0.95$, which is $4.8$--$8.8\times$ faster than CAGRA's per-window rebuild, $1.8$--$2.5\times$ faster than the more recent Tagore, and $3.3$--$147\times$ faster than the CPU dynamic indexes. In addition, the memory footprint of ETALE stays bounded where tombstone-only systems grow indefinitely.

</details>


### [246] [Not Every Sync Is Safe: Calibrated DiLoCo Scheduling for Shared AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02544)
*Maxwell Twelftree,David Lemphers,An-chi He,Yue Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出WA-DiLoCo方法，通过评分控制器权衡学习者进度与集群压力，并引入校准协议来确定何时能击败匹配随机延迟策略。在vLLM副车重放实验中，结合EWMA突发预测可将SLO违例从6.54%降至5.09%，但核心贡献是报告协议：要求在实际副车效果量迁移、无同步负载匹配和匹配随机包络线下验证服务SLO改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DiLoCo式训练调度研究评估工作负载感知策略时，大多省略了能隔离时序与预算的匹配随机延迟控制基线，导致报告的性能提升可能归因于窗口选择而非真正的策略优势。

Method: 提出Workload-Aware DiLoCo (WA-DiLoCo)，包含一个基于评分的控制器（权衡学习者进度与集群压力），以及一个校准协议（确定何时能击败匹配随机延迟）；在突发场景下增加一步EWMA突发预测。

Result: 匹配随机延迟在受控压力测试和真实vLLM副车重放中优于或持平所有无预测策略；WA-DiLoCo加EWMA在真实副车重放中将SLO违例从6.54%降至5.09%（8/10种子，p=0.021）；离线Calibrated-WA显示剩余空间为4.45% vs 6.26%。

Conclusion: 报告策略改进前必须包含三个要素：实际副车效果量迁移、无同步负载匹配和匹配随机包络线；核心可部署经验是协议本身而非特定算法。

Abstract: DiLoCo-style training reduces communication by letting learner islands train locally before occasional outer synchronization, making it attractive for fragmented industrial AI fleets where training shares hardware with latency-sensitive serving. The question for such fleets is when an outer merge is worth its system cost, and whether choosing \emph{which} windows to defer matters at all. Existing scheduling studies evaluate workload-aware policies against fixed-period baselines, but most omit the control that isolates timing from budget: matched random deferral, which inherits the controller's synchronization budget but is not itself deployable. This omission is consequential: across controlled stress tests and real vLLM sidecar replays, matched random ties or beats every forecast-free policy we test, so gains reported against weaker baselines cannot be attributed to window choice. We fill this gap with Workload-Aware DiLoCo (WA-DiLoCo), a score-based controller that weighs learner progress against fleet pressure, and a calibration protocol that determines when matched random can be beaten, then demonstrate that it can. In the bursty regime where calibration exposes request-overlap structure, adding a one-step EWMA burst forecast to the online controller beats matched random in real vLLM sidecar replay, reducing SLO violations from 6.54\% to 5.09\% (8 of 10 seeds, $p=0.021$); offline Calibrated-WA, a non-deployable bound, shows the remaining headroom at 4.45\% versus 6.26\%. The deployable lesson remains the protocol: report real-sidecar effect-size transfer, a no-sync load match, and a matched-random envelope before claiming serving-SLO improvement.

</details>


### [247] [MLSYSIM: First-Principles Infrastructure Modeling for Machine Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02558)
*Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MLSYSIM是一个基于第一性原理的分析框架，通过需求-供给抽象、维度严格的Python引擎和类型化溯源注册表，将系统"物理规律"形式化，能够在亚秒级内探索从微瓦级微控制器到千兆瓦级数据中心的全栈ML系统设计空间，识别关键约束并综合出理想硬件规格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统从实验室走向关键基础设施，其支撑系统涵盖从亚毫瓦级微控制器到多吉瓦级数据中心集群的极广范围。现有解决方案中，经验性分析需要目标硬件在手，周期精确模拟需要数小时，缺乏能够快速进行全栈架构推理的工具。

Method: 提出MLSYSIM框架，核心包括：(1) 需求-供给抽象，解耦计算需求与硅供给及环境上下文；(2) 维度严格的Python引擎，运行时强制单位完整性以防止静默转换错误；(3) 类型化、可溯源注册表确保每个输入都有文档化的来源；(4) 编码22个"系统墙"的术语分类，通过28个可组合模型和求解器实现亚秒级设计空间探索。

Result: 实现了亚秒级设计空间探索，能够识别绑定约束并综合出覆盖整个ML系统生命周期的理想硬件规格。

Conclusion: MLSYSIM为全栈ML系统架构推理提供了一种快速、可溯源、维度严谨的分析工具，填补了经验性分析与周期精确模拟之间的空白。

Abstract: As machine learning shifts from laboratory curiosity to critical infrastructure, the systems that sustain it span an extraordinary range, from sub-milliwatt microcontrollers to multi-gigawatt datacenter fleets. Reasoning across this range is hard: empirical profiling requires the target hardware in hand, while cycle-accurate simulation costs hours per configuration, leaving no tool for rapid, full-stack architectural reasoning. We present MLSYSIM (Machine Learning Systems Infrastructure Modeling), a first-principles analytical framework that formalizes the "physics of systems" into a dimensionally-strict Python engine. MLSysim is built on a demand-supply abstraction that decouples computational demand from silicon supply and environmental context, and it enforces unit integrity at runtime so the silent conversion errors that plague ad-hoc modeling cannot occur. Every input is drawn from a typed, provenance-tracked registry, so no number enters an analysis without a documented source. On this engine we codify a taxonomy of 22 "Systems Walls" resolved by 28 composable models and solvers, enabling sub-second design-space exploration that identifies binding constraints and synthesizes ideal hardware specifications across the entire ML systems lifecycle.

</details>


### [248] [From Tensor Buffer to Distributed Memory Hierarchy: A Survey of KV Cache Management for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02574)
*Jie Li,Tongyang Wang,Yong Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对LLM服务中的KV缓存管理系统进行了系统分类，从四个维度（局部性、生命周期、所有权、底层设施）分析了30多个系统，归纳出五种架构原型，并指出了当前评估中缺失的七个关键测量指标及对应的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存已从临时张量演变为LLM服务中的一级内存对象，现有系统众多但缺乏统一的分类框架来指导系统设计和比较。

Method: 采用四轴分类法（局部性、生命周期、所有权、底层设施）对30余个KV管理系统进行分析，归纳出五种架构原型，并审计现有评估方法。

Result: 归纳出五种架构原型：local-paged、disaggregated-pipeline、shared-store、memory-pool、hybrid-tier；发现在固定工作负载和硬件后，所有权是分布式系统间设计差异的主要来源；识别出七个缺失的KV特定测量指标。

Conclusion: 当前KV缓存管理评估存在系统性不足，七个缺失的测量指标对应了容错、隔离、分层驱逐、推测解码、MoE服务、共享缓存语义等开放问题，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become a first-order memory object in LLM serving rather than a temporary per-request tensor. This survey classifies more than thirty KV-management systems and frameworks using four axes: locality, lifetime, ownership, and substrate. The axes reveal five architectural archetypes -- local-paged, disaggregated-pipeline, shared-store, memory-pool, and hybrid-tier. Once workload and hardware are fixed, ownership accounts for much of the remaining design variance among distributed systems. The survey also audits current evaluations and identifies seven missing KV-specific measurements, linking them to open problems in fault tolerance, isolation, tiered eviction, speculative decoding, MoE serving, and shared-cache semantics.

</details>


### [249] [Scaling Weisfeiler-Leman Expressiveness Analysis to Massive Graphs with GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02603)
*Filippo Biondi,Mirco Tribastone,Max Tschaikowski*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性代数解释的1-WL测试稳定染色算法，包含随机化精炼算法（具有紧概率保证）和保持正确性的批处理方案，可将图分解为独立可处理的子图。CUDA实现在GPU上实现了相比经典CPU方法高达两个数量级的加速，并首次成功计算了超过300亿条边的网络规模图的稳定染色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1-WL测试的稳定染色是图神经网络表达能力的重要上界，但经典算法存在两个瓶颈：一是本质上串行，无法利用现代并行硬件；二是全局算法，需要全图内存，限制了在大规模真实实例上的应用。

Method: 利用1-WL稳定染色的线性代数解释，提出：(i) 随机化精炼算法，具有紧概率保证；(ii) 保持正确性的批处理方案，将图分解为可独立处理的子图，同时保证返回原图的稳定染色。该方法直接映射到GPU高效原语。

Result: CUDA实现相比经典CPU分区精炼算法加速高达两个数量级；首次成功计算超过300亿条边的网络规模图的稳定染色，而CPU基线超时或失败。

Conclusion: 该方法有效克服了1-WL稳定染色计算的串行和全局性瓶颈，利用GPU并行计算实现了大幅加速，使得在超大规模图上计算稳定染色成为可能。

Abstract: The stable coloring of the Weisfeiler-Leman (1-WL) test is a cornerstone of Graph Neural Networks because it provides an upper bound to the expressive power of message-passing architectures. Unfortunately, computing it presents two fundamental bottlenecks. First, classic algorithms are inherently sequential and cannot exploit modern massively parallel hardware. Second, these are \emph{global} algorithms, i.e., they require availability in memory of the full graph, severely limiting applicability to real-world instances. We leverage a linear-algebraic interpretation of 1-WL stable coloring and introduce two key contributions: (i)~a randomized refinement algorithm with tight probabilistic guarantees and (ii)~a correctness-preserving batching scheme that decomposes the graph into independently processable subgraphs while provably returning a stable coloring of the original graph. This approach maps directly to GPU-efficient primitives. In numerical experiments, our CUDA implementation delivers speedups up to two orders of magnitude over classical CPU-based partition refinement and, for the first time, successfully computes stable colorings on web-scale graphs with over 30 billion edges, where CPU baselines time out or fail.

</details>


### [250] [Fine-Grained Computation Offload for Off-the-Shelf Servers in Tens of Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02630)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出通过"重路由"（rerouting）而非重写（rewrite）来实现硬件加速器卸载（offload）与请求处理的重叠。核心思路是利用服务器已有的并发机制（请求挂起/恢复）和延迟响应原语，将卸载任务提交给执行器后挂起当前请求，待卸载完成再恢复。在10个现成服务器上仅需修改22-138行代码，恢复1.2-5.4倍性能；LD_PRELOAD实现可在未修改的线程-连接二进制上获得17.3倍加速。文章还分析了重路由破坏run-to-completion原子性带来的竞态风险，并提出透明页保护检测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件加速器（GPU、FPGA、远程安全模块、后量子KEM、推理服务器等）已成为在线服务的关键路径。对于微秒到毫秒级的细粒度卸载，传统的上下文切换和忙等待均不可行（前者代价与卸载相当，后者浪费核心）。现有解决方案需要重写异步框架、更换运行时或数据平面OS、手工调优集成，成本高昂。作者观察到并发已经存在于每个服务器中——处理并发请求的本质就是挂起和恢复请求，因此重叠卸载无需重写，只是一个路由问题。

Method: 方法分为三步：(1) 将卸载任务提交给执行器（executor）；(2) 利用服务器自带的延迟响应原语（deferred-response primitive）挂起当前请求；(3) 卸载完成后恢复请求。实验覆盖10种现成服务器，包含所有生产级并发模型（线程-连接、事件循环、协程等）。仅需修改22-138行代码，最多修改一个文件。极限情况通过LD_PRELOAD注入纤程运行时，在未修改的线程-连接二进制上实现重路由。针对重路由破坏run-to-completion原子性带来的竞态问题，建立了测量分类法，识别风险仅限于未加锁的共享聚合数据，并提出透明页保护检测方案（在Redis上验证）。

Result: 在10个现成服务器上，22-138行代码修改即可恢复1.2-5.4倍性能。服务器的并发模型和卸载的权重可预先预测性能增益。极限情况下LD_PRELOAD纤程运行时在未修改的线程-连接二进制上获得17.3倍加速。性能瓶颈受限于设备吞吐量和服务器自身的重叠容量。竞态风险被限定在未加锁的共享聚合数据，透明页保护检测方案在Redis上验证有效。

Conclusion: 硬件加速器卸载与请求处理的重叠无需重写整个系统架构，利用服务器已有的并发机制即可实现。这是一种路由问题而非重写问题，以极低的工程代价获得显著性能提升（1.2-5.4倍，极限17.3倍）。重路由破坏原子性带来的竞态风险可通过透明页保护有效检测和防护。该方法适用于各种并发模型的现成服务器，性能增益可预测。

Abstract: Hardware accelerators now sit on the critical path of online serving. GPUs, FPGAs, and increasingly remote services such as hardware security modules, post-quantum KEMs, and inference servers. For fine-grained offloads (microseconds to a few milliseconds) the classic responses to the resulting stall both fail: a context switch costs as much as the offload, and a busy-wait burns the core. Overlapping the offload with other requests is the fix, and prior systems obtain it by adding concurrency: an async-framework rewrite, a new runtime or dataplane OS, or a hand-tuned point integration.
  We observe that the concurrency already exists: serving concurrent requests is suspending and resuming them, so every server ships the machinery overlap needs. Overlap is then a routing problem, not a rewrite problem: submit the offload to an executor, suspend the request with the server's own deferred-response primitive, resume it on completion. Across ten off-the-shelf servers spanning every production concurrency model, this recipe takes 22-138 lines added, at most one modified, and recovers 1.2-5.4x on real hardware; the server's concurrency model and the offload's weight predict both numbers in advance, and the win is bounded by device throughput and the server's own overlap capacity. At the limit, an LD_PRELOAD fiber runtime injects the reroute into an unmodified thread-per-connection binary (17.3x) within a characterized envelope. Rerouting suspends run-to-completion atomicity; a measured taxonomy confines the hazard to unlocked shared aggregates, and a transparent page-protection detector guards exactly those, validated on stock Redis.

</details>


### [251] [Remora: Scale-out Deterministic Execution for Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02817)
*Zhengqing Liu,Alberto Sonnino,Igor Zablotchi,Eleftherios Kokoris Kogias,Marios Kogias*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Remora是一个面向现代模块化区块链的横向扩展智能合约执行引擎，采用非对称架构（集中式调度+分布式执行）、对象版本化与严格所有权模型，实现确定性横向扩展执行。通过无状态-有状态分离执行和局部感知调度，达到最高25万TPS吞吐量，延迟降低5ms，相比现有方案提升3倍性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代区块链采用共识与执行解耦的模块化架构，共识算法的进步使执行层成为新瓶颈；单节点验证器无法满足日益复杂和计算密集的智能合约执行需求，亟需横向扩展的执行引擎设计。

Method: 1) 非对称架构：集中式交易调度+分布式执行；2) 对象版本化方案与严格所有权模型，保证确定性横向扩展执行；3) 无状态-有状态分离：将状态无关任务与共识阶段重叠执行；4) 局部感知（locality-aware）和负载均衡的调度方案。

Result: 相比现有最优确定性执行方案，吞吐量提升3倍，扩展到25万TPS（匹配现代共识性能），延迟降低最多5ms，并能弹性适应突发工作负载和动态访问模式。

Conclusion: Remora通过创新的架构设计和执行优化，有效解决了区块链执行层的扩展瓶颈问题，证明了横向扩展执行引擎在实际工作负载下的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: Modern blockchains rely on a modular architecture that decouples consensus from execution. Recent advances in consensus algorithms have shifted the bottleneck to the execution layer, which must deterministically follow the consensus order and handle increasingly complex, compute-intensive smart contracts. We identify that single-node validators cannot keep up, motivating the need for a scale-out design. We design Remora, a scale-out smart contract execution engine. Remora adopts an efficient asymmetric architecture with centralized transaction dispatching and distributed execution, and depends on an object versioning scheme with a strict ownership model to guarantee deterministic scale-out execution. Remora achieves up to 3x throughput improvement compared to state-of-the-art deterministic execution schemes, scales up to 250k TPS, matching modern consensus performance, and reduces latency by up to 5ms. We also show that Remora elastically adapts to bursty workloads and dynamic access patterns using real-world traces. Remora's main performance benefits come from a novel stateless-stateful separation during smart contract execution, which overlaps the execution of state-independent tasks with consensus, and a new locality-aware and load-balanced scheduling scheme.

</details>


### [252] [Cassandra: Consensus with Partial Progress via Robust Partitionable View Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02856)
*Shaokang Xie,Dakai Kang,Junchao Chen,Suyash Gupta,Daniel P. Hughes,Mohammad Sadoghi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Cassandra是一种拜占庭容错共识协议，通过两阶段认证框架和独立推进的pacemaker机制，在网络分区时仍能保持部分进度（非零吞吐），并在恢复后合并分片链，同时保持与现有BFT协议相当的稳定性能（16副本90万TPS/0.31s延迟，104副本48万TPS/0.75s延迟）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BFT共识协议在网络分区时因无法形成强仲裁而丧失活性，无法推进任何有用工作。Cassandra旨在解决这一困境，使系统在分区期间仍能取得部分进展而不牺牲安全性。

Method: 提出两阶段认证框架，将可用性与提交解耦；引入无需强仲裁即可推进视图的pacemaker机制，并在关键路径外校准超时；通过PoA（Proof of Availability）支持分区期间的投机性吞吐，允许各分区独立扩展链，网络恢复后进行链合并与协调。

Result: 稳定条件下，16副本达90万TPS（延迟0.31s），104副本达48万TPS（延迟0.75s）。严重分区下，通过PoA支撑维持非零投机吞吐，可在恢复后协调合并。

Conclusion: Cassandra在保证BFT安全性的前提下，显著提升了网络分区时的可用性，且稳定环境下性能与主流BFT协议相当，兼具鲁棒性与效率。

Abstract: Replicated databases and permissioned blockchain systems rely on Byzantine Fault-Tolerant (BFT) consensus to maintain a globally consistent order of transactions across distributed replicas. These protocols preserve safety even under asynchrony, as they commit a transaction only after agreement among a strong quorum of replicas. During network partitions, however, when no strong quorum is reachable, they lose liveness and cannot make useful progress.
  In this paper, we present Cassandra, a consensus protocol that enables partial progress without sacrificing safety. Cassandra achieves this through a two-tier certification framework that decouples availability from commitment, allowing each partition to extend its own chain and reconcile these chains once the network is restored. To support this, Cassandra introduces a pacemaker that advances views without requiring a strong quorum and calibrates each replica's timeout off the critical path.
  Our evaluation results show that Cassandra remains competitive with state-of-the-art BFT protocols under stable conditions, sustaining 900K TPS at 16 replicas and 480K TPS at 104 replicas, with latency ranging from 0.31s at 16 replicas to 0.75s at 104 replicas. Under severe partitions, Cassandra maintains non-zero speculative throughput through PoA-backed progress, preserving work that can be reconciled once connectivity is restored.

</details>


### [253] [Agentic Orchestration of HPC Applications in Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02925)
*Vanessa Sochat,Daniel Milroy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文设计基于LLM的智能代理，实现HPC应用在云上从容器构建到Kubernetes部署、优化和扩展研究的全生命周期自动化，在21种实例类型上验证了线性扩展和性能优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs正在变革科研实践，推动人类工作向自主化系统转型，这一转变需要新的架构范式以及对LLM优缺点的深入理解。

Method: 设计智能代理，自动化完成HPC应用实验的全生命周期：容器创建与构建、Kubernetes部署、优化以及扩展研究编排。针对4个知名HPC应用构建多平台镜像，在21种Kubernetes实例类型上进行优化。

Result: 实现了经人类专家验证的线性扩展模式，改进了任务完成时间，并总结了智能代理设计与协作的最佳实践建议。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的代理系统能够有效自动化HPC云上工作流，实现线性扩展和性能优化，为自主化科研基础设施提供了可行的架构范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are serving as a catalyst of change for research practices, touching the daily lives of staff scientists, software engineers, and system administrators. The developments promise new degrees of autonomy, where categories of human work and decision making are replaced by autonomous, goal-oriented systems. This transition necessitates novel architectural paradigms and solid understanding of the strengths and limitations of LLMs. In this work, we design agents to intelligently deliver the entire life-cycle of an HPC application experimental run in cloud -- creation and build of a container, deployment in Kubernetes, optimization, and orchestration of a scaling study. We pursue this task for four well-known HPC applications to build multi-platform images and optimize across 21 instance types in Kubernetes. We demonstrate successful linear scaling with patterns approved by human experts, designs that improve work time to completion, and review suggested best practices for agentic design and collaboration.

</details>


### [254] [A Workflow-Aware Serving Layer for Agentic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02942)
*Jiayi Qian,Zishen Wan,Hanchen Yang,Chun Tao,Souvik Kundu,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Dyserve是一个工作流感知的服务层，通过整数线性规划(ILP)在异构后端池中为每个工作流节点联合优化模型选择、验证器选择和后端分配，并利用离线性能画像和动态负载调整策略来弥合模型服务引擎与智能体框架之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型服务引擎只能独立执行单个调用，看不到工作流结构；而智能体框架虽能编排工作流，但无法感知后端负载。两者都无法在运行时条件下为每个节点联合选择模型、验证器和后端，导致效率低下。

Method: Dyserve将工作流中每个节点的模型和验证器选择编译为整数线性规划(ILP)问题，在异构后端池上求解；利用技能条件化的离线性能画像跨工作流迁移；通过按模型吞吐量扫描让硬件信息仅通过该途径进入优化；加权策略将强模型和验证集中到错误传播最远的节点上；在准入时预求解多个压力级别的策略，并在负载变化时切换请求的未提交后缀，避免求解器处于负载切换路径上；失败工具调用触发一次性残差重解，保留已提交工作。

Result: 论文提出了Dyserve系统，但未在摘要中给出具体实验数值结果。

Conclusion: Dyserve通过工作流感知的服务层设计，实现了在异构后端上对模型、验证器和后端的联合优化，填补了模型服务引擎与智能体框架之间的空白，能够适应不同负载条件下的延迟-质量偏好。

Abstract: Agentic AI applications form an emerging serving workload in which a request creates a workflow: a directed acyclic graph of LLM and tool calls that exposes per-node model choices and optional quality operators such as verifiers. This workload falls between two existing layers. Model-serving engines execute individual calls efficiently but cannot see workflow structure, while agent frameworks fix the workflow but cannot see backend load, so neither jointly chooses each node's model, verifier, and backend under serving-time conditions.
  We present Dyserve, a workflow-aware serving layer that fills this gap. Dyserve compiles each workflow's per-node model and verifier choices in one integer linear program (ILP) over a heterogeneous backend pool, priced by skill-conditioned offline profiles that transfer across workflows. This couples with hardware entering only through per-model throughput sweeps, and is weighted to concentrate strong models and verification on the nodes whose errors propagate the furthest. Because no single latency-quality preference fits every workload mix, Dyserve pre-solves the program at several pressure levels at admission and shifts a workflow's uncommitted suffix among these strategies under load, keeping the solver off the load-shift path; a failed tool call triggers a one-time residual re-solve that preserves committed work.

</details>


### [255] [Cross-IP Request Coalescing: Relocating the Fan-out Point in Virtualized I/O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02969)
*Kiseok Kim,Hyeontae Joo,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对虚拟化环境中AI工作负载的存储I/O路径性能问题，提出跨IP请求合并（cross-IP request coalescing）方法。通过在SPDK vhost-user后端将客户机提交的多设备I/O单次请求分解并分发到各目标设备，将原有多次客-后端往返缩减为一次提交，在虚拟化环境中实现了最高1.78倍的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据中心AI工作负载规模增长，虚拟化层存储I/O路径性能成为关键瓶颈。单用户请求需跨越多个IP块（如存储、GPU、加速器），每个设备在虚拟化下都会引发独立的客-后端栈遍历，导致往返和上下文切换开销随设备数量累积。现有用户态内核旁路框架（如SPDK）虽优化了内核I/O路径，但仍存在每设备独立提交结构带来的开销。

Method: 提出跨IP请求合并技术：将扇出点从客户机侧迁移到SPDK vhost-user后端。客户机将多设备I/O作为单个复合请求提交，后端加速bdev将其分解并分发到各目标设备，以单次提交替代多次每设备客-后端往返传输。

Result: 在基于SPDK的虚拟化环境中，相比每设备基线方案，所提方法实现最高1.78倍的延迟降低，且优势随并发度增加而扩大。

Conclusion: 跨IP请求合并有效减少了虚拟化多设备场景下的I/O路径开销，为数据密集型AI工作负载的存储性能优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cloud data centers rely on virtualization technologies to serve AI workloads in multi-tenant environments. With the growing scale of data-intensive AI workloads, the performance of storage I/O paths at the virtualization layer has become a critical factor. A single user request often crosses multiple IP blocks, where functional units such as storage, GPU, and accelerator devices under virtualization fan out into separate stack traversals between the guest and the backend. As a result, round-trip and context-switching overheads accumulate with the number of devices. In this letter, we identify that a dominant factor in this overhead lies not in the kernel-mediated I/O path alone, but in the per-device submission structure itself, which persists even in user-space, kernel-bypass storage frameworks such as SPDK. To address this, we propose cross-IP request coalescing, which relocates the fan-out point from the guest to the SPDK vhost-user backend. The guest submits multi-device I/O as a single compound request, and the accelerated bdev at the backend decomposes and dispatches it to each target device, replacing multiple per-device guest-backend round trips with a single submission. Evaluation in an SPDK-based virtualized environment shows that the proposed approach achieves up to 1.78x lower latency than the per-device baseline, with the benefit growing as concurrency increases.

</details>


### [256] [HyperParallel-Mpipe: A Composable Algebra System for Optimizing MLLM Training over Supernode Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03229)
*Chong Li,Zhengdao Yu,Nelson Lossing,Thibaut Tachon,Pierre Leca,Etienne Filhol,Yujie Yuan,Chong Bao,Teng Su*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Mpipe通过调度代数派生出多模态感知的异构并行调度方案，将模态编码器计算重新映射到空闲的流水线区域，从而在Ascend 910C NPU集群上实现最高2.70倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型训练相比传统decoder-only LLM训练存在显著的MFU低下问题，亟需针对多模态场景特点优化训练效率。

Method: 提出Mpipe框架，利用调度代数从紧凑的调度规范派生出具体的运行时行为；基于该代数导出transpose方案——一种多模态感知的异构并行调度，将模态编码器计算重新映射到原本空闲的流水线区域。

Result: 在Ascend 910C NPU集群上，小规模设置下实现2.70倍加速，512卡大规模设置下实现1.21倍加速。

Conclusion: Mpipe通过调度代数和异构并行调度有效解决了多模态大模型训练中的流水线空闲问题，显著提升了训练效率。

Abstract: Modern AI applications have expanded beyond text-only interaction into a wide range of multimodal scenarios, making multimodal large language models (MLLMs) crucial for both research and industry. However, compared with traditional decoder-only LLM training, large-scale MLLM training often shows much lower MFU. We analyze the key pain points in MLLM training and introduce Mpipe, which uses a schedule algebra to derive concrete runtime behavior from a compact schedule specification. From this algebra, Mpipe derives transpose, a multimodal-aware heterogeneous parallel schedule that remaps modality-encoder computation into otherwise idle pipeline regions. On Ascend 910C NPU clusters, Mpipe achieves 2.70x speedup in a small-scale setting and 1.21x speedup in a 512-card large-scale setting.

</details>


### [257] [SPORK: Self-Speculative Forking to Accelerate Agentic LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03333)
*Huajun Bai,Weiwei Lv,Huichuan Zheng,Youyou Lu,Jiwu Shu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SPORK是一种无需训练的自我推测工具执行控制器，通过在模型生成思维链时提前分叉预测并执行工具调用，将工具执行与推理过程重叠，从而减少LLM Agent在思考-行动-观察循环中的GPU等待时间。实验表明，该方法在Qwen3-32B上将GAIA P95延迟降低18%，且不损失任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent的Thought-Action-Observation循环通常是串行的：模型推理、发出工具调用、GPU空闲等待结果返回。这种等待占工作负载中16-37%的墙钟时间，现有推测工具执行系统需要辅助预测器、历史轨迹或静态工作流图，缺乏免训练、开箱即用的部署方案。

Method: SPORK (Self-sPeculative fORKing) 包含四个核心组件：(1) 前缀缓存分叉（prefix-cache fork），降低探针成本；(2) 置信度门控（confidence gate），过滤错误预测；(3) 部分令牌接受（partial-token accept），将被拒绝的探针转化为推测解码草稿；(4) 成本模型，判断推测何时能达到盈亏平衡。系统在生成开始时创建一个分叉探针，预测即将调用的工具名称，提前执行工具调用，使其与剩余的思维链解码阶段重叠。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，分叉探针对Qwen3-32B即将调用的工具名称预测准确率达74.6-99.6%；在真实工具基准测试中，SPORK将Qwen3-32B的GAIA P95延迟从131.9秒降低至108.1秒（降低18%）；该机制在4B到32B的模型规模、密集和混合专家模型上均有效；任务准确率与基线相比在1个百分点以内或更好。

Conclusion: SPORK是一个薄控制器，部署在标准完成API之上，无需重新训练、无需辅助模型、无需离线轨迹，且与令牌级推测解码正交。作为一个训练免费、即插即用的方案，SPORK有效解决了LLM Agent中工具调用的等待延迟问题。

Abstract: LLM agents are becoming a common interface for research, coding, and question answering, yet their Thought-Action-Observation loop is often serial: the model reasons, emits a tool call, then idles the GPU until the result returns. This wait consumes 16-37% of wall time in our workloads and 35-61% in prior reports. Speculative tool execution can hide this wait, but existing systems need auxiliary predictors, historical traces, or static workflow graphs, leaving a gap for training-free, day-one deployment. We observe that the model can be its own predictor: a probe forked at the start of generation predicts Qwen3-32B's upcoming tool name with 74.6-99.6% accuracy across five benchmarks. We present SPORK (Self-sPeculative fORKing), a training-free controller that dispatches the speculated tool call early, overlapping its execution with the remaining chain-of-thought decode. A cost model captures when speculation breaks even, and each component improves one of its terms: a prefix-cache fork cuts probe cost, a confidence gate filters mispredictions, and partial-token accept turns rejected probes into speculative-decoding drafts. On acceptance, the tool result is ready when reasoning ends; on rejection, SPORK falls back to serial execution with no correctness penalty. On real-tool benchmarks, SPORK cuts Qwen3-32B's GAIA P95 by 18% (131.9 to 108.1 s); the mechanism holds across model sizes from 4B to 32B and across dense and mixture-of-experts models, with task accuracy within 1 pp of baseline or better wherever measured. SPORK deploys as a thin controller over standard completion APIs (no retraining, no auxiliary models, no offline traces) and is orthogonal to token-level speculative decoding. SPORK is open source at https://github.com/baihuajun24/spork.

</details>


### [258] [Scale-Free Beamforming using Swarm Arrays for Remote Sensing under Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03499)
*Bradley Hamilton,Raghu Mudumbai,Soura Dasgupta,Benjamin Peiffer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布式算法，用于自主中继群阵在无需先验信道知识的情况下计算最优迫零波束成形权重，该算法具有尺度无关性，计算和带宽开销完全独立于阵列规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在群阵中，中继节点需要协同转发期望信号并消除干扰，但传统方法需要先验信道知识，且计算和通信开销随阵列规模增长。本文旨在设计一种分布式的、尺度无关的算法来解决这一问题。

Method: 基于"集体阵列"概念，施加群约束：阵列元素只能集体地（而非单独地）与外部节点通信。在此限制下，设计分布式算法计算迫零波束成形权重，无需先验信道知识。

Result: 该算法实现了尺度无关性，计算和带宽开销不随阵列规模增长，同时对噪声和信道时变具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 尽管群约束是非常严格的限制，但它允许实现迫零波束成形等有用计算，并具备尺度无关性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: We consider a swarm array of autonomous relays that seek to cooperatively forward a desired signal to a fusion center with the maximum possible fidelity while canceling out a number of interferers. We present a distributed algorithm for computing the optimal zero-forcing beamforming weights at the relays without requiring prior channel knowledge. Crucially, our algorithm is {\it scale-free} in the sense that the computational and bandwidth overheads are completely independent of the size of the array. We build on recent work that introduced the concept of a Collective Array that enables such {\it scale-free} computation by imposing a constraint that the array must always function as a {\it swarm} i.e. array elements can only ever communicate with external nodes collectively and never individually. While this is a very severe restriction, we show that it allows useful computations such as zero-forcing beamforming while being robust to noise and channel time-variations.

</details>


### [259] [BrownoutMoE: Structure-Aware Expert Grouping for Efficient and Accurate LLM Web-based Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04164)
*Yi Ding,Minxian Xu,Zhengxin Fang,Kejiang Ye,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: BrownoutMoE提出了一种面向MoE大模型推理的结构感知优化框架，通过强化学习将专家重组为组，提升GPU利用率和吞吐量，同时最大程度减少精度损失。实验表明，与基线相比，精度损失降低71.4%，吞吐量提升2.24倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE推理系统主要优化运行时执行（如调度、通信重叠、内核融合），但未改变原始专家组织结构，导致热专家和冷专家访问严重不均衡，GPU并行度未被充分利用，限制了推理效率。

Method: 受服务计算中"brownout"范式启发，将MoE模型中的专家按层重组为分组；将分组策略建模为学习问题，使用强化学习搜索使精度损失最小的专家分组方案；引入分组一致的蒸馏过程，生成兼容标准推理管线的可直接部署模型。

Result: 相比基线方法，精度损失降低最多71.4%；吞吐量提升最多2.24倍。

Conclusion: BrownoutMoE通过结构层面（而非仅运行时层面）优化专家组织方式，有效解决了MoE推理中专家访问不均衡导致的效率问题，兼顾了推理效率与服务精度。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in Web-facing services, where inference must be both accurate and responsive under bursty demand. Although MoE models improve parameter efficiency through sparse expert activation, efficient MoE inference remains challenging in practice. A major reason is the highly imbalanced expert access pattern during inference: a few hot experts process most routed tokens, while many cold experts are rarely activated, leaving GPU parallelism underutilized. Existing systems mainly optimize runtime execution, such as scheduling, communication overlap, and kernel fusion, but usually preserve the original expert organization and therefore do not address the structural inefficiency caused by fragmented expert usage. In this paper, we present \textbf{BrownoutMoE}, a structure-aware optimization framework for efficient and accurate MoE inference services. Inspired by the brownout paradigm in service computing, BrownoutMoE reorganizes experts into groups to improve utilization and system efficiency while maintaining service quality. Specifically, we formulate layer-wise expert grouping as a learning problem and employ reinforcement learning to discover grouping strategies that minimize accuracy degradation. We further introduce a grouping-consistent distillation process to produce deployable models that are compatible with standard inference pipelines. Experimental results demonstrate that BrownoutMoE reduces accuracy degradation by up to 71.4% and improves throughput by up to 2.24x over baselines.

</details>


### [260] [CoCoScale: Leveraging Layer-wise Scaling to Unlock the Potential of Online LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04181)
*Jingfeng Wu,Yiyuan He,Minxian Xu,Xitong Gao,Chong Ma,Le Chen,Min Shen,Lin Qu,Kejiang Ye,CHengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCoScale，一种层级别动态扩展机制，通过将热点层的并行度弹性扩展到空闲设备上，实现细粒度资源调整，显著降低冷启动延迟（97.9%-99.3%），并在生产负载下降低平均延迟20.7%-28.1%，实现完全SLO达标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统在处理高度动态且偏斜的负载时，实例级扩缩容机制存在粗粒度资源调整的限制：扩容需要冷启动完整模型副本导致高延迟，缩容又使系统在突发流量下性能下降。

Method: 提出CoCoScale，一种层级别动态扩展机制，选择性地将热点层的并行度扩展到从低利用率设备回收的空闲资源上，实现弹性数据并行，无需改变模型架构或增加硬件开销。

Result: 相比传统扩容方案，冷启动延迟降低97.9%-99.3%；在生产负载下，平均延迟降低20.7%-28.1%，实现完全的SLO达标。

Conclusion: CoCoScale通过层级别细粒度弹性扩展，在不增加硬件成本的前提下，显著提升了LLM服务系统在动态偏斜负载下的自适应能力和资源效率。

Abstract: Online large language model (LLM) serving has become the backbone of modern AI applications, powering diverse downstream services through shared hardware clusters. However, modern serving systems frequently encounter highly dynamic workloads characterized by severe workload skewness, where a small fraction of model instances receives the vast majority of traffic. Existing instance-level scaling mechanisms are limited by coarse-grained resource adjustment: scaling up requires the cold-start of full-model replicas, incurring substantial latency, while scaling down leaves the system vulnerable to performance degradation during sudden traffic surges. The key insight of this work is that LLM serving offers a unique opportunity for fine-grained scaling. In this paper, we propose CoCoScale, a layer-wise dynamic scaling mechanism that selectively expands the parallelism of hot layers onto idle resources reclaimed from underutilized devices, enabling elastic data parallelism without altering model architectures or adding hardware overhead. Evaluations demonstrate that CoCoScale significantly reduces cold start latency by 97.9%-99.3% compared to traditional scale up. Under production traces, CoCoScale reduces average latency by 20.7\%--28.1\% and achieves full Service Level Objective (SLO) attainment, demonstrating superior dynamic adaptability and resource efficiency.

</details>


### [261] [Sangam: Efficiently Serving Diffusion LLMs with the AR Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04206)
*Nitin Kedia,Saurabh Agarwal,Myungjin Lee,Aditya Akella*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Sangam是一个针对扩散语言模型(dLLM)的推理服务系统，通过赤字令牌预算调度器和混合服务策略，解决了dLLM推理中预填-解码干扰和资源分区问题，在预填密集型和解码密集型工作负载上分别实现了8-20%和9-20%的平均延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型(dLLM)生成文本时采用迭代去噪掩码响应，其双向注意力机制阻止了精确的自回归式KV缓存。现有近似缓存技术（如Fast-dLLM和dKV-Cache）会重复刷新KV激活并在中间解码间复用，导致重复的预填/解码结构。这使得AR服务机制与dLLM相关但不能直接适用：dLLM解码是块级而非令牌级、预填会重复出现、双向注意力阻止了用于无阻塞协同服务的分块预填机制。因此需要专门为dLLM设计高效的推理服务系统。

Method: Sangam提出：(1) 赤字令牌预算调度器，优先接受进行中的解码，仅在累积令牌预算允许时接受完整不可分割的预填，并将未使用预算结转到下一轮，实现摊销式无阻塞调度；(2) 混合服务策略，将溢出的预填任务卸载到解码工作者上以缓解预填资源不足，并使用相同的赤字预算调度器保护这些工作者的解码不受溢出影响。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B ShareGPT解码密集型工作负载上，协同服务比混合执行降低平均延迟9-20%；在Dream-7B arXiv预填密集型工作负载上，混合执行比协同服务降低平均延迟8-20%。

Conclusion: dLLM服务设计空间与AR服务类似，受预填-解码干扰和预填/解码分区问题主导。Sangam通过赤字预算调度和混合服务策略有效应对了这些挑战，在不同工作负载类型上展示了各自的优势场景。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising a masked response and can commit multiple output positions per model invocation. Their bidirectional attention prevents exact autoregressive-style KV caching, since committing one position shifts the KV activations of all others. Approximate caching techniques such as Fast-dLLM and dKV-Cache refresh KV activations repeatedly and reuse them across intervening decodes, inducing a repeated prefill/decode structure. This makes AR serving mechanisms relevant to dLLMs, but not directly applicable. dLLM decodes are block-sized rather than token-sized, prefills recur, and bidirectional attention precludes the chunked prefill mechanism used for stall-free colocated serving. We present Sangam, a serving system for cached dLLM inference. Sangam introduces a deficit token-budget scheduler that admits in-flight decodes first, admits whole indivisible prefills only when the accumulated token budget allows, and carries unused budget forward. This achieves amortized stall-free scheduling. Disaggregated serving avoids prefill-decode interference but suffers from prefill/decode resource partitioning problem. Sangam adopts a hybrid serving strategy, overflowing prefills onto decode workers to relieve prefill under-provisioning, and uses the same deficit-budget scheduler to protect those workers' decodes from the overflow. We show that like AR serving, dLLM serving design space is governed by prefill-decode interference and prefill/decode partitioning. Colocated serving is most effective on decode-heavy workloads, cutting mean latency by 9-20% over hybrid execution on LLaDA-8B ShareGPT; while hybrid execution is most effective on prefill-heavy workloads, cutting mean latency by 8-20% over colocated execution on Dream-7B arXiv. Sangam is available at https://github.com/UT-InfraAI/sangam.

</details>


### [262] [Air-Plan: Query-Optimized Topology Selection for Over-the-Air Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04254)
*Kaushal Attaluri,Rebeca P. Diaz-Redondo,Manuel Fernandez Veiga*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AIRPLAN是一个面向空中计算去中心化联邦学习（OTA-DFL）的查询优化拓扑选择框架，它将拓扑选择形式化为基于代价的查询优化问题，利用Count-Min Sketch统计量估计工作负载特征，在候选拓扑中评估图感知代价模型，选择满足目标精度SLA且训练成本最小的通信图。实验表明，AIRPLAN在91.4%的工作负载中匹配了最优拓扑，开销小于1.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然空中计算（OTA）聚合已被广泛研究用于集中式联邦学习，但其与去中心化联邦学习（DFL）的集成尚未被充分探索，且现有工作中缺乏 principled 的通信拓扑选择方法。

Method: 1) 建立OTA-DFL与分布式查询处理之间的形式化等价关系；2) 将拓扑选择构建为基于代价的查询优化问题；3) 使用隐私保护的Count-Min Sketch统计量估计工作负载特征；4) 设计图感知代价模型评估候选拓扑；5) 选择满足目标精度SLA且训练成本最小的通信图。

Result: 在5个图族、3个视觉基准、4种客户端规模和多个SNR设置下，AIRPLAN在91.4%的工作负载中匹配了最优拓扑，引入的开销小于1.8%。此外，推导了拓扑感知稀疏化的理论误差界，证明连接良好的拓扑能更好地容忍激进压缩。

Conclusion: AIRPLAN引入了一种桥接无线联邦学习与分布式查询优化的系统视角，为OTA-DFL提供了 principled 的拓扑选择方法。

Abstract: Over-the-air (OTA) aggregation exploits the superposition property of wireless multiple-access channels to aggregate model updates from multiple devices within a single transmission slot, significantly reducing communication latency. While OTA computation has been extensively studied for centralized federated learning (FL), its integration with decentralized federated learning (DFL) remains largely unexplored, and principled communication topology selection is absent from existing work.
  We present AIRPLAN, a query-optimized topology selection framework for Over-the-Air Decentralized Federated Learning (OTA-DFL). AIRPLAN establishes a formal equivalence between OTA-DFL and distributed query processing, enabling topology selection to be formulated as a cost-based query optimization problem. Using privacy-preserving Count-Min Sketch statistics, AIRPLAN estimates workload characteristics, evaluates a graph-aware cost model across candidate topologies, and selects the communication graph that minimizes training cost while satisfying a target accuracy SLA.
  Experiments across five graph families, three vision benchmarks, four client scales, and multiple SNR settings show that AIRPLAN matches the oracle-optimal topology in 91.4% of workloads while introducing less than 1.8% overhead. We further derive theoretical error bounds for topology-aware sparsification, demonstrating that well-connected topologies better tolerate aggressive compression. AIRPLAN introduces a systems-oriented perspective that bridges wireless federated learning and distributed query optimization.

</details>


### [263] [GORIO: GPU-Centered Remote I/O for Graph ANNS over NVMe-oF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04415)
*Gen Zhang,Wenhao Gu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GORIO是一种面向图ANNS的GPU中心化远程I/O系统，通过将查询状态与决策保留在GPU上、CPU仅作为NVMe-oF传输代理，实现了比现有方案快1.31倍至3.73倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有远程存储路径以CPU为中心，但图ANNS的下一次数据访问由GPU图遍历发现，CPU中心的I/O路径与GPU遍历不匹配，导致性能瓶颈。

Method: GORIO将查询演化、缺页生成、待查状态和恢复决策保留在GPU上；设计两层架构：(1) GPU直接远程I/O路径，将本地缺页转换为分阶段远程操作；(2) ANNS特定的调度机制，重叠图遍历与远程页面服务。

Result: 在SIFT1M DiskANN风格图ANNS工作负载上，GORIO比最先进远程I/O参考路径快1.31倍，比直接远程页面缓存路径快3.73倍。

Conclusion: GORIO证明了GPU中心化的远程I/O架构对图ANNS的有效性，为向量数据库和RAG服务中的大规模图ANNS提供了可行的存储解耦方案。

Abstract: Graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) is increasingly used in vector databases and retrieval-augmented generation services, but large vector indexes often exceed the memory capacity of a single GPU server. NVMe over Fabrics (NVMe-oF) provides an attractive storage-disaggregation substrate, yet existing remote storage paths are still largely CPU-centered: the CPU forms I/O requests, drives transport progress, and determines when GPU computation can resume. This organization is poorly matched to graph ANNS, where the next data access is discovered inside GPU graph traversal.
  This paper presents GORIO, a system study that extends GPU-centered local I/O to remote storage and specializes the resulting substrate for graph ANNS over NVMe-oF. GORIO keeps query evolution, page-miss generation, pending-query state, and resume decisions on the GPU, while the CPU acts only as an NVMe-oF transport and completion proxy. The design has two layers: a GPU-direct remote I/O path that turns local page-cache misses into split-phase remote operations, and ANNS-specific scheduling mechanisms that overlap graph traversal with remote page service. On a SIFT1M DiskANN-style graph workload over an RDMA NVMe-oF path, GORIO is 1.31$\times$ faster than the state-of-the-art remote-I/O reference path and 3.73$\times$ faster than the direct remote page-cache path. These results demonstrate a concrete GPU-centered remote I/O substrate for graph ANNS.

</details>


### [264] [Adaptive Space-efficient Collectives for Dynamic and Unstructured Sparsity on GPU Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04676)
*Lannie Dalton Hough,Emir Gencer,Hoffmann Muki,Abhinav Bhatele*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用稀疏性加速集体通信的新方法，设计了基于位向量格式Pici的三种集体通信原语（all-gather、reduce-scatter、all-reduce），在99%稀疏度下相比NCCL实现最高5.25倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集体通信库（如NCCL）仅针对稠密数据优化，处理稀疏数据时存在通信冗余（传输零值）以及格式转换开销和缩减操作导致的稠密化问题，亟需高效的稀疏感知通信方案。

Method: 提出基于位向量（bitvector）的轻量级格式Pici，支持低开销的快速压缩/解压；设计自适应稀疏度的集体通信算法，在通信过程中动态调整数据表示形式以平衡传输开销与计算开销。

Result: 在99%输入稀疏度下，all-gather、reduce-scatter和all-reduce分别实现高达5.25倍、2.5倍和2.66倍相对于NCCL的加速。

Conclusion: 通过Pici格式和自适应算法，有效解决了稀疏数据通信中的冗余传输和稠密化问题，显著提升稀疏场景下的集体通信性能。

Abstract: High-performance collective communication primitives are necessary for a variety of high performance computing (HPC) and machine learning (ML) workloads. State-of-the-art collective communication libraries such as NCCL optimize exclusively for dense data. However, when sending sparse data, we can reduce communication volume by not sending zeros. Unfortunately, explicitly handling sparsity introduces challenges such as format conversion overheads and densification during collectives that involve reductions. In this paper, we introduce sparsity-exploiting algorithms for three collectives that address these challenges: all-gather, reduce-scatter, and all-reduce. Our collective implementations are backed by a new bitvector-based format, Pici, designed for low overhead and fast (de)compression at moderate sparsities. Further, our algorithms adapt to the level of sparsity in data, modifying its representation during the course of the collective. At 99% input sparsity, our collectives achieve up to 5.25x, 2.5x, and 2.66x speedups over NCCL for all-gather, reduce-scatter, and all-reduce, respectively.

</details>


### [265] [Direct Model State Migration for Elastic Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04749)
*Weijian Liu,Mingzhen Li,Rui Kang,Chen Sun,Guangming Tan,Weile Jia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ETC是一个无检查点的状态迁移框架，通过利用状态局部性和直接P2P通信，实现弹性混合并行LLM训练，相比检查点方案减少2.33-6.37倍迁移开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检查点的状态迁移机制需要将完整状态持久化到存储后再恢复，导致所有GPU在迁移期间停滞，即使经过优化仍因跨内存层次的数据移动而产生高昂延迟。

Method: 提出ETC框架：1) 利用状态局部性最小化GPU间数据移动，用直接P2P通信替代存储持久化；2) 通过通信融合消除节点碎片化；3) 集成到Megatron-LM中。

Result: 在不同并行配置下，相比基于检查点的方案，迁移开销降低2.33倍至6.37倍。

Conclusion: ETC通过高效的状态迁移，使弹性训练在生产环境中变得可行。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) training shall adapt to dynamic resources in shared clusters to tackle the elasticity, including passive preemption and optimistic scaling. State migration across device sets is required when altering the hybrid-parallel configuration due to dynamic resources. Existing solutions rely on checkpoint-based mechanisms, which persist complete states to storage for resuming with re-assigned resources, forcing all GPUs to stall when transferring model states. Despite optimization efforts, checkpoint-based solutions incur prohibitive latency due to data movement across memory hierarchies.
  We propose ETC, a checkpoint-free state migration framework for elastic hybrid-parallel LLM training. We exploits the state locality to minimize inter-GPU data movement, replacing storage persistence with direct peer-to-peer communication. Besides, we eliminate node fragmentation through communication coalescing. Integrated with Megatron-LM, ETC reduces migration overhead by 2.33$\times$ to 6.37$\times$ compared to checkpoint-based solutions across diverse parallel configurations. By enabling efficient migration, ETC unlocks practical elastic training in production environments.

</details>


### [266] [Orcaella: Hybrid Fault Tolerance with Client-Selectable Finality Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04789)
*Lefteris Kokoris-Kogias,Alberto Sonnino*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在混合拜占庭-崩溃错误模型下，研究了状态机复制中的两消息延迟提交的容错界限，证明了n ≥ 5f+3c+1的紧界，并提出了一个更鲁棒的提交路径，在四消息延迟下可容忍额外的f_abc < n-3f-2c的活-但-腐化错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协议如Minimmit需要n ≥ 5f+1的强假设，而Hydrangea和Kudzu虽处理混合错误但主要关注乐观快速路径。本文旨在混合模型下精确刻画两消息延迟提交的容错极限，并探索更高效的容错路径。

Method: 理论分析与紧界证明：先证明了两消息延迟提交的紧界n ≥ 5f+3c+1；然后通过扩展设计，提出了一个四消息延迟的提交路径，可容忍额外f_abc个活-但-腐化错误；在活性违规时切换到同步恢复模式，仅保留鲁棒路径的安全保证。

Result: 对于f=16, c=6, n=99，获得一个提交路径：活性容忍22%节点失败，1-RTT安全容忍16%节点作恶，2-RTT安全容忍54%节点作恶。

Conclusion: 本文在混合错误模型下给出了两消息延迟提交的紧容错界，并通过多路径设计在保证安全的前提下提升了整体系统的容错能力，特别是在不同延迟条件下提供了差异化的容错保证。

Abstract: Classical partially synchronous state machine replication, as in PBFT, tolerates f Byzantine replicas among n at least 3f+1 using three communication steps per request. Recent protocols such as Minimmit achieve two-message-delay decisions under stronger size assumptions, notably n at least 5f+1 when any silent replica must be counted as a potential equivocator. Hydrangea and Kudzu treat mixed Byzantine and crash faults, focusing on providing a fast-path under optimistic conditions while maintaining a fall-back commitment path similar to PBFT. In this paper, we also consider a mixed model, but focus on studying the fault tolerance of the 2-message-delay commit. For this, we prove a tight bound of n at least 5f+3c+1. Extending this result, we also show that there exists a more resilient commit path that allows an extra f_abc < n-3f-2c alive-but-corrupt faults at 4-message-delays. Core liveness is claimed in executions with at most f equivocators; if this regime is violated (e.g., AbC-induced forks), the protocol enters synchronous recovery, where only the resilient-path safety guarantee is preserved. As a result, for f=16, c=6, and n=99, we obtain a commit path that tolerates 22% of replicas failing for liveness, 16% equivocating for 1-RTT safety, and 54% equivocating for 2-RTT safety.

</details>


### [267] [Performance evaluation of scheduling tasks in many-core systems utilizing processes and threads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04821)
*Mejgan Dedaj,Argyro Gailla,Theofanis Ioannou,Stamatia Kastrinaki,Hermione Kimpouropoulou,Dimitrios Kontodimos,Kleopatra Kontogianni,Sotirios Kontogiannis,Michail Panagiotidis Kannas,Anastasia Papouda,Anna Maria Sidiropoulou,George Tavridis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了多核共享内存系统上基于进程和线程的调度器在处理大型三维张量行排序工作负载时的可扩展性。实验表明，线程调度器性能最优，其中动态和guided调度效果最佳；进程调度器中，pipe-based设计可扩展性最强，小负载用一对一pipe，大负载用多对多pipe。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多核共享内存系统上内存密集型行排序任务，评估不同进程级和线程级调度策略的可扩展性与性能表现。

Method: 使用三维张量的行快速排序作为内存密集型工作负载，在24核x86-64平台上比较了多种进程调度器（bounded prolific、bounded collective、三种pipe-based producer-consumer）和线程调度器（static、dynamic、guided、chunk-based、chunk-stealing、adaptive chunk、AIMD）。

Result: 线程调度器整体性能最优，dynamic和guided调度实际效果最佳；进程调度器中pipe-based设计可扩展性最强，一对一pipe适合小负载，多对多pipe适合大负载。

Conclusion: 轻量级线程调度是共享内存行排序的最优选择；AIMD/adaptive调度和pipe-based进程调度在争用感知、显式进程间协调和分布式异构负载管理中仍有重要价值。

Abstract: This study assesses the scalability of process-based and thread-based schedulers for many-core shared-memory systems using a memory-intensive row-wise quick-sort workload on large three-dimensional tensors. The process-based evaluation considers bounded prolific, bounded collective, and three pipe-based producer-consumer schedulers: one-to-one, one-to-many, and many-to-many. These pipe schedulers dynamically stream task identifiers to worker processes, exchanging increased inter-process communication overhead for enhanced runtime load balancing and flexible chunk-based task dispatching. The thread-based evaluation examines static, dynamic, guided, chunk-based, chunk-stealing, adaptive chunk, and AIMD adaptive scheduling strategies. The AIMD scheduler employs an additive-increase multiplicative-decrease policy inspired by TCP congestion control, utilizing an exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA) of CPU utilization to regulate a contention window that limits the number of concurrently active chunks. The adaptive chunk scheduler further modifies chunk size based on observed per-thread execution speed. Experimental results on a 24-core x86-64 platform indicate that thread schedulers deliver the highest overall performance, with dynamic and guided scheduling yielding the most favorable practical outcomes. Among process schedulers, pipe-based designs demonstrate the strongest scalability, with one-to-one pipes excelling for smaller workloads and many-to-many pipes preferred for larger workloads. In summary, lightweight thread scheduling is optimal for shared-memory row sorting, while AIMD/adaptive scheduling and pipe-based process scheduling remain valuable for contention-aware execution, explicit inter-process coordination, and distributed-style heterogeneous workload management.

</details>


### [268] [No Distributed Quantum Advantage for 3-Coloring Rooted Trees and 2-Coloring Even Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04852)
*Pierre Fraigniaud,Frédéric Magniez,Isabella Ziccardi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明量子资源在分布式计算中的 LOCAL 模型下，对于3染色有根树和2染色偶长环问题无法带来加速，分别达到了与经典算法相同的 Ω(log* n) 和 n/2-1 轮下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨量子资源能否在分布式计算中帮助克服网络中的局域性约束，特别是针对有根树的3染色问题（Coiteux-Roy 等人未解决的情况）以及偶长环的2染色问题（改进已有下界）。

Method: 使用颜色提升（color lifting）技术，该技术类似 Linial 的经典论证，将 Δ-ary 树的3染色下界归约到 Ω(log* Δ)，进而得到有根树的 Ω(log* n) 下界；对于偶长环的2染色，通过量子-LOCAL 模型分析得到 n/2-1 轮下界。

Result: 1) 量子-LOCAL 模型中，以至少 1-O(1/log n) 概率正确3染色 n 节点有根树需要 Ω(log* n) 轮；2) 以至少 1-1/Δ 概率正确3染色任意 Δ-ary 树需要 Ω(log* Δ) 轮；3) 即使有预共享纠缠，以至少 1-O(1/n) 概率正确2染色 n 节点偶长环需要 n/2-1 轮。

Conclusion: 量子资源（无论是否有预共享纠缠）对于有根树的3染色和偶长环的2染色问题无法提供任何优势，其下界与经典确定性算法匹配甚至更紧。

Abstract: Significant effort has been devoted over the past decade to understanding whether quantum resources can provide advantages in distributed computing, and in particular whether they can help overcome locality constraints in networks, typically in Linial's LOCAL model. Recently, Coiteux-Roy~et~al.~(STOC 2024) showed that quantum resources do not help for 3-coloring \textit{unrooted} trees: in particular, their lower bound holds in the stronger \textit{non-signaling} model, which formalizes the principle of physical causality in distributed computing. The case of \textit{rooted} trees, however, was left open by their work.
  For rooted trees, the deterministic Cole-Vishkin algorithm 3-colors $n$-node trees in $O(\log^\star n)$ rounds, matching Linial's classical $Ω(\log^\star n)$ lower bound (FOCS 1987). In this paper, we show that any algorithm in quantum-LOCAL (without pre-shared entanglement) that properly 3-colors $n$-node rooted trees with probability at least ${1-O(1/\log n)}$ must perform $Ω(\log^\star n)$ rounds. That is, quantum resources provide no advantage for 3-coloring rooted trees. To get this result, we show a lower bound of $Ω(\log^\star Δ)$ for 3-coloring any $Δ$-ary tree with success probability at least $1-1/Δ$. The proof uses a \textit{color lifting} technique that bears similarity to Linial's original argument.
  We also show, as a separate result, that 2-coloring even-length $n$-node cycles with probability $1-O(1/n)$ requires $n/2-1$ rounds in the quantum-LOCAL model, even with pre-shared entangled states. This improves the previously known $\lceil (n-2)/4 \rceil$ lower bound of Gavoille, Kosowski, and Markiewicz (DISC 2009) by a factor of two, and shows that quantum algorithms cannot save even a single round over classical deterministic algorithms for 2-coloring even-length cycles.

</details>


### [269] [Symmetry all the way down](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04887)
*Ignacio Amores-Sesar,Christian Cachin,Simon Holmgaard Kamp,Juan Villacis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明非对称信任虽然能容忍更多失效模式，但在解决需要深度≥2的任务（如可靠广播、共识）时，并不比经典对称仲裁系统更强；其额外能力仅限于深度≤1的简单任务（如一致性广播）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对称信任允许每个进程指定自己的失效假设，从而比对称仲裁系统容忍更多的失效场景，但尚不清楚在这些额外场景中是否真能解决分布式任务，以及能解决哪些任务。

Method: 利用非对称信任的深度层次（Amores-Sesar et al., OPODIS~'25）对任务所需深度进行刻画；构造一个编译器，将任意拜占庭非对称仲裁系统转化为对称仲裁系统，并证明该转化在深度≥2的任务中保持可解性；通过紧性证明表明深度≤1的场景无法被编译器处理。

Result: 对于任意拜占庭非对称仲裁系统，每个允许解决深度≥2任务的失效场景，都能被适当地构造的拜占庭对称仲裁系统所容忍；而非对称信任独有容忍的额外失效模式仅能解决深度≤1的任务，且该结果是紧的。

Conclusion: 非对称信任严格扩大了可容忍的失效模式集合，但在解决需要深度≥2的任务时并未提供额外能力，这精确刻画了非对称信任的计算能力边界。

Abstract: Asymmetric trust generalizes classical symmetric quorum systems by allowing each process to specify its own failure assumptions. While this flexibility enables tolerance of strictly more failure scenarios, it is not known if, in these cases, it is actually possible to solve distributed tasks, and if so, which.
  We answer this question using the depth hierarchy for asymmetric trust (Amores-Sesar et al., OPODIS~'25), which characterizes how much a process must rely on others to solve a task. We prove that asymmetric trust does not increase the solvability of tasks requiring depth two or more, such as reliable broadcast or consensus. Specifically, for any Byzantine asymmetric quorum system, every failure scenario that permits solving a task requiring depth at least two can also be tolerated by a suitably constructed Byzantine symmetric quorum system. We show this via a compiler that transforms asymmetric quorum systems into symmetric ones.
  The additional failure patterns tolerated exclusively by asymmetric trust correspond to scenarios in which only simpler tasks requiring depth one or less (such as consistent broadcast) can be solved. We further prove that this result is tight in the depth hierarchy, meaning that there exist no compilers that produce symmetric quorum systems that are valid also in failure scenarios where correct processes have depths one or less.
  Our results clarify the precise power of asymmetric trust. While it strictly enlarges the set of tolerable failure patterns, it does not provide additional strength for solving tasks requiring depth two or higher.

</details>


### [270] [TARE: Tail Aware Evaluation of HPC Job Runtime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04935)
*Haili Xiao,Can Wu,Shasha Lu,Xiaoning Wang,Yining Zhao,Rong He*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对HPC作业运行时间预测的尾部分布评估方法（GeoAccuracy加权资源使用量+十分位数/分割分析），发现用户提供的预估时间（UserReq）在尾部分布中表现优于机器学习模型，并据此设计混合调度策略（XGBoost用于多数作业，UserReq用于顶部十分位），在线回放使平均等待时间降低8%，回填作业增加50%-115%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于均值准确率的评估方式在重尾工作负载下会低估少数大作业对调度的实际影响，需要一种聚焦尾部分布的评估方法才能真实反映预测质量与调度性能之间的关系。

Method: 采用GeoAccuracy（按资源使用量加权）、十分位数分析和分割分析来评估预测模型；对比XGBoost、Last2与UserReq在NREL Eagle和ALCF Mira/Intrepid三个生产数据集上的表现；基于离线分析结果设计混合调度策略，并在四个生产队列上进行在线回放验证。

Result: 基于均值准确率各方法差距不大，但GeoAccuracy显示出明显分离，UserReq在顶部十分位上的GeoAccuracy最高且低估率最低，该模式在滚动分割中稳定。混合策略在线回放使平均等待时间降低8%，回填作业增加50%-115%。

Conclusion: 离线评估聚焦尾部分布能更好刻画与调度相关的预测质量，并能有效指导调度策略的设计。

Abstract: Runtime estimates affect reservation quality, backfilling opportunities, and queue delay in HPC schedulers. Under heavy tailed workloads, however, averaging over jobs can misrepresent scheduling impact because a small fraction of jobs dominates resource usage. This paper presents an empirical evaluation methodology for HPC job runtime prediction that focuses on the tail, combining GeoAccuracy weighted by resource usage with decile and split analyses. Using production traces from NREL Eagle and ALCF Mira/Intrepid, we compare XGBoost and Last2 against the user provided walltime estimate at submission (UserReq). Across all three datasets, evaluation focused on the tail changes the offline conclusion: MeanAccuracy keeps the methods relatively close, whereas GeoAccuracy reveals clearer separation and makes UserReq's strength in the upper tail visible. In the top decile, UserReq achieves the highest GeoAccuracy and lowest underestimation rate on all three datasets, and this pattern remains stable across rolling splits. We then translate this signal into a simple hybrid scheduling policy that keeps XGBoost for most jobs and routes the top decile by proxy_cost at submission to UserReq. Online replay on four production queues reduces mean wait time by up to 8% and increases backfilled jobs by 50% to 115%. These results show that offline evaluation focused on the tail better characterizes prediction quality relevant to scheduling and informs scheduling policy design.

</details>


### [271] [When Words Predict Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04951)
*Anubhab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对分布式LLM调度器在EPO专利文本（受Article 84 EPC约束）中因语言刚性与人类/机器 authorship 统计不可分导致的VRAM崩溃问题，提出CPU侧的语言资源预测（LRF）网关。该网关提取16维文本结构向量，使用XGBoost预测陷阱带成员概率，并结合动态闭式路由阈值进行请求分流，实现边缘GPU内存分配前的前置决策。6000请求实测显示，误路由率降至0.087-0.095（基线0.849），峰值VRAM稳定在4.82 GiB（上限8 GiB），AUROC达0.84，动态阈值相比静态阈值减少8.2%误路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分布式LLM调度器依赖静态token计数或滚动延迟均值，在面对法定约束文本（如EPO专利权利要求）时发生失效。此类文本的语言刚性导致人机 authorship 统计不可区分，中途消歧迫使动态多模型集成扩展，引发不可预测的KV-cache和权重分配激增，导致消费级边缘GPU VRAM饱和并出现严重OOM崩溃。

Method: 提出CPU侧Linguistic Resource Forecasting (LRF)网关，在边缘GPU内存分配前对请求进行前置分析。方法包括：(1) 提取16维文本结构向量；(2) 使用XGBoost预测器预测陷阱带成员概率；(3) 通过实时延迟遥测计算动态闭式路由阈值；(4) 根据预测概率与阈值的比较，将请求路由至本地Qwen2.5-7B边缘worker或远程对比集成模型。

Result: 6000请求实测中，LRF网关将误路由率降至0.087-0.095（token计数基线为0.849），降低一个数量级。峰值边缘VRAM稳定在4.82 GiB（低于8 GiB上限），在27倍WAN延迟变化下保持稳定。XGBoost预测器AUROC达0.84，动态阈值相比静态阈值减少8.2%误路由。

Conclusion: LRF网关通过前置语言资源预测和动态路由决策，有效解决了法定约束文本场景下分布式LLM调度中的VRAM崩溃问题，在误路由率和资源边界控制上均显著优于传统方法。该方案适用于消费级边缘GPU环境，具有良好的鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: Standard distributed \ac{llm} schedulers rely on static token counts or rolling latency averages, making them susceptible to failures on statutorily constrained text. On \ac{epo} claims governed by Article 84 \ac{epc}, linguistic rigidity makes human and machine authorship statistically indistinguishable. Resolving this ambiguity mid-flight forces dynamic multi-model ensemble expansion, triggering unpredictable KV-cache and weight-allocation spikes that saturate consumer-grade edge GPU VRAM and cause severe \ac{oom} crashes. To prevent hardware collapse, we propose a CPU-side Linguistic Resource Forecasting (LRF) gateway. The gateway extracts a 16-dimensional text-structure vector and applies an XGBoost predictor to forecast trap-band membership. The resulting escalation probability ($\Pesc$) is evaluated against a dynamic, closed-form routing threshold ($\Tauroute(t)$) computed via real-time latency telemetry. Requests are safely routed to either a local Qwen2.5-7B edge worker or a remote contrastive ensemble (Qwen2.5 7B + 32B) on an NVIDIA H100 \emph{before} any edge GPU memory is allocated. In a 6,000-request live trial, the LRF gateway reduced the operational misroute fraction ($R_{\mathrm{mis}}$) to $0.087$--$0.095$, an order of magnitude below the token-count baseline ($0.849$). Peak edge VRAM remained safely bounded at $\SI{4.82}{\gibi\byte}$ (under the $\SI{8}{\gibi\byte}$ ceiling) across a $27\times$ variation in \ac{wan} delay. The predictor achieved a live-trial AUROC of $0.84$, and the dynamic $\Tauroute(t)$ controller yielded an $8.2\%$ relative reduction in misroutes compared to an equivalent static threshold.

</details>


### [272] [Communication-Aware Placement and Pruning for Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05116)
*Xiao Shi,Yingying Sun,Jiangsu Du,Zhiguang Chen,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CAP框架，通过专家放置和剪枝优化MoE模型分布式推理中的通信效率，在单节点和多节点实验中实现1.23x-1.86x吞吐量提升，并在有损加速下保持更好模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型扩展到数百专家后，放置和剪枝决策显著影响通信量，进而影响分布式推理性能，现有方法未充分协同考虑计算、通信和精度三者关系。

Method: 提出CAP框架，包含三个组件：(1) 共激活驱动的专家放置，将频繁共激活的专家分组以减少通信；(2) 通信-计算权衡调整，生成不同计算负载和通信量的放置方案；(3) 通信感知的专家剪枝，选择性移除路由目标以降低通信并控制精度损失。

Result: 在单节点和多节点实验中，相比DeepSeek EPLB和vLLM顺序放置，吞吐量提升1.23x-1.86x；在有损加速场景下，同等目标加速比下保持更好的模型精度。

Conclusion: CAP框架通过联合优化专家放置和剪枝，有效平衡计算、通信与精度，为不同硬件配置提供高效的MoE推理策略。

Abstract: As MoE models scale to hundreds of experts, placement and pruning decisions increasingly dictate communication volume, affecting the performance of distributed inference across GPUs and nodes. We propose CAP (Communication-Aware Assignment and Pruning), a framework that considers computation, communication and accuracy together for efficient MoE inference through expert placement and pruning. It consists of three components: (1) Co-activation driven expert placement, which groups frequently co-activated experts to reduce inter-device and inter-node communication; (2) Communicationcomputation trade-off adjustment, which generates placements with different computational load and communication volume; and (3) Communication-aware expert pruning, which selectively removes routing destinations to reduce communication with limited accuracy degradation. By combining these components, CAP selects an efficient operating strategy for different hardware configurations. Across our single-node and multi-node experiments, it achieves 1.23x - 1.86 x throughput improvement over DeepSeek EPLB and sequential placement in vLLM, and preserves better model accuracy at the same target speedup under lossy acceleration.

</details>


### [273] [CATs: Secure Blockchain Interoperability with Cross-chain Atomic Transactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05387)
*Andreas Penzkofer,Franck Cassez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨链原子交易（CAT）协议，通过共享协调层实现不同区块链间的可组合原子执行，在异步通信和拜占庭行为下提供原子性保证，同时保持链自治性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决跨区块链互操作中的关键挑战：在异步通信和拜占庭节点存在的情况下，提供跨链交易的原子性保证，同时保持各链的自治性。

Method: 设计了包含排序器、交易处理器、协调器和确认层的共享协调层架构；将交易执行分为接受集和推迟集；引入超时机制和依赖深度界限来保证活性和减少级联延迟。

Result: 形式化分析证明了强安全性和活性保证；实验评估表明当跨链交易占比适中时CAT成功率高，并揭示了CAT成功率与依赖交易延迟之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该协议实现了快速、安全、确定性的跨链原子执行，同时保持链自治性，为可扩展的区块链互操作性解决方案奠定了基础。

Abstract: We propose a protocol for cross-chain atomic transactions (CATs), enabling composable atomic execution across different blockchains. The protocol addresses the key interoperability challenge of providing atomicity guarantees in the presence of asynchronous communication and Byzantine actors. It preserves chain autonomy by allowing each blockchain to maintain its own execution model while participating in coordinated cross-chain operations. The design introduces a shared coordination layer involving sequencers, transaction processors, a coordinator, and a confirmation layer which together ensure that either all parts of a CAT succeed or none do.
  To prevent unnecessary blocking, we separate transaction execution into accepted and postponed sets, with the coordination layer resolving the outcomes of CATs within a few rounds. We further introduce timeouts and dependency-depth bounds for liveness and mitigation of cascading delays. Our formal analysis establishes strong safety and liveness guarantees and demonstrates that the protocol achieves minimal blocking for independent transactions while ensuring bounded blocking time for dependent transactions. Experimental evaluation shows high CAT success when cross-chain transactions are a modest share of traffic, and characterizes the CAT-lifetime trade-off between success and dependent-transaction latency.
  This protocol enables fast, secure, and deterministic atomic cross-chain execution while preserving chain autonomy, providing a foundation for scalable blockchain interoperability solutions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [274] [iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02542)
*Yuan Zhang,Jingfei Ni,Guanchen Lu,Shiqi Zhang,Qingshan Xu,Chi Liu,Xin Nie,Wenjie Xu,Lin Gao,Zhiyuan Cheng,Mingxin Zhou,Jiajia Wu,Diyuan Liu,Jia Pan,Chao Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni，一种统一多模态基础模型，通过共享多模态自注意力机制联合建模视觉、语言和动作，采用类脑-小脑协作架构，四阶段训练策略，在具身智能任务中实现指令理解、世界建模和动作生成的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将视觉-语言推理、视频世界建模和动作生成分离为级联流水线，导致接口瓶颈和复合预测误差，缺乏统一的具身智能基础模型。

Method: 提出统一多模态Omni框架，包含视觉-语言模型(VLM)、视频生成模型(VGM)和动作生成模型(AGM)，三者通过共享多模态自注意力通信。采用脑-小脑协作架构：VLM+VGM构成高层大脑（指令理解、任务规划、状态预测），AGM构成低层小脑（生成可执行动作块）。构建包含动作标注和动作自由的具身视频数据集，采用四阶段渐进训练策略。

Result: 在具身智能任务上实现了统一的指令理解、环境演化预测和精确动作控制，克服了级联管道中的接口瓶颈和误差累积问题。

Conclusion: 统一多模态框架比级联管道更有效，共享注意力机制和分阶段训练策略是实现通用具身智能体的可行路径。

Abstract: General-purpose embodied agents must understand multimodal instructions, anticipate how their environment will evolve, and produce precise control actions over extended horizons. Existing approaches typically specialize in visual-language reasoning, video-based world modeling, or action generation, while cascaded pipelines that first synthesize future observations and then infer actions can introduce interface bottlenecks and compound prediction errors. We present iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni, a unified multimodal foundation model that jointly models vision(videos and images), language, and action within a single Omni framework. Its modality-specific visual-language, video-generation, and action-generation components communicate through shared multimodal self-attention. This design establishes brain-cerebellum collaboration: the vision-language modeland video generation model form a high-level brain for instruction understanding, task planning, progress tracking, and future visual-state prediction, whereas the action generation modelserves as a low-level cerebellum that directly converts planned subgoals and shared multimodal context into executable action chunks. To develop these capabilities, we combine action-annotated and action-free embodied videos from human demonstrations and robot interactions with embodied reasoning, embodied perception, and general-purpose image-text data to construct a comprehensive dataset. We further adopt a four-stage strategy that progressively trains the VLM, VGM, and AGM before jointly fine-tuning the complete model.

</details>


### [275] [Internal Pluralism and the Limits of Pairwise Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02672)
*Bailey Flanigan,Michelle Si*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了内部多元主义（个体同时持有多种权威性优先级）如何破坏局部成对比较在偏好学习中的两个核心假设：局部比较足以反映全局偏好，以及人们总能果断做出比较。作者通过形式化模型揭示了两种失败模式——优先级的全局性导致局部比较失效，以及优先级冲突导致被迫比较时的行为扭曲。研究发现允许报告"无法决定"可显著减少学习准确偏好所需的查询次数，并指向直接 eliciting 优先级的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部成对比较被广泛用于学习人们对决策规则的偏好（如参与式设计、对齐问题），但其依赖两个强假设：局部比较足以反映全局偏好，且人们总能果断比较。论文旨在检验这些假设在个体内部存在多元优先级（内部多元主义）时如何被破坏。

Method: 构建关于决策规则偏好的形式化模型，其中个体根据多个权威性优先级评估规则。基于此模型分析两种失败模式：（1）优先级（如比例性、平等主义、平等待遇）的全局性导致局部比较无法捕捉；（2）局部可表示的优先级之间冲突会导致被迫比较时的行为扭曲。进一步通过模型研究允许报告"无法决定"的替代方案。

Result: （1）全局性优先级（如平等主义）使得局部比较无法充分捕获偏好信息；（2）优先级冲突会导致被迫比较时产生代价高昂的行为扭曲；（3）允许报告"无法决定"可显著减少学习准确偏好所需的查询次数。

Conclusion: 论文指出现有偏好学习方法需要转向直接 eliciting 个体内部优先级的方法，从而获得更忠实、可解释的价值偏好信息。
